热木星与短周期行星1 篇

Stellar-Field-Driven Induction Heating Unlikely To Cause Significant Heating in Giant Exoplanets

Madison Brady, Darryl Z. Seligman, Cassidy Walker · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究估算了围绕具有偏斜磁偶极子恒星运行的已知巨行星群体所能耗散的磁感应功率，发现即使在极端偏斜磁场条件下，绝大多数已知系外行星的磁感应加热功率也不足以引起可观测的表面温度变化。研究扩展了对行星偏心率影响的计算，并指出虽然磁扩散过程主导当前分析，但自转快速的热木星可能具有高磁雷诺数而进入感应主导机制。总体而言，尽然当前已知行星难以观测到显著的恒星诱导欧姆加热，但不排除高磁活性恒星周围偏心倾斜短周期行星存在可观测磁加热现象的可能性。
英文原摘要
A planet moving through a changing magnetic field will have a current induced on it by that magnetic field. If the magnetic Reynolds number in the upper atmosphere of this planet is small, the dissipation of this current could result in observable changes to the planet's properties. In this paper, we estimated the total power that would be dissipated in the observed giant exoplanet population if they orbited stars with misaligned magnetic dipoles. We found that, even if all planets orbit stars with extremely misaligned surface magnetic fields, the vast majority of known exoplanets are unlikely to dissipate enough magnetic power to cause observable changes in their surface temperatures. We also extended our calculations to estimate the impact that a low-but-nonzero planetary eccentricity would have on the induced power, and provide a code that implements these calculations. Overall, while it is unlikely that any currently known exoplanets are experiencing a high enough degree of stellar-induced Ohmic heating to be observable, we cannot rule out the possibility that an eccentric, inclined planet on a short-period orbit around a superlatively magnetically active star could have observable magnetic heating. We note that our calculations assume that magnetic diffusion dominates over induction in the upper atmospheres of these planets. However, hot Jupiters with large wind speeds may have high magnetic Reynolds numbers and therefore be in the inductive regime.
hot Jupiter
系外行星观测1 篇

Combining TTVs, RVs, and astrometry to characterize distant giant companions of transiting hot Jupiters: Application to WASP-4

J. -V. Harre, Louise D. Nielsen, A. M. S. Smith · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究结合径向速度、凌星计时变化和盖亚天体测量数据，通过光行时效应来表征热木星WASP-4b的外侧巨行星伴星，改进了WASP-4c的轨道参数测定（最小质量9.3±1.7木星质量，轨道周期~29年），并排除了WASP-4b显著的轨道衰减。该三重数据融合方法在盖亚DR5的模拟中可分别将WASP-4c的轨道周期、下合时间和径向速度半振幅误差减少约10%、25%和33%，为刻画热木星系统外侧伴星提供了一个新的有力工具。
英文原摘要
The origins and architectures of hot Jupiter (HJ) systems remain unclear, with potential explanations for the tight orbits being in-situ formation, disk-migration, or high-eccentricity migration. These mechanisms can leave different imprints on the orbital architecture, but tidal interactions act to erase these imprints for HJs, leading to orbital circularization, realignment, and potentially orbital decay. The latter can be mimicked by the introduction of light travel-time effects (LTTE) caused by an outer giant companion, as for WASP-4$\,$b. We examine the WASP-4 system using newly obtained ESO$\,$3.6/HARPS radial velocity (RV) observations and archival transit-timing data, leveraging the LTTE. Furthermore, we present a novel approach of analyzing HJ systems in sight of $\textit{Gaia}$'s Data Release 4. We propose combining RV, transit-timing, and astrometry data, making use of the LTTE as a proxy for the orbital motion of a possible outer companion. We combine and fit all currently available data and simulate $\textit{Gaia}$ DR4 and DR5 astrometric measurements of WASP-4 and perform an injection and recovery procedure to estimate their impact on the recovered planetary parameters of WASP-4$\,$c. The procedure employs a combined model that describes RVs, transit timing variations (TTVs) via the LTTE, and astrometry simultaneously. Analyzing the existing data, we improve the planetary parameters of WASP-4$\,$c, finding a minimum mass of $9.3\pm1.7\,M_\mathrm{Jup}$ with an orbital period of $10\,600^{+1100}_{-1000}\,$d, or about $29\,$yr. Furthermore, we find that there is no significant orbital decay taking place for WASP-4$\,$b. In simulations of $\textit{Gaia}$ DR5, our three-pronged approach reduces the error bars of WASP-4$\,$c's parameters by about $10\%$, $25\%$, and $33\%$ for its orbital period, time of inferior conjunction, and RV semi-amplitude, respectively. (abridged)
radial velocity · transit timing · astrometry · astrometric
行星形成与盘演化1 篇

Self-Consistent Microscopic Description of the 146Sm Half-Life for Early Solar System Chronology

Shulin Tang, Yibin Qian, Chong Qi, Ramon A. Wyss et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究建立了自洽的微观α衰变框架,将α粒子视为四核子团簇,通过壳层模型和团簇模型的耦合来描述146Sm的衰变,解决了其半衰期测量和理论计算长期存在的矛盾。通过在相邻放射性核素上的交叉验证,得到146Sm的半衰期为75.1+6.1-5.6百万年,精度达±6%,为早期太阳系行星和小行星的成因和演化年代学研究提供了可靠的同位素时标。
英文原摘要
The extinct \textit{p}-process radionuclide $^{146}$Sm, decaying to $^{142}$Nd, is a key short-lived chronometer for the formation and early evolution of terrestrial planets and planetesimals. However, there has been a long-standing discrepancy in both the experimental measurements and the theoretical evaluations of the $^{146}$Sm half-life. To address this, a self-consistent microscopic framework for $α$ decay is developed in this work, in which the $α$ particle is treated as a four-nucleon quartet. Its formation amplitude in the nuclear interior is constructed from a coherent superposition of multiple pure single-particle configurations weighted by the pairing occupation amplitudes. Coupling this shell amplitude to the $α$ cluster that survives in the low-density surface region yields a self-consistently determined two-state (shell~$\oplus$~cluster) preformation weight and effective potential. The calculated half-lives for the neighboring emitters of $^{146}$Sm are in good agreement with the measured values. Cross-validation over the neighboring emitters reproduces each excluded half-life without any nucleus-specific adjustment, and yields $^{146}$Sm predictions consistent to within $\pm 6\%$ of the resulting half-life $T_{1/2}=75.1^{+6.1}_{-5.6}$~Ma. This provides a solid calibration for theoretical studies of the early solar system.
planetesimal
行星大气3 篇

JWST Rocky Worlds DDT Observations of LTT 1445 A b and c in Secondary Eclipse Using MIRI/F1500W

Jegug Ih, Shubham Kanodia, Nicholas Wogan, Mark Fortune et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过JWST/MIRI在15 μm波段对近邻M矮星系统LTT 1445 A b和c的次级食观测,研究岩石行星大气保留条件。联合贝叶斯反演和前向模型分析表明,内行星b不具有可观测的大气(CO₂分压<0.6 mbar,表面压力≲0.1 bar),而外行星c的大气约束条件较弱。这一发现表明宇宙岸线(XUV驱动的大气逃逸边界)比标准预测更接近无气体一侧,为M矮星周围岩石行星大气演化机制提供了重要观测约束。
英文原摘要
The JWST Rocky Worlds Director's Discretionary Time (RWDDT) program uses secondary eclipse observations to determine the conditions under which rocky exoplanets around M dwarfs retain their atmospheres. We present RWDDT MIRI/F1500W observations of LTT 1445 A b and c, the transiting rocky siblings in the nearest such system (6.9 pc), comprising two eclipses of each planet across three visits. We perform three independent reductions and a joint fit that includes all existing JWST data and 220 radial velocities, improving the precision on the planetary radii, masses, and orbital parameters by factors of 1.6-2.3. We measure eclipse depths of $157^{+18}_{-17}$ ppm and $154^{+54}_{-37}$ ppm for planets b and c, corresponding to 15 $μ$m brightness temperatures of $528^{+39}_{-29}$ K and $625^{+109}_{-79}$ K, both consistent with the dark, bare rock limit. For planet b, combining these data with the archival MIRI/LRS spectrum in a Bayesian retrieval limits the CO$_2$ partial pressure to $<0.6$ mbar and the surface pressure to $\lesssim0.1$ bar, while forward models exclude thick aerosol layers that could mimic a bare surface in transmission. We conclude that planet b has no substantial atmosphere. For planet c, a potentially grazing eclipse and overlapping events leave the depth poorly constrained; the data rule out only thick, CO$_2$-bearing atmospheres ($\gtrsim1$ bar with $\geq1\%$ CO$_2$). LTT 1445 A b is the planet closest to the canonical XUV-driven Cosmic Shoreline observed in eclipse so far. Its lack of a substantial atmosphere implies that the Cosmic Shoreline cannot lie much farther toward the airless side than nominally predicted.
atmosphere · aerosol

Stellar Flares Rather than High T int as Possible Cause for the Missing Methane in Young Exoplanets: The Case Study of V1298 Tau b

Danica Adams, Pierre-Alexis Roy, Hilke E. Schlichting, Björn Benneke · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究以年轻系外行星V1298 Tau b为例，探讨了甲烷缺失问题的可能成因。研究通过大气光化学模型计算表明，频繁的恒星耀斑而非过高的行星内禀温度可以解释该行星大气中甲烷的耗尽现象——耀斑诱发的快速光解速率超过下层扩散补充速度，且当耀斑频率高于甲烷恢复的扩散时间尺度时，大气会达到新的稳定态而保持甲烷缺失。该机制可以自洽地再现V1298 Tau b的JWST观测特征，为理解年轻活跃恒星周围系外行星大气组成演化提供了新的物理图像。
英文原摘要
Abstract Recent observations of V1298 Tau b, a warm sub-Neptune ( R = 9.85 ± 0.3 R ⊕ , M = 13.1 ± 5.3 M ⊕ , T eq = 670 K) orbiting a young (10–30 Myr) K star, show depleted methane and surprising abundances of CO 2 in its atmosphere. This makes the planet an interesting target to study the “missing methane problem,” where Hubble Space Telescope, Spitzer, and JWST have revealed many sub-Jovian planets with effective temperatures cooler than 800 K to be depleted in methane compared to equilibrium chemistry predictions. On V1298 Tau b, an intrinsic temperature ( T int ) of 500 K is required to explain the methane depletion if strong mixing from a warm interior is the reason for the missing methane, but this T int is too large for a planet of its age and size according to standard interior structure evolution theories. Here, we show that stellar flares can result in methane depletion in a warm planet’s atmosphere with nominal T int ∼ 100 K. During a stellar flare, fast methane photolysis rates cannot be balanced by diffusion from below into the upper atmospheric layers probed by observations. Young active stars flare frequently, and we show that the timescale of diffusion for the methane to recover from a flare-induced depletion can be slow compared to the flare frequency. Over time, the atmosphere reaches a new steady state set by the average flare frequency and energy. This new steady state shows depleted methane in the 2 mbar region, and abundant CO, CO 2 , and HCN, which results in synthetic transmission spectra that reproduce the main features of the recent JWST observations of V1298 Tau b.
atmosphere · atmospheric · transmission spectr

From Young to Older Disks: JWST/MIRI Evidence for Fading Molecular Emission and Hints for Elevated C/O in Upper Scorpius

Chengyan Xie, Ilaria Pascucci, Feng Long, Uma Gorti et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究采用JWST/MIRI光谱学对Upper Scorpius协会中14个5-10 Myr的较老原行星盘进行观测,并通过局部热平衡分析推断气体性质,发现这些盘表现出分子发射衰减、检测率下降且C/O比升高的特征。与1-3 Myr的年轻系统对比分析表明,随着盘龄增长,内盘气体分子质量降低、发射温度下降,且大约一半的老盘呈现出高于年轻盘最大值的C/O比,与岩石漂移理论相符。这些结果表明内盘气体从年轻系统到老系统经历了化学演化过程,对原始行星大气的吸积具有重要意义。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present JWST/MIRI spectroscopy of 14 disks in the older (∼5–10 Myr) Upper Scorpius (USco) association and use slab of gas in local thermal equilibrium to infer basic gas properties. We find that one-half of these disks are molecular rich, with detections of H 2 O, CO 2 , HCN, C 2 H 2 , and H 2 , while the other one-half are molecular poor, showing no molecular emission other than H 2 . We further combine this sample with 10 other USco disks from the AGE-PRO program and compare the combined older sample to young (∼1–3 Myr) JDISCS Cycle 1 systems, which are analyzed in a similar manner. We find that USco disks have lower detection rates of major molecular species but a significantly higher detection rate of rarer C-bearing molecules such as C 4 H 2 . At a given accretion luminosity, molecular line luminosities are systematically lower in USco than in young disks, and the scaling relations with accretion luminosity differ between the two populations. Moreover, we find that about one-half of the older disks, preferentially the millimeter-faint and likely more compact disks, have observable mass ratios of C- to O-bearing molecules that are higher than the maximum values in the young sample. These results point to reduced inner-disk molecular gas masses, cooler emitting layers, and higher inner gas C/O ratios in older disks, the latter being consistent with pebble drift. Taken together, our findings provide evidence for chemical evolution of inner-disk gas from young to older systems, with important implications for the accretion of primordial planetary atmospheres.
atmosphere

HD 210058 b and BD+37 4838 b: A precessing eccentric hot Jupiter and a misaligned substellar companion around two A-type stars

Z. Balkóová, J. Žák, M. Skarka, M. C. Johnson et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过TESS测光和HARPS-N光谱的多普勒层析成像技术，测量了A型星HD 210058和BD+37 4838周围两个伴星候选体的自转轨道倾角。结果表明HD 210058 b是质量约10木星质量的热木星，轨道存在显著偏心和约860年周期的节点进动，预计仅可再观测约6年；而BD+37 4838 b是轨道倾斜的亚恒星伴星，其行星性质仍需进一步确认。这些发现为早型星周围近距热木星的形成迁移机制提供了新的约束。
英文原摘要
Relatively few A- and F-type stars are known to host exoplanets, and likewise few planets have measured spin-orbit angles, defined as the angle between the planet's orbital axis and the star's rotational axis. Expanding the sample of such systems is important for constraining the formation and migration mechanisms of close-in giant planets around early-type stars. We aim to measure the sky-projected spin-orbit angles of HD 210058 b (TOI-1355 b) and BD+37 4838 b (TOI-1414 b), two objects listed as candidate exoplanets, and assess the planetary nature of these systems. Both of their host stars are fast rotators. We analyzed TESS photometry in combination with the transit time series of HARPS-N spectra. For both objects, we created cross-correlation functions from the spectra to analyze Doppler tomography and detect and model the Doppler shadow. We also used photometric observations to refine several poorly constrained photometric and spectroscopic parameters of both the host stars and their companions. When possible, we used the derived results to constrain the companion masses. We verified the planetary and substellar nature for both objects. We report HD 210058 b to be a giant hot Jupiter with a mass of sim10, , residing on a slightly misaligned orbit with a sky-projected spin-orbit angle łambda=-32_ M_ Jup -6 ^ +4 ,̊m deg. Archival photometry reveals a continuously changing inclination and impact parameter, indicating nodal precession with a period of sim860,̊m yr and implying that further transit observations will be possible for only about six more years. The second target, BD+37 4838 b, orbits a hybrid γ Dor-δ Sct pulsator on a misaligned orbit with +1.4 _ -2.3 ,̊m deg. Since we could not reliably recover its mass, we only report an upper limit of sim24.3, , leaving the possible planetary nature of this object unresolved. M_ Jup

系外行星观测5 篇

A Joint Study of Epoch Astrometry and Radial Velocities of Gaia-4b

Shubham Kanodia, Suvrath Mahadevan, Gudmundur Stefansson, Domenico Barbato et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究联合分析了Gaia-4系统的精密位置天文时间序列和来自四台独立光谱仪的44条径向速度数据,利用跨越12年基线的互补数据集,通过开发的pyGaiafits框架精确确定了系统参数,将Gaia-4b的真实质量确定为10.70±0.32木星质量。关键发现是本研究未发现文献中先前报告的长期二阶径向速度趋势的显著证据,而早期Gaia位置天文数据的约束消除了仅跨1.5轨道周期的径向速度数据中轨道周期与长期趋势之间的简并性。这项工作展示了位置天文和径向速度联合分析在破解系统参数退化性问题上的重要作用。
英文原摘要
Gaia-4b is the first confirmed exoplanet discovered with Gaia astrometry, based on its identification as a possible exoplanet in the solution in Gaia Data Release 3 and subsequent confirmation with radial velocity. In this work we present a \texttt{python} framework called \texttt{pyGaiafits} that can jointly analyze astrometric time series and radial velocities. We use this to combine the precise along-scan astrometric time series for Gaia-4 from the Gaia DR4 pre-release with 44 radial velocities (RVs) obtained over three years from four independent spectrographs (HPF, HARPS-N, NEID, and FIES). By jointly fitting these two complementary datasets that collectively span a 12-year baseline ($\sim$7 orbital periods of Gaia-4b), we refine the system parameters and determine the true mass of Gaia-4b to be $10.70\pm0.32$~$M_J$. Critically, we do not find significant evidence for the long-term quadratic RV trend previously reported in the literature, recovering $\ddotγ = 5.5^{+8.3}_{-6.7}\;\mathrm{m\,s^{-1}\,yr^{-2}}$ consistent with zero. We discuss how this discrepancy likely arises from a degeneracy between orbital period and long-term trends when RVs span only $\sim$1.5 orbital cycles --- a degeneracy that is broken by anchoring the ephemeris with early Gaia astrometry.
radial velocity · astrometry · astrometric

Minimum observations for accurate Exoplanetary mass estimation with Radial Velocity Analysis (MINERVA)

C. Di Maio, S. Benatti, S. Colombo, G. Piccinini et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究开发了MINERVA工具,通过模拟包含恒星活动、天气中断和季节可见性等真实观测条件的径向速度时间序列,估计达到目标行星质量精度所需的观测次数。研究基于FGKM型恒星建立了模型,并利用GAPS和ArMS数据为K型星色球活动与径向速度离散度之间建立了新的经验标定。该工具在已知行星质量的目标上进行了验证,并应用于Ariel任务候选样本,为大规模系外行星质量测量和大气表征提供了优化的观测策略指导。
英文原摘要
Precise planetary mass measurements are essential for constraining the internal structure and envelope properties of exoplanets and to break key degeneracies in atmospheric retrievals. Estimating the observational effort needed to reach a desired mass precision is therefore fundamental for the optimisation of current and future radial velocity (RV) surveys. We present MINERVA, a tool designed to estimate the required number of RV observations to measure transiting planet masses to a given precision, providing a robust scheduling framework for large-scale samples. MINERVA simulates realistic RV time series for targets orbiting FGKM-type stars. The code ingests real-world observational timestamps to account for weather interruptions and seasonal visibility gaps. To model stellar activity, the pipeline draws ages from local neighbourhood distributions, uses empirical calibrations to derive rotation and magnetic activity levels, and translates these into time-varying RV signals. We also derived a new empirical calibration between chromospheric activity and RV dispersion for K-type stars, using Global Architecture of Planetary Systems (GAPS) and Ariel Mass Survey (ArMS) data. We validated MINERVA on targets with known planetary masses, yielding required observational efforts highly consistent literature values. To demonstrate its capabilities on statistically significant samples, we applied the code to a subsample of the Ariel Mission Candidate Sample. Adopting HARPS-N/TNG as the reference instrument, we estimated the observing nights necessary to achieve a 30% planetary mass precision, providing an optimised observational roadmap for atmospheric characterisation. MINERVA's probabilistic and flexible architecture allows the optimisation of ongoing RV campaigns, the evaluating of undersampling risks in active stars, and the dynamic guidance of population-wide characterisation strategies.
radial velocity

Merging of Multiple CMEs as the Solar Source of the Great Geomagnetic Storm in 2024 May

Chin-Chun Wu, Kan Liou, Brian E. Wood · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究采用全球磁流体动力学模拟模型，将2024年5月8-9日五个日冕物质抛出(CMEs)作为压力脉冲注入模拟域，探讨了为何初速度仅为530-1280 km/s的中等规模耀斑产生的CMEs却引发了第六大地磁暴(Dst min = −406 nT)。研究结果表明，单个CME模拟的激波到达时间误差范围为1.0-18.5小时，而五个CME联合模拟时误差缩小至0.2-6.0小时，并且更好地再现了Wind卫星观测的径向速度分布，证实了多个CME合并是2024年5月地磁暴的主要太阳源。
英文原摘要
Abstract The 2024 May 10–11 great geomagnetic storm ( Dst min = −406 nT) is ranked as the sixth largest geomagnetic event on record. The solar source of the May storm is still unsettled. It was associated with five halo coronal mass ejections (CMEs) erupting during May 8–9, but the Wind spacecraft only recorded an interplanetary shock at 16:32 UT on May 10 and a likely second one at 21:36 UT on the same day. In addition, the five CMEs were associated with moderate X-ray flares (M8.7–X2.2) and initial speeds of only 530–1280 km s −1 ; yet, they produced a very intense geomagnetic storm. Here we employ a global magnetohydrodynamic simulation model to address this puzzle. The CMEs, simulated with pressure pulses, were injected into the inner boundary (18 solar radii) of simulation domains with speeds estimated from the white-light coronagraph images. An error range of 1.0–18.5 hr is found for the shock arrival time (SAT) when individual CMEs are simulated, and an error range of 0.2–6.0 hr is found for the SAT when the five CMEs are simulated together. The latter simulation reproduces the radial velocity profile in much better agreement with the Wind observations than any individual one does, suggesting that merging of the CMEs is responsible for the May geomagnetic storm as inferred previously.
radial velocity

Searching for Circumplanetary Dust in the Cavity of the AB Aurigae Disk with ALMA

Lillian Yushu Jiang, Brendan P. Bowler, Ya-lin Wu, Yifan Zhou · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用ALMA进行了迄今最灵敏和最高分辨率的0.88毫米观测，在AB Aurigae过渡盘空洞中搜索与候选原行星相关的周行星尘埃。研究在AB Aur b和其他候选原行星位置未检测到点源，尘埃质量上限为≲0.0059地球质量，同时12CO发射显示气体在候选行星位置无明显局部过量或运动学异常。这些结果表明，如果这些候选体确实存在，其周行星物质必须具有极低的尘埃质量、小的物理尺度、光学厚的尘埃或缺乏大毫米粒子。
英文原摘要
Abstract The young intermediate-mass star AB Aurigae is surrounded by a transition disk whose cavity contains several proposed protoplanets, including the debated source “AB Aur b” at a projected separation of ≈ 0 . ″ 6 (≈93 au). To search for circumplanetary material associated with these candidates, we have obtained the most sensitive (∼20 μ Jy beam −1 rms) and highest angular resolution (∼30 mas) Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) Band 7 (0.88 mm) observations of AB Aur to date. We recover the asymmetric dust ring in the continuum emission peaking at ∼ 0 . ″ 9 (∼140 au) and compact emission at the stellar position, but detect no point source at the location of AB Aur b or any of the other proposed protoplanet candidates down to a 3 σ limit of 60 μ Jy. For optically thin emission, this corresponds to a dust mass of ≲0.0059 M ⊕ (for a dust temperature of 40 K), comparable to the circumplanetary disk around PDS 70 c. The 12 CO emission fills the continuum cavity, revealing an inner spiral structure and a clear rotation pattern. The gas at the position of AB Aur b is smoothly connected to the ambient disk with no localized excess or obvious kinematic deviation. If any proposed candidates are accreting, their circumplanetary material must have a low dust mass, a small physical size, dust that is optically thick, or a dearth of large millimeter-emitting grains.

Dynamical Mass Constraints on Transition Disk Perturbers Using the G23H Catalog

Dori Blakely, William Thompson, Doug Johnstone, Jessica Speedie et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究利用校准后的Hipparcos和Gaia绝对位置天文数据的新颖组合，对11个过渡盘系统中的扰动体进行了动力学质量约束，在其中4个系统中发现伴星证据，在HD 142527和MWC 758中有显著探测。对于7个无探测的系统，研究获得了关于伴星质量和半长轴的严格上限（如TW Hya系统中质量>3木星质量的伴星上限为半长轴>2天文单位）。通过多个模型的交叉验证，研究发现可能存在影响原行星盘系统的短期位置天文噪声，并为Gaia DR4数据对PDS 70和WISPIT 2系统中原行星的动力学质量约束前景进行了预测。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present dynamical mass constraints on perturbers in 11 transition disk systems using a novel combination of calibrated Hipparcos and Gaia absolute astrometry data. Out of the sample of 11, we find support for companions in four systems, with significant detections in two. These systems are HD 142527, where we clearly detect the known low-mass stellar companion HD 142527 B and MWC 758, where we detect a likely substellar companion. We also find moderate evidence of companions to AB Aur and UX Tau A. For the seven systems with nondetections, we find no evidence for companions more massive than ∼12 M Jup with a semimajor axis greater than 3 au for both HD 100546 and HD 100453, nor for companions more massive than ∼3 M Jup with a semimajor axis greater than 2 au for TW Hya. We also find no evidence for stellar-mass companions with semimajor axes between ∼4 and ∼25 au for HD 34282, HD 97048, CQ Tau, and RY Lup. In addition to our fiducial model, we perform cross-validation between astrometry sources. By comparing results across models, we find tentative evidence of a short-timescale excess astrometric noise that may impact some protoplanetary disk systems. We conclude with predictions for the prospects of making dynamical mass constraints on protoplanets in protoplanetary disk systems with Gaia Data Release 4 (GDR4) using detailed simulations of GDR4 data of PDS 70 and WISPIT 2.

Carbon dioxide and amorphous water ices in the debris disk around β Pictoris

S. K. Betti, C. H. Chen, C. Xie, K. Worthen et al. · Science
本研究利用詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜对β Pictoris原行星盘进行了近红外光谱观测,检测到了非晶态水冰和二氧化碳冰的特征吸收。我们推断二氧化碳冰与水冰以1:10的比例均匀混合,表明这些冰体形成于星际介质等寒冷环境中。这一发现表明原行星盘内频繁的碰撞作用是维持冰尘粒均匀分布的关键因素,为理解行星形成区域的物质组成和动力学过程提供了新的观测证据。
英文原摘要
The star β Pictoris is orbited by a dusty debris disk, within which planet formation is occurring. The outer disk is sufficiently far from the star that volatile molecules are expected to freeze into solid ices. We observed the β Pictoris disk in the near-infrared using the James Webb Space Telescope. The spectrum shows features due to amorphous water ice (<7% crystallinity) and carbon dioxide (CO 2 ) ice in both the 12 CO 2 and 13 CO 2 isotopologues. We infer that the CO 2 ice is intimately mixed with the water ice at a ratio of 1:10. This implies that the ices formed in cold conditions, likely in the interstellar medium. Frequent collisions within the debris disk are required to explain the observed uniform distribution of icy dust grains.

A first look at the protoplanet with VLTI/GRAVITY+

, K. Abd El Dayem, R. Abuter, N. Aimar et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用VLTI/GRAVITY+对年轻原行星进行了首次高精度观测，通过光谱能量分布分析和高精度位置天文学测量，确定该原行星有效温度在900-1300 K，轨道离心率中等，距主星约60 au，并对其大气性质和环行星盘进行了约束。研究结果独立确认了该伴星的自发光点源特性，但需要进一步更高信噪比或更长波长的观测来深入表征其大气成分和环行星物质。
英文原摘要
Confirmed observations of young protoplanets actively undergoing the process of formation have remained rare to date. In this article , we present new insights into the nature of the recently identified protoplanet (age sim5 Myr) from Very Large Telescope Interferometer/GRAVITY+ observations. Through analysis of the spectral energy distribution (SED) and astrometry of with the inclusion of new GRAVITY+ data, we investigated the nature of the protoplanet's thermal emission and orbital properties. We performed an orbit fit with the inclusion of new high-precision GRAVITY+ astrometry to provide an updated orbital solution. We also analyzed the medium resolution GRAVITY+ K band spectrum in combination with new Very Large Telescope/SPHERE and literature photometry, exploring both the atmospheric properties and the constraints on the circumplanetary environment. Finally, we checked for any signal from a resolved circumplanetary disk in the GRAVITY+ data. We detected the protoplanet . Analysis of the SED reveals an effective temperature in the 900 to 1300 K regime and at sim322 mas separation from its host star. The new GRAVITY+ astrometry prefers an orbit with moderate eccentricity a slight preference for atmospheric models including clouds over pure blackbody emission . Although the GRAVITY+ observations also do not resolve a circumplanetary disk, we place an upper limit on the possible spatial extent of such a disk. The new GRAVITY+ observations independently confirm as a self-luminous and point-like companion that resides within the gap of the transitional disk at sim60 au from its host star. Further observations -- either at higher signal-to-noise ratios or at longer wavelengths -- are required to characterize the atmosphere and examine the presence of circumplanetary material from thermal emission of the dust.

Molecular mapping of an exoplanet with JWST: Detection of NH3 and HCN in the temperate super-Jupiter Epsilon Indi Ab

R. Kravtchenko, O. Berné, I. Schroetter, P. Amiot et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究采用JWST的MIRI和NIRSpec对距离最近的超木星系外行星Epsilon Indi Ab进行红外分光观测，通过高通滤波和交叉相关方法克服了恒星污染，成功检测到该系外行星大气中的NH₃、H₂O和HCN等分子。研究结果表明Epsilon Indi Ab是迄今最冷的确认存在HCN和NH₃的系外行星，HCN的存在暗示其大气经历强烈的竖直混合，这验证了JWST红外分光学结合交叉相关技术在表征直接成像系外行星大气中的可行性，为跨越太阳系和热木星温度范围的行星大气物理化学性质研究开辟了新途径。
英文原摘要
Epsilon Indi Ab is currently the coldest and one of the closest directly imaged exoplanets (T_ eff = 275 K, d=3.6 pc). It offers a unique opportunity to test our understanding of the physical and chemical properties of gas giants across the full temperature range spanned by hotter directly imaged exoplanets and the cooler giants of the Solar System. We characterize the atmosphere of Epsilon Indi Ab using infrared spectroscopic observations. We present JWST spectroscopic observations of Epsilon Indi Ab obtained with MIRI in the medium-resolution spectrograph mode (4.9--27.9 μm) and NIRSpec in the integral field unit mode (2.87--5.27 μm). The data are highly contaminated by the stellar light from the bright host star. We therefore adopted a high-pass filtering and cross-correlation approach based on atmospheric models to detect the planetary signal and specific molecules. petitRADTRANS The cross-correlation analysis enabled us to robustly detect the planet with both instruments with a signal-to-noise ratio (S/N) up to mathrm S/N =15.0. Using molecular mapping, we securely identified the presence of (maximum mathrm NH3 S/N = 14.4), (maximum mathrm H2O S/N = 11.3), (mathrm HCN S/N = 5.3) and marginally detected (mathrm PH3 S/N = 4.1), (mathrm CH4 S/N = 3.8), (mathrm C2H2 S/N = 3.9) and (mathrm CO2 S/N = 2.6). In particular, Epsilon Indi Ab becomes the coldest exoplanet in which and have been identified so far. The presence of HCN likely implies intense vertical mixing. NH3 HCN These first results confirm that infrared spectroscopy combined with cross-correlation techniques can be used to characterize the atmosphere of directly imaged exoplanets with JWST, even when the contamination by the host star is critical.

Resolving inhomogeneous hot and ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres with ANDES: Insights from simulated ELT observations

A. Sánchez-López, E. Pallé, A. P. Millán · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过端到端模拟评估了ELT的ANDES仪器对系外行星大气非均质性结构的刻画能力，对HD189733b和WASP-76b等热木星和超热木星进行了伪二维大气传输谱模拟，包含温度结构、化学组分、风场和自转展宽等物理过程。研究表明ANDES能在单次凌星事件中以极高精度探测主要分子和金属物种，并通过时间分辨反演捕捉大气的温度和速度梯度，但标准一维均质模型反演会产生系统性偏差；该工作揭示了ELT时代高分辨率系外行星光谱的解释需要多维前向模型来同时约束相位依赖的边缘贡献、速度场和化学梯度。
英文原摘要
The advent of the ELT will move high-resolution exoplanet spectroscopy from atmospheric detection towards spatial and temporal characterisation. In particular, ANDES is expected to deliver transmission spectra of hot gas giants with sufficient information content to probe limb-to-limb differences, atmospheric winds, and time-variable chemical signatures within single transits. We assessed the capabilities of ANDES to characterise inhomogeneous hot and ultra-hot Jupiter atmospheres, and we evaluated whether standard 1D retrieval approaches could remain adequate in this information-rich regime. We performed end-to-end simulations of ANDES transmission spectroscopy for the benchmark hot Jupiter HD,189733,b and the ultra-hot Jupiter WASP-76,b. The simulated atmospheres were built with a pseudo-2D limb framework including different thermal structures, equilibrium chemistry, wind and rotational broadening, and phase-dependent limb contributions. We then analysed the synthetic datasets with cross-correlation maps and Bayesian retrievals over the full ANDES wavelength ranges. ANDES is expected to yield very high-significance detections of the dominant molecular and atomic species in single transits. For HD,189733,b and hot gas-giants, H_2O dominates the near-infrared signal, while CO, CH_4, and H_2S are detectable over more restricted regions of the explored composition grid. For WASP-76,b and ultra-hot Jupiters, near-infrared molecules and optical metals produce strong signals, with Fe, Ti, V, and Ca tracing the hotter limb. Time-resolved retrievals recover the expected evolution of temperature and velocity as different limb contributions dominate the observed spectrum, indicating that ANDES can probe atmospheric gradients on timescales of about some tens of minutes for favourable systems. However, we caution that retrievals performed with homogeneous 1D models yield precise but biased molecular abundances and derived elemental ratios, frequently over-constraining species that act as nuisance absorbers to compensate for unmodelled pseudo-2D structure. Our results show that ANDES will enable spatially and temporally resolved studies of hot-Jupiter atmospheres, but also that its data quality will expose the limitations of standard retrieval frameworks. The full potential of ANDES for an exoplanet atmosphere characterisation will only be realised through a paradigm shift in retrieval methods: whereas 1D retrievals remain useful diagnostic tools, the interpretation of ELT-era high-resolution spectra will require multi-dimensional forward models, capable of fitting phase-dependent limb contributions, velocity fields, and chemical gradients.

New line-driven mass-loss rate predictions for central stars of planetary nebulae: Luminosity dependence when approaching the Eddington limit

J. Krtička, J. Kubát, I. Krtičková · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究使用全局风模型系统地研究了行星状星云中心星的流线驱动风与光度的依赖关系，发现质量损失率对光度高度敏感，而终端速度主要取决于有效温度且对光度弱敏感。研究预测的参数与观测值基本符合，但指出平面平行模型对温度低于40kK星体的电离光子流计算不足，且风效率较低表明Wolf-Rayet型中心星与重元素丰度升高的天体相关联。
英文原摘要
After expelling most of their envelopes, asymptotic giant branch stars turn bluewards in the Hertzsprung-Russel diagram and become hot objects. These objects ionise the surrounding material, possibly creating a planetary nebula. Because they are hot and rather luminous, they are able to launch a line-driven wind. The luminosity dependence of these winds has not yet been studied in detail using global wind models that consistently consider the interaction of the photosphere and the wind. We provide line-driven wind models of the central stars of planetary nebulae focusing on the dependence of these winds on luminosity. While the mass-loss rate is strongly sensitive to stellar luminosity, the terminal velocity increases with effective temperature and only weakly depends on luminosity. The predicted values agree reasonably well with observations, except that observational mass-loss rates show a rather large scatter, and the predicted terminal velocities are slightly lower than observational values. We provide ionising fluxes and show that for stars cooler than approximately 40,kK, the ionising fluxes computed from plane-parallel models are inadequate. The wind efficiency stays rather weak, which implies that the Wolf-Rayet type of central stars is connected with objects with an elevated mass fraction of heavy elements.

ExoPlanetLab: Bayesian orbit fitting of Gaia-DR4 epoch astrometry, radial velocities and relative astrometry

David Hobbs · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
ExoPlanetLab是一个开源的贝叶斯轨道推断工具包,用于从盖亚DR4星历位置测量、径向速度和相对位置测量联合反演行星和伴星轨道,采用学生t分布描述重尾数据、鲁棒开普勒周期图和多种采样器(集合MCMC、嵌套采样器、可逆跳跃采样器)。在模拟和真实数据上的验证表明,该方法对双行星系统的参数约束精度达0.1σ以上,信噪比≥10的信号可完全检测,且不产生虚假探测,但周期覆盖率略低于标称值(95%置信区间内覆盖真实周期的比例为90%),同时计算成本随测量数量和伴星数量增加而快速增长。
英文原摘要
Gaia Data Release 4 (DR4) provides epoch astrometry from which thousands of giant planets and many substellar and stellar companions can be characterised. We present ExoPlanetLab, an open-source package for Bayesian orbit inference from Gaia epoch astrometry, radial velocities (RVs) and relative astrometry, validated on simulated and real data. The model orbit is compared with the epoch residuals after removing the part absorbed by the single-star fit. A Student-t likelihood describes heavy-tailed data. A robust Keplerian periodogram with bootstrap false-alarm probabilities gives starting values for a joint fit. Five samplers are available: tempered ensemble MCMC, two nested samplers and two reversible-jump samplers. Evidences for a fixed number of companions select that number. Acceleration and scan-angle models test whether an orbit is warranted. The samplers agree within 0.1 sigma on a simulated two-planet system. In 180 injections all fits converge, and all signals with signal-to-noise ratio S >= 10 are detected. The posteriors show no significant bias, but the period coverage is below nominal: 90% of the true periods lie within their 95% credible intervals. None of 30 companion-free data sets gives a false detection. On DR4 pre-release data the orbit of Gaia BH3 is recovered within 1.4 sigma of published solutions. The Student-t model brings the astrometric period and eccentricity of HD 114762 onto its RV orbit. Eight simulated relative positions narrow the period's credible interval to 0.27 of its width, and Octofitter agrees within 0.2 sigma on the same data. The code is validated for single sources with DR4-like epoch astrometry, with or without RVs. Relative astrometry is tested on simulated data only. The main limits are an uncorrelated noise model and the computing cost, which grows linearly with the number of measurements and steeply with the number of companions.

Nucleation of water ice under weak ionization via clustering on negative ions

André Nicolov, Efstratios M. Kritikos, William A. Goddard III., Paul M. Bellan · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过弱电离实验室等离子体模拟发现，在低温（~200 K以下）条件下，水分子在负离子（主要为OH⁻(H₂O)ₙ）聚集成核而自发形成冰粒，而这一过程不依赖外部杂质且不需正离子参与。通过时间分辨离子质谱表明负离子团簇作为冰核的关键前驱体，随温度降低而增大后迅速消失，与宏观冰粒快速生长的临界温度相符。该机制对原行星盘和地球中层等弱电离、缺乏预存尘埃的冷环境中的水冰形成具有重要意义，在高紫外照射使水分子部分解离的盘面位置，负离子路径可能主导冰的核成过程。
英文原摘要
Water-ice grains are shown to form spontaneously in a cold, weakly-ionized laboratory plasma with trace water admixture when neutrals and ions are cooled below $\sim$200 K. These conditions serve as an analog of ice nucleation in cold astrophysical and atmospheric environments. The ice grains form without the presence of particulate impurities, and do not form in the absence of ionization, indicating that nucleation is induced by ions. Time-resolved ion mass spectrometry provides separate detection of clusters of water molecules on both positive and negative ions. Hydrated negative ions, mainly $\mathrm{OH^-(H_2O)_n}$, grow to $\sim$200 amu, while hydrated positive ions, mainly $\mathrm{H^+(H_2O)_n}$, remain small and short-lived. As the temperature is reduced from room temperature toward 200 K, negative ion clusters grow in size and then drop sharply in abundance, consistent with the onset of rapid growth into macroscopic ice grains below a critical temperature. This indicates that hydrated negative ions are key precursors to ice nucleation in cold, weakly ionized environments, such as the Earth's mesosphere and protoplanetary disks, providing a concrete pathway for ice formation in environments where the nucleation mechanism has remained unresolved. This negative ion pathway is estimated to dominate over ice deposition on pre-existing dust grains in protoplanetary disk locations where there is sufficient UV to partially dissociate water molecules, and in addition, where negative ions such as OH- can form readily through electron attachment at low temperature.
protoplanetary disk

Two-stage Cooling in the Protoplanetary Disk Governs the Chemical and Isotopic Signatures of Carbonaceous Chondrites

Yuanyuan Tian, Liang Zhang, Zhengbin Deng · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过碳质球粒陨石中球粒与基质的原位化学分析和硅同位素测量，结合两阶段冷却模型，揭示了原行星盘热力学演化对行星体积累物质化学成分的控制机制。研究表明，中等难熔元素遵循闭系统互补关系，而中等挥发性元素则因开放系统气体损失而耗尽，非平衡冷凝过程主导了球粒与基质的同位素偏移。该模型将快速冷却阶段（驱动动力学控制的凝聚）与缓慢冷却阶段（允许挥发性元素逃逸）相结合，解释了难熔元素的局部互补性和挥发元素的全球混合趋势，建立了原行星盘的原始化学非均一性框架，对陆地行星的挥发元素亏损具有重要指示意义。
英文原摘要
Abstract The thermodynamic evolution of the solar protoplanetary disk fundamentally governed the chemical makeup of accreting planetesimals. While the chondrule–matrix complementarity in carbonaceous chondrites has long been used to trace these processes, its origin—whether from a single closed reservoir or the mechanical mixing of independent components—remains highly debated. Here, we combine in situ chemical analyses of matrices across six major carbonaceous chondrite groups with high-precision silicon isotope data for paired chondrules and matrices to resolve this dichotomy. We demonstrate that moderately refractory elements (Mg/Si, δ 30 Si) strictly follow closed-system complementarity, whereas matrices are systematically depleted in moderately volatile elements (MVEs), necessitating open-system gas loss. Furthermore, the subequilibrium chondrule–matrix δ 30 Si offsets point to kinetically suppressed, nonequilibrium condensation rather than pure equilibrium gas–solid exchange. To unify these contradictory constraints, we propose a two-stage cooling model driven by transient heating events in dynamically confined dust-enriched disk regions. An initial rapid cooling stage drives kinetically controlled condensation, locking in the chemical and isotopic complementarity of refractories while accommodating localized alkali recondensation. A subsequent slower cooling stage retains MVEs in the gas, allowing disk turbulence to physically separate and strip away the volatile-rich gas prior to local accretion. This semiopen framework permits inherited dust to mix with locally formed condensates during late-stage cooling, producing a composite matrix reservoir that can reconcile local component-specific complementarity with bulk-scale binary mixing trends. This fractionation mechanism establishes a primordial chemical heterogeneity in the protoplanetary disk that might be further inherited by accreting planetesimals, shaping the bulk volatile depletion of terrestrial planets.

A Possible Non-Transiting Companion in the TOI-2109 System Supported by Transit Timing Variations

Kaviya Parthasarathy, Prangsutip Cherdwongsung, Ing-Guey Jiang, Li-Chin Yeh et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用跨越2006-2025年的87个凌日观测纪元(来自TESS、CHEOPS、HST和地基测光),通过凌日时间变化(TTV)方法探测TOI-2109系统中的非凌日伴星。通过比较线性历元、轨道衰减、近日点进动、正弦模型和N体动力学模型五种物理模型,发现该系统最佳解释为一颗处于7:3平均运动共振附近的非凌日伴星,其轨道周期为1.578天,质量约0.70木星质量。两行星动力学模型相对正弦模型的BIC差值为4.95,提供了中等强度的证据支持引力扰动来自额外行星的结论。
英文原摘要
Transit timing variations are a powerful method for detecting non-transiting planets, particularly in systems near mean-motion resonances. We investigate the ultra-short-period hot Jupiter TOI-2109 b using 87 transit epochs spanning two decades (2006-2025), combining observations from TESS, CHEOPS, HST, and ground-based photometry. We compare five timing models representing different physical scenarios: a linear ephemeris, orbital decay, apsidal precession, a sinusoidal model, and a two-planet n-body dynamical model. The orbital-decay fit yields $\dot{P} = -3.96 \pm 2.05$ ms yr$^{-1}$, providing no significant evidence for ongoing orbital decay. The sinusoidal model reveals a periodic transit-timing-variation signal with an amplitude of $\approx 44\,s$. Dynamical modelling favours an additional non-transiting companion near the 7:3 mean-motion resonance with an orbital period of $1.57813\pm 8.9\times10^{-5}$ days and a mass of $0.70 \pm 0.02\,M_{\rm Jup}$. The two-planet model yields $Δ\mathrm{BIC}=4.95$ relative to the sinusoidal model, indicating positive evidence that the observed transit timing variations are best explained by gravitational perturbations from an additional planet.

The Brown Dwarf Desert is a Mass-Ratio Desert: Astrometric Demographics of 63,254 Stars

William Thompson, Alexandre Bouchard-Côté, Dori Blakely, Trevor Campbell et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过联合建模Hipparcos和Gaia DR2/DR3的天体测量数据，分析了63,254颗恒星的宽轨道伴星样本，首次在统一框架内测量了木星到B星等多种恒星类型上行星和恒星伴星的发生率。研究发现"褐矮星沙漠"本质上是一个质量比特征，在所有恒星质量上都在q≈2%处出现伴星耗尽，而非固定伴星质量，且在沙漠上下方分别呈现不同的金属丰度趋势。这表明质量比而非绝对质量是区分行星和恒星伴星的物理分界线，暗示两个不同的形成通道。
英文原摘要
Brown dwarfs are rare as close companions to stars compared to planets and stellar companions, a paucity known as the brown dwarf desert. Whether the desert sits at a fixed companion mass or a fixed companion-to-star mass-ratio $q$ has been difficult to test, because microlensing, radial velocities, and direct imaging have sampled comparatively narrow slices of stellar mass and separation. Here we apply a novel joint-modelling method to Hipparcos and Gaia DR2/DR3 astrometry from 63,254 stars to complete this picture with a sample 1-2 orders of magnitude larger than those of previous wide-orbit demographic studies. For the first time, we present a single survey that measures occurrence rates of both planetary and stellar companions on 3--94 yr orbits ($\sim$2--20\,au) across host masses from M dwarfs to B stars. We find that the desert is a mass-ratio feature: companions are strongly depleted at $q\approx2\%$ independent of stellar mass from M to B stars, and a desert at fixed companion mass is excluded. Furthermore, we find a positive trend in occurrence with metallicity below the mass-ratio desert, but a negative trend above. The desert therefore separates two distinct populations, plausibly two formation channels, that each scale with stellar mass: the lightest stellar-like companions of K stars ($\sim13 \, M_{jup}$) are less massive than the heaviest planet-like companions of B stars ($\sim27, M_{jup}$), which continue well beyond the deuterium-burning limit. Mass-ratio, not mass, is therefore the physically meaningful boundary between planets and their stellar-like counterparts.

Joint Gaia-Hipparcos astrometry, RV, and Imaging posteriors from the G23H Hipparcos sample

William Thompson, Dori Blakely, Alexandre Bouchard-Côté, Trevor Campbell et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用Hipparcos和Gaia的联合天体测量数据、径向速度和成像信息，对10^5颗恒星样本进行贝叶斯建模，得到了恒星和行星伴星存在的统一后验概率分布。该方法结合了多个数据源的约束（包括Hipparcos和Gaia多版本的绝对天体位置、光度学、径向速度变异及公共自行伴星信息），能够用于新伴星的检测、后续观测的靶源优选和行星统计学研究。研究成果以机器可读目录和网络平台的形式公开发布，该框架也适用于Gaia DR4数据的处理。
英文原摘要
We present uniform posteriors indicating the presence or absence of stellar and planetary companions over a sample of $10^5$ stars in Hipparcos and Gaia. We use the "G23H" catalog to fit a model that incorporates absolute astrometry constraints from Hipparcos, Gaia DR2, and Gaia DR3, as well as Gaia DR3 photometry, excess RV variability, and includes information from the presence or absence of common-proper motion companions. The resulting posteriors can be used directly to detect new companions, to target RV and imaging follow-up observations, and for downstream demographics studies. We highlight example detections of known and new candidate companions and the companion detection rate across the HR diagram. The modelling approach we present is directly applicable to Gaia DR4 epoch astrometry and RVs. All posteriors are provided as a machine-readable catalog and user-accessible website http://exopo.st/.
astrometry

The Neptunian ridge depends on the choice of linear or logarithmic period

Kaiming Cui, David J. Armstrong · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过对海王星脊（Neptunian ridge）的重新分析，揭示了其作为行星超密集区的报告结果主要源于采用线性周期（dN/dP）而非对数周期（dN/dlnP）的统计方式；当采用行星种族统计学标准的对数周期表示时，海王星脊与稀树草原的对比度从1.6-2.2下降至0.8-1.2，与无超密集的模拟种群结果一致。研究表明，已发表的海王星脊很大程度上是统计方法选择的产物，而非观测行星群体的本质特征。
英文原摘要
The Neptunian ridge has been reported as an overdensity of planets with radii of $\simeq$5-8 R$_\oplus$ and orbital periods of $\simeq$3-6 d, located between the Neptunian desert and the savanna. It was first identified in Kepler data and has recently been reanalysed using our TESS occurrence rates, where it appears as an approximately twofold excess relative to neighbouring regions. We show that the reported excess arises from expressing the occurrence density per unit period, $\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}P$, rather than per unit logarithmic period, $\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}\ln P$, where the logarithmic form is standard in planet demographics. Because the savanna interval used for comparison is twice as wide in period as the ridge, a population uniform in $\ln P$ naturally produces a factor-of-two contrast in $\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}P$. When expressed per unit $\ln P$, the published TESS and Kepler ridge-to-savanna contrasts decrease from 1.6-2.2 to 0.8-1.2, all consistent with unity. A simulated population with a desert but no intrinsic excess reproduces the appearance of a ridge in $\mathrm{d}N/\mathrm{d}P$ precisely. Reanalysing the TESS-SPOC occurrence rates of 5.5-8.5 R$_\oplus$ planets around FGK stars, we find a ridge-to-savanna ratio of $0.91^{+0.21}_{-0.16}$ per unit $\ln P$, which does not support the claimed overdensity at the ridge. Taken together, these tests imply that the published ridge is a result of the scale choice used, rather than a definitive property of the observed planet population.
occurrence rate

ALMA detection of continuum emission from GQ Lup B

Pietro Pirola, Stefano Facchini, Gabriele Cugno, Myriam Benisty et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用ALMA在0.89 mm波段检测到了环绕褐矮星/行星质量伴星GQ Lup B的尘埃盘连续谱辐射，这是继PDS 70 c和SR 12 c之后第三个在毫米波段观测到的此类伴星盘。观测结果表明该盘的半径小于希尔球半径的1/3（<2.3 au），盘质量极低（~0.0032 M⊕），但具有很高的吸积率，其盘质量-吸积率比（~10⁴年）与恒星质量天体中的最高吸积者相当。作者认为GQ Lup B周期性穿越主星周边盘可能是维持其高吸积率的关键机制，这为理解多星系统中伴星盘的演化和物质供应提供了新的观察证据。
英文原摘要
GQ Lup B is a massive (10-40 M$_{\text{Jup}}$) companion orbiting the young T-Tauri star GQ Lup A at a projected distance of $\sim$110 au (0.7''). It is outside the circumprimary disk in the submm continuum, but its ${\sim}\,63^\circ$ inclined orbit intersects the primary's gas disk twice per period. We report a 5.8$σ$ detection of continuum emission at the location of GQ Lup B, obtained with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) at 0.89 mm (band 7). We associate this emission with a dusty disk around the companion, already detected by VLT/NACO and JWST/MIRI in the mid-Infrared. To date, this is the third disk observed at mm wavelengths around a companion spanning the brown dwarf and planetary mass regimes, following PDS 70 c, a gas giant embedded in the parent circumstellar disk, and SR 12 c, a planetary-mass companion at 1200 au from the primary. We retrieve a point source with peak intensity $54.5\pm9.4\,μ\rm Jy\, beam^{-1}$, co-spatial with GRAVITY astrometry. The emission is spatially unresolved, placing an upper limit on the source radius of 2.3 au (from a synthesized beam of $\sim\,29\times25$ mas). For comparison, this is smaller than 1/3 of the Hill's radius (3-5 au for GQ Lup B, depending on the assumed mass), the theoretical upper limit for circum-planetary disks. Assuming optically thin thermal emission from mm-size grains in a disk-like structure, we infer a mass of 0.0032 M$_\oplus$, while the optically thick limit yields an emission radius of 0.2 au. Assuming a gas to dust ratio of 100, our results indicate a very low disk mass to accretion rate ratio, of the order of $\sim10^4$ years, analogously to the highest accretors in the stellar mass regime. In the case of GQ Lup B, this can be explained by its periodic crossings of the circumprimary disk, which could deliver a substantial amount of gas to the companion's disk over a long timescale.
astrometry

Receiving Exoplanet Messages with Gravitational-wave Carriers: The Gravitational-wave Radial Velocity Method

Casey McGrath · The Astrophysical Journal
本工作提出利用引力波作为载体的径向速度方法来探测绕紧凑天体（白矮星、中子星、黑洞等）轨道运行的系外行星，通过分析相位调制指数这一对长周期大质量行星更敏感的参数，研究了该方法在中频（LISA）和高频（LVK）引力波谱段的可行性。研究发现从中频到高频谱段相位调制指数增长O(10^4-10^6)倍，大大增强了行星存在引起的信号功率重新分布，并推导了功率损耗和信噪比的解析表达式，为该方法在系外行星质量-周期参数空间中的最优应用范围提供了指导。
英文原摘要
Abstract Gravitational-wave (GW) radial velocity (RV) may offer a new method for detecting exoplanets, one that would primarily target planets orbiting GW-emitting, ultracompact, post-main-sequence stellar objects such as white dwarfs, neutron stars, and black holes. In this work we show that while similar to electromagnetic-based RV, this GW-based method depends on the “phase modulation index” (PMI), a parameter that is more sensitive to high-mass, long-period exoplanets. We investigate the potential of GW RV for GW sources in both the mid-frequency spectrum (where the upcoming LISA mission will operate) and the high-frequency spectrum (where the current LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA (LVK) observatories operate). Remarkably, we find that the PMI increases by a factor of ∼ O 1 0 4 − 1 0 6 going from the mid-frequency to the high-frequency GW spectrum, which results in a greater redistribution of observed power due to the exoplanet’s presence. By analyzing the power content of the observed GW signal, we derive analytic expressions to predict the GW carrier frequency power loss due to the exoplanet and the signal-to-noise ratio of the GW source and the exoplanet. We also explore the effects of the additional Doppler effect caused by the motion of the GW observatory and the potential for GW source parameter bias introduced by the presence of an undetected exoplanet. All of this helps us better understand where the GW RV method may work best in the exoplanet mass–period discovery space.
radial velocity

Multimessenger Exoplanets: Combining Infrared Excess and Astrometry with Gravitational-wave Detections of Double White Dwarf Exoplanets

Casey McGrath, Mary Anne Limbach, Scott C. Noble, Vincent Kofman · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究探索利用引力波与电磁观测相结合的多信使方法来检测双白矮星联星周围的行星。通过LISA观测双白矮星系统产生的引力波信号可以探测绕其公转的行星，随后利用JWST红外超额辐射和Gaia/HWO天体测量技术进行电磁学确认。以已知双白矮星系统ZTF J2243+5242为例，研究表明这两种电磁技术可以独立确认引力波探测到的行星，且敏感参数空间相重叠，为这一新型系外行星探测方法的可行性和互补优势奠定基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract Multimessenger astrophysics provides a powerful framework for characterizing astrophysical systems through multiple, independent signals rather than relying solely on electromagnetic (EM) observations. While most prior multimessenger studies have focused on the properties of ultracompact objects, recent work has shown that gravitational waves (GWs) detectable with the upcoming Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) observatory can also be used to detect exoplanets orbiting stellar remnants. In particular, this includes circumbinary planets around tight double white dwarf (DWD) systems. This population is unique: no such planets are currently known around DWDs, and only a handful of circumbinary planets around main-sequence stars and planets orbiting white dwarfs (WDs) have been identified. Independent EM confirmation will be essential, especially for the first GW-detected exoplanets, to validate this method. While EM techniques are unlikely to efficiently survey large populations of DWDs, GW detection followed by targeted EM confirmation provides one pathway for identifying and characterizing these systems. Using a representative case, the known DWD ZTF J2243+5242 scaled to a fiducial distance of 150 pc, we compute the detectability of circumbinary exoplanets via LISA GW observations. We then demonstrate two complementary EM techniques—JWST infrared (IR) excess and Gaia or Habitable Worlds Observatory astrometry—that could independently confirm a GW-detected planet in this fiducial system. We present the first exploration of IR excess around DWDs as a viable exoplanet detection method, extending its established use for solitary WDs. Both EM techniques overlap with regions of parameter space where LISA is sensitive to DWD exoplanets, enabling robust confirmation and characterization.
astrometry

Entering the Era of Circumplanetary Ring Detection with Direct Imaging

Samantha N. Hasler, Alexandra Z. Greenbaum, Geoffrey Bryden, Vanessa Bailey et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究开发了一套混合正向模型,耦合光线追踪框架与土星环的卡西尼观测反射率谱,模拟了不同倾角和环向斜率下类木星系外行星的反射光相位曲线,以预测环对直接成像亮度的影响。模拟表明,在几何条件有利时,环可将可见光通量比增强至土星等效环的3.1倍或更高,但在某些情况下也会降低系统总亮度,且环的贡献对向斜率和轨道位置高度敏感。研究强调多波段观测和系外环的几何敏感性对罗曼太空望远镜等直接成像任务的行星特性推断和观测规划的重要意义。
英文原摘要
Abstract Rings are ubiquitous among the giant planets in the solar system, yet their presence around exoplanets remains observationally unconstrained. As direct imaging missions such as the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope approach first light, understanding the influence of circumplanetary rings on reflected light photometry is critical for the accurate interpretation and follow-up of direct imaging observations. We present a hybrid forward model for reflected light phase curves of ringed exoplanets, coupling a ray-casting framework for computing mutual planet–ring shadowing with empirical reflectance spectra from Cassini observations of Saturn’s rings. We model Jupiter-like planets with opaque, icy rings spanning 1–10× the radial extent of Saturn’s A, B, and C rings across a grid of inclinations and ring obliquities. Our simulations show that rings enhance the flux ratios at favorable ring geometries. Saturn-equivalent rings increase peak flux by a factor of 3.1 in the visible, while more extended rings produce enhanced contrasts exceeding an order of magnitude. The flux contribution of a ring is highly sensitive to obliquity and orbital location. At certain geometries, rings can suppress total system brightness below that of a ringless planet. We highlight multiband observations as a complementary route for diagnosing the presence of an exoring. We also apply the model to υ Andromedae d as an observational test case and discuss the implications for exoring detectability with the Roman Coronagraph. Exorings have the potential to bias inferred planetary properties from direct images and must be properly accounted for in observation preparation and interpretation.
direct imaging

Signatures of CH4 and NH3 in the atmosphere of β Pictoris b

A. Barone, M. Janson, A. Brandeker, T. Stolker et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过结合CRIRES+光谱仪在H、K、L和M波段的44个光谱设置对系外行星β Pictoris b进行高分辨率近红外光谱观测,采用分子模板交叉相关方法搜索其大气中的分子成分。研究确认了该行星大气中CH₄和CO的存在(信噪比分别约为200和50),并初步探测到NH₃和H₂O信号(信噪比分别约为5.3和5.7),其中CH₄和NH₃若得到确认,将是在该行星大气中的首次检测。这些结果表明多波段光谱综合分析能够有效提高对年轻行星微弱分子信号的探测灵敏度。
英文原摘要
β Pictoris is a nearby (19.7 pc), young (∼ 24 Myr), bright (V = 3.85) planetary system hosting three directly imaged planets. Previous high-resolution spectroscopy of b has detected and CO but not or Combining observations across a broad wavelength range has been shown to maximise sensitivity to such weak molecular signals. We searched for previously undetected molecular species in the atmosphere of b using high-resolution near-infrared spectra. We combined 44 CRIRES+ spectral settings covering the H, K, L, and M bands and cross-correlated the extracted planetary spectra with molecular templates. For species with uncertain detectability, we weighted the observations by their molecular information content and data quality, independent of the measured candidate-species cross-correlation functions. We recover and CO at cross-correlation S/N values of approximately 200 and 50, respectively. We additionally identify tentative and signals near the expected radial velocity of b, with S/N values of approximately 5.3 and 5.7. Equivalent extractions at the same projected separation on the opposite side of the star show no comparable peaks. We classify the and signals as tentative because their cross-correlation S/N values do not fully account for possible residual systematics. If confirmed, these would be the first detections of both species in the atmosphere of b.

Simulation of a Jovian Planet Undergoing Gas Accretion

Fabian Menezes · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过FARGO3D磁流体动力学数值模拟，探讨了原行星与原行星盘的相互作用及其引发的轨道迁移过程，采用变化初始径向位置的参数扫描方法（五组2木星质量原行星模拟）来验证该参数对行星-气体相互作用和气隙形成的影响。研究结果表明，初始径向位置是控制行星与盘内气体相互作用方式及气隙形成特征的关键参数。
英文原摘要
To understand the process of planet formation in the Solar System or any other stellar system, we need to comprehend the interaction between forming planets and the protoplanetary disk. This work presents a study of the interaction of the protoplanetary disk with the protoplanet and the following types of orbital migration that can occur, and to verify this interaction, numerical simulations were performed using the magneto-hydrodynamics code FARGO3D. The researchers made five simulations with a 2-Jupiter-mass planet in the protoplanetary disk. The only parameter varied was the initial radial position with the aim of verifying the influence of this parameter on the behavior of planetary migration. As a result of these simulations, we find that the initial radial coordinate is a very significant parameter in how the planet interacts with the gas in the disk and the formation of gas gaps.

Kinematic Signatures of Stellar Flybys in Protoplanetary Discs

Vasundhara Prasad, Cristiano Longarini, Carolin N. Kimmig, Cathie J. Clarke · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过三维光滑粒子流体力学模拟研究了恒星飞掠对原行星盘的扰动效应，重点关注近星点后数千年的演化过程。研究发现恒星飞掠虽然初期导致盘的翘曲和扭转，但这种几何扰动会在粘性阻尼作用下快速消散，然而由ALMA观测的CO运动学显示复杂的非开普勒螺旋特征在盘形态松弛后仍长期存在，包括共面飞掠情况下完全不产生翘曲的情形。因此，动力学残差单独不足以确定盘是否存在翘曲，散射光观测提供了独立诊断以识别螺旋臂和侧向不对称性。
英文原摘要
Recent high-resolution molecular line observations of protoplanetary discs have revealed large-scale non-Keplerian features in the gas kinematics of several systems, which can be interpreted as signatures of warps in the disc. Warps can arise from a range of physical mechanisms, and it remains unclear whether the observed kinematic residuals can be uniquely attributed to warping, or to different competing effects. Stellar flybys are one such mechanism capable of both warping a disc and directly perturbing its kinematics. Using 3D smoothed particle hydrodynamics simulations, we study the evolution of protoplanetary discs perturbed by inclined and coplanar stellar flybys, focusing on timescales of several thousand years after periastron rather than the immediate aftermath of the encounter. We find that inclined stellar flybys initially induce warping and twisting in the disc, but that this geometric perturbation is damped on a timescale set by the disc viscosity. Despite this geometric damping, synthetic ALMA observations of the CO kinematics show that complex non-Keplerian spiral features persist in the velocity residuals long after the warped morphology has relaxed, including in coplanar encounters where no warp is ever induced. Fitting these residuals with a simple warped-disc model, the degree of warping in the disc is systematically overestimated. We therefore conclude that kinematic residuals alone are insufficient to establish that a disc is warped, as flybys induce kinematic complexity independently of warps. We further show that scattered light observations provide an independent diagnostic, showing spiral arms and lateral asymmetries.
protoplanetary disc

Using Scattered Near-infrared Light to Map Water Ice in Prestellar Cores with SPHEREx

Tamojeet Roychowdhury, Jennifer B. Bergner, Jens Kauffmann, Thushara G. S. Pillai et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究利用SPHEREx散射近红外光谱观测，首次获得四个原星核中3 μm水冰吸收特征的空间分辨图像，揭示了冰在致密核中的空间分布规律。研究发现两个低密度核的水冰吸收在中心达到峰值符合预期，但两个最密集的核心表现出意外的中心吸收下降现象，这无法通过Bonnor-Ebert球模型的几何构型、冰质量分数或冰成分空间差异等因素解释。该工作确立了SPHEREx散射光谱学作为研究致密核中冰分布的强大工具，为理解行星形成摇篮中的物质组成提供了新视角。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present the first coreshine-derived, spatially resolved maps of the 3 μ m H 2 O ice absorption feature in four prestellar cores, using SPHEREx spectra. Ices are a key component of dense cores in molecular clouds, playing a central role in the chemistry of planet formation around young stars. However, spatially resolved abundance studies remain limited, typically relying on unevenly distributed background star sight lines. Here, we take advantage of the all-sky spectrophotometric capabilities of SPHEREx to construct ice absorption maps with uniform spatial resolution using the illumination of dense cores by scattered Galactic radiation, or coreshine. To demonstrate proof of concept, we analyse the spatially varying H 2 O ice absorption in four nearby (∼140 pc) prestellar cores—L1544, CrA 151, L260 and L1512. Two cores follow the expected spatial trend of ice absorption peaking at the centre, but the two densest cores show a surprising drop in observed ice absorption in the innermost regions. To interpret the absorption maps, we construct analytical and simulated models of a Bonnor–Ebert sphere illuminated by scattering. We study the effects of different geometric configurations, ice mass fractions, and spatial differences in ice composition. None of these can explain the reduced central absorption, pointing to an unexplained physical or chemical effect operating in the densest prestellar regions. Our simulations further show that spectra derived from coreshine provide a robust tracer of spatially varying ice density and composition, establishing SPHEREx scattered-light spectroscopy as a powerful new probe of ice in dense cores.

Reproducing the stellar-mass dependence of the giant planet occurrence rate with pebble accretion models

Heather F Johnston, O Panić, S Reffert, B Liu et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过群体合成模型，基于小行星驱动的核心吸积理论，探讨了巨行星形成与恒星质量的依赖关系。研究采用三个径向速度巡天的联合数据约束模型，发现当假设更大质量恒星周围的吸积率更高且盘寿命更短时，模型可以准确再现巨行星发生率在~1.7-2M⊙恒星附近的峰值分布。此外，随着恒星质量增大，失控气体吸积发生在更大的轨道距离和更早的时间。
英文原摘要
Abstract The stellar mass dependence of the unbiased giant planet occurrence rate may be the best statistical tool to constrain the formation of such planets. This rate rises and falls as a function of stellar mass, peaking around stars of ~1.7 − 2M⊙. In this work, we carry out a population synthesis study, using pebble-driven core accretion model of planet formation, to investigate the planet formation conditions that may be responsible for this stellar-mass dependence. We use the inferred giant planet occurrence rate of three combined homogenised radial velocity surveys (EXPRESS, PPPS, and Lick giant star survey) to constrain the models. We find that we can produce a synthetic giant planet population with closely aligned occurrence and properties when we base our model on observationally-supported assumptions that accretion rates are higher and disk lifetimes are shorter around more massive stars. We also find that in this scenario, the runaway gas accretion occurs at a larger orbital distance and earlier times as the stellar mass increases.

Beyond the Fast Rotators: A JWST Multi-Cycle Treasury Survey of Substellar Weather

Natalia Oliveros-Gomez, Elena Manjavacas, Niall Whiteford, Caprice Phillips et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究提出使用JWST进行系统的多周期观测，通过时间分辨红外光谱学研究棕矮星和巨行星大气的天气变化及其与自转周期、光谱类型和观测几何的依赖关系。该项目结合宽波段红外覆盖和多个自转周期的监测，能够约束云结构、热扰动等物理过程，同时获得一致的数据集用于与三维大气环流模型对比。该统一的人口水平研究框架将显著推进对棕矮星和直接成像系外行星大气多样性与演化的理解。
英文原摘要
Atmospheric variability provides a unique window into the three-dimensional structure, dynamics, and evolution of brown dwarf and giant exoplanet atmospheres. Recent JWST time-resolved spectroscopic observations have revealed complex wavelength-dependent variability, offering unprecedented insights into cloud structures, thermal perturbations, and atmospheric chemistry. However, existing programs have focused primarily on individual benchmarks and specific scientific questions, resulting in heterogeneous spectral and temporal coverage and leaving important regions of the substellar population underexplored, particularly across a broad range of rotation periods, spectral types, and viewing geometries. A JWST Multi-Cycle Treasury program could systematically investigate substellar weather across a diverse, well-characterized population. By combining broad infrared spectroscopic coverage with observations spanning multiple rotations and strategically selected epochs, this program would constrain the contributions of different physical mechanisms while investigating how atmospheric structures depend on rotation, viewing geometry, and fundamental physical properties. Extended monitoring would also improve rotational-period determinations and reveal how atmospheric features evolve from rotation to rotation and over longer timescales. This coordinated effort would provide a consistently designed and uniformly processed dataset of time-resolved spectra and variability diagnostics, enabling population-level comparisons, testing predictions from three-dimensional circulation models, and establishing a framework for interpreting the atmospheric diversity and evolution of brown dwarfs and directly imaged giant exoplanets.
atmosphere · atmospheric

Planetary material is a major source of hydrogen in helium-atmosphere white dwarfs

Paula Izquierdo, Andrew Swan, Christopher J. Manser, Boris T. Gänsicke et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过构建包含扩散和吸积两种途径的物理模型，利用DESI和SDSS巡天中约10000-19000K的氦大气白矮星样本，研究了这些白矮星大气中氢的来源。研究发现至少22±2%具有可检测氢的氦大气白矮星必然从其行星系统吸积了氢，表明行星物质（冰、含水矿物或大气）是解释白矮星族群氢谱演化的必要成分，而非仅限于极端富氢个例。
英文原摘要
Many white dwarfs with helium-dominated atmospheres show traces of hydrogen in their spectra. On average, the total mass of hydrogen within their convective envelopes increases with age, but there is no consensus on the origin of such hydrogen. Hypotheses include upward diffusion from hidden reservoirs deep within the envelope, or accretion, alongside heavier elements, of planetary material. We construct a simple model that includes both pathways, and test it using two independent samples of helium-atmosphere DB white dwarfs from the DESI and SDSS spectroscopic surveys, selected to have Gaia colours BP-RP > -0.25, corresponding roughly to 10000 < $T_{\rm eff}$ < 19000K. We find that at least $22 \pm 2$ per cent of the helium-atmosphere white dwarfs with detectable hydrogen must have accreted some of it from their planetary systems, via ices, hydrated minerals or atmospheres. We thus demonstrate that planetary hydrogen is a necessary ingredient to explain the spectral evolution of white dwarfs as a population, rather than only extreme hydrogen-enriched cases.

Hubble Space Telescope Observations of Active Asteroid (101955) Bennu

David Jewitt, Max Mutchler, Yoonyoung Kim · The Astronomical Journal
本研究利用哈勃太空望远镜对活跃小行星(101955)贝努进行高分辨率观测,通过近核表面亮度轮廓和粒子尾迹分析,得到其质量损失率上限为0.12 kg/s和6×10⁻³ kg/s,比其他活跃小行星远程测量值低1-2个数量级。研究表明许多小行星的低活动率可能低于遥感观测的检测阈值,推断主带小行星群的累积尘埃产生速率仅为维持黄道光稳态所需速率的<1%-10%。
英文原摘要
Abstract Measurements from NASA’s OSIRIS-REx spacecraft revealed (101955) Bennu as a member of the active asteroid class. These objects exhibit transient, cometlike activity, commonly producing particle trails best observed from a vantage point close to the projected orbit plane. Accordingly, we obtained deep, high-resolution observations of Bennu at orbit plane crossing in 2023, using the Hubble Space Telescope. We find no evidence for ejected material, and place model-dependent limits to the mass loss of dM / dt < 0.12 kg s −1 from an analysis of the near-nucleus surface brightness profiles and dM / dt < 6 × 10 −3 kg s −1 from the absence of a measurable particle trail. These limits are one to two orders of magnitude smaller than rates of mass loss measured remotely in other active asteroids and raise the possibility that low activity in many or most asteroids might fall beneath the prevailing thresholds offered by remote observations. Taking a still smaller mass-loss rate estimated from OSIRIS-REx data, we calculate a specific rate L ∼ 1 0 − 7 [kg km −2 s −1 ] and extrapolate to the entire main-belt asteroid population. We estimate a cumulative dust production rate ∼ 850 r min − 1 / 2 [kg s −1 ], where r min is the minimum asteroid radius in meters. For r min > 1 m, this is <1% to 10% of the rate needed to sustain the Zodiacal cloud in steady state.

Does Cumulative XUV Unify the Neptunian Desert?

Qunfeng Jiang · The Astronomical Journal
本研究探究累积高能紫外辐射(XUV)是否能统一解释海王星沙漠现象——即在轨道周期数日的轨道上缺乏2-10地球半径行星的现象。通过分析1850颗开普勒和TESS行星数据,结合恒星活动演化模型,作者发现使用累积XUV历史而非现在的光度通量可以显著缩小不同谱型恒星间的偏差(从-1.065 dex减小到-0.369 dex),且这一结论在考虑恒星年龄和自转初始条件变化后仍然稳健。研究表明累积XUV能够在很大程度上解释海王星沙漠的宿主星依赖性,但当前数据尚不足以确定独立于轨道周期的通用XUV阈值。
英文原摘要
Abstract The Neptunian desert is the scarcity of roughly 2–10 R ⊕ planets on orbits of a few days. Atmospheric escape driven by stellar X-rays and extreme-ultraviolet (XUV) radiation may shape its lower boundary by stripping hydrogen–helium envelopes. Because M dwarfs and F-, G-, and K-type stars have different activity histories, present bolometric irradiation may not trace the cumulative high-energy dose that drives escape. We ask whether cumulative XUV yields a common boundary across spectral type and whether the convergence survives star-to-star variation in age and rotation. Using 1850 Kepler and Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite planets, Model for Rotation of Stars activity tracks, and a global boundary model, we find that the M-dwarf–FGK offset shrinks from −1.065 dex in bolometric flux to −0.369 dex for a common 5 Gyr median-rotation XUV history. Assigning plausible ages and initial rotations to individual hosts preserves the smaller XUV offset in every trial, with a median reduction of 69.1%. A common-period null yields comparable alignment. Cumulative XUV therefore substantially reduces the host-type dependence, but the present data do not establish a universal XUV threshold independent of orbital period.

Non-monotonic oxygen ion escape during the transition from an induced to an intrinsic Martian magnetosphere

Yuchen Cao, Haoyu Lu, Jinbin Cao, Shibang Li et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究采用三维多流体磁流体动力学模型（含Hall效应）模拟火星从感应磁层到本征磁层的演变过程，通过改变偶极场强度（0-120 nT）系统研究氧离子逃逸机制。研究发现，随着磁场增强，磁压增大导致磁顶距离延伸，驱动电场远离重离子区域，日侧羽流逃逸通道被抑制，但总体O⁺逃逸率呈现非单调变化趋势，在45-60 nT磁化状态下达到全球峰值。这一结果反映了日侧羽流通道被逐步抑制与夜侧和尾向逃逸通道因磁拓扑重构而增强之间的动态竞争，表明古火星磁场的保护作用不遵循"磁场越强保护越好"的简单规律。
英文原摘要
Understanding the evolution of the global dipole magnetic field on ancient Mars is critical for reconstructing its paleo-environment. However, the effectiveness of this intrinsic dipole field in protecting the atmosphere and the relationship between magnetic shielding and ion escape rates remain subjects of debate. In this study, we utilize a three-dimensional multi-fluid magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) model incorporating the Hall effect, with equatorial dipole field strengths ranging from 0 to 120 nT, to simulate the transition from an induced to an intrinsic magnetosphere. To understand the underlying ion escape mechanisms, we conduct an in-depth analysis of the variations in the driving electric fields (convection, Hall, and ambipolar) and evaluate the trans-terminator and radial O^+ fluxes across different altitudes and solar zenith angle ranges. Our results reveal that as the dipole field strengthens, the increased magnetic pressure significantly extends the magnetopause standoff distance. This effectively displaces the driving electric fields away from heavy-ion-rich regions, which significantly suppresses and spatially deflects the dayside plume escape channel at lower altitudes. Notably, despite this localized shielding, the total O^+ escape rate exhibits a distinct non-monotonic trend, reaching a pronounced global peak at an intermediate magnetization state of approximately 45-60 nT. By evaluating the trans-terminator and radial fluxes, we suggest that this peak reflects a dynamic competition between the progressive suppression of the dayside plume channel and the enhanced redistribution of O^+ toward the nightside and tailward escape channel under the reconfigured magnetic topology. This finding demonstrates that the ancient Martian magnetic field does not strictly follow a "stronger field equals better protection" paradigm, offering new insights into how planetary magnetic field evolution controls heavy-ion loss.

A TESS Disappearing Act: The Vanishing Transits of TOI-2535 b

Samuel W. Yee, Erik A. Petigura, Rafael Brahm, Karen A. Collins et al. · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究发现了首颗被直接观测到凌星现象消失的系外行星TOI-2535b，其在7年的TESS观测中凌星位置以每年0.16的速率离开恒星盘面。通过联合分析凌星时间、凌星持续时间变化和径向速度数据，揭示系统中存在一颗土星质量或更大的近邻行星扰动体，两者处于平均运动共振关系且轨道互斜角≳5°，主要通过轨道面进动驱动凌星性质变化。该研究展示了迄今最极端的行星-行星动力学相互作用在人类观测时间尺度上驱动轨道演化的案例。
英文原摘要
Abstract Planetary transits require a fortuitous alignment between a planet’s orbit and our line of sight, but dynamical interactions can render this geometric coincidence only temporary. We report the discovery of the hot Jupiter TOI-2535b, the first confirmed planet orbiting a single star whose transits have been directly observed to disappear. Over 7 yr of photometry from NASA’s Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS), the planet’s transit chord traversed and eventually moved off the stellar disk entirely, with an impact parameter changing at the rate of b ̇ = 0.16 yr − 1 . The transit midtimes also show significant departures of ±6 hr from a linear ephemeris. Joint modeling of the transit timing and duration variations (TTVs and TDVs) and NEID radial-velocity data indicates the presence of a nearby, mutually inclined ( i mut ≳ 5 ∘ ) planetary perturber, with the TDV being primarily due to precession of the planets’ orbital planes. Our dynamical modeling reveals that the perturber is likely of Saturn-mass or greater and in a mean-motion resonance with TOI-2535b, making this an unusual system that contains two short-period giant planets. The TDVs measured for TOI-2535b represent one of the most extreme examples of planet–planet interactions driving orbital evolution on human timescales.

Aging in space: Space weathering age of S-type and V-type asteroid families from the Sloan Digital Sky Survey

Lakshika Palamakumbure, Tomáš Kohout, Grigori Fedorets · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究开发了基于支持向量回归的机器学习框架，利用可见光反射光谱估算S型和V型小行星族的空间风化年龄，通过与实验室模拟和陨石光谱数据对标进行训练和验证。应用该模型于斯隆数字巡天数据分析十个S型小行星族和灶神星族，发现内小行星带系统（Flora和Vesta）的空间风化年龄与动力学族龄基本一致，而Eunomia、Maria和Koronis等中外小行星带系统则显示风化年龄明显小于动力学年龄。这表明小行星表面演化由空间风化效率、YORP驱动的风化层重排、族形成机制和成分共同控制，空间风化年龄反映的是表面成熟度而非绝对年龄，其在不同小行星带区域表现出的差异揭示了行星际环境条件对表面演化的影响。
英文原摘要
Space weathering (SW) progressively modifies the optical properties of bodies with no atmosphere, enabling surface exposure ages to be inferred from time-dependent spectral changes. In this study, we develop a machine-learning framework based on a support vector regression (SVR) model to estimate the SW age of S-type and V-type asteroid families from visible reflectance spectra. The SW age is defined as the time since an asteroid has been exposed to the interplanetary environment after a breakup or resurfacing event. We trained and validated the SVR model on laboratory SW simulations (H+ irradiation and laser pulse irradiation) using published reflectance spectra of olivine, pyroxene, and olivine-pyroxene mixtures and chondritic meteorites representative of S-type and V-type asteroid composition. We applied the model to Sloan Digital Sky Survey photometric data to estimate SW ages for ten S-type asteroid families and Vesta family. The model yielded SW age distributions that show broad agreement with dynamical family ages for Flora and Vesta, suggesting a quasi-steady evolution after family formation where SW and resurfacing operate in approximate balance. These inner-belt systems are consistent with frequent but shallow Yarkovsky-O'Keefe-Radzievskii-Paddack (YORP)-driven regolith reworking, which does not fully reset spectral maturity. In contrast, Eunomia, Maria, and Koronis exhibit systematically younger SW ages relative to their dynamical ages, indicating more efficient surface renewal and/or slower weathering at larger heliocentric distances combined with episodic resurfacing on timescales of 10^8 years. Asteroid surface evolution reflects the interplay between SW efficiency, YORP-driven regolith redistribution, family formation mechanisms, and composition. In the inner belt, regolith reworking may preserve spectral maturity, whereas differences in resurfacing and weathering conditions across the mid- and outer belt may contribute to the decoupling of SW and dynamical ages. The SW age therefore provides an effective measure of surface maturation rather than a direct proxy for absolute age.
