Composition gradients in sub-Neptunes: K2-18 b and TOI-270 d as case studies

Luca Morf, Ravit Helled · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过对比纯绝热分层模型和含成分梯度非对流区域的复杂内部模型，评估了成分梯度对亚海王星K2-18 b和TOI-270 d内部结构推断的影响。研究发现成分梯度显著扩展了可行的内部结构范围，使氢氦含量的最大值增加数倍至数十倍，同时削弱了氢氦与铁丰度等参数间的相关性。建议在亚海王星内部建模中应默认包含成分梯度和非对流区域等复杂特征，以避免纯绝热模型对组成范围的低估和对观测精度影响的高估。
英文原摘要
Structure models of sub-Neptunes commonly assume purely adiabatic interiors with distinct layers of homogeneous composition. We assess how allowing for more complex interiors with composition gradients affects the inferred internal structure and bulk compositions of the sub-Neptunes K2-18,b and TOI-270,d. We compare purely adiabatic models with distinct layers against models that include composition gradients and stable non-convective regions. We present solutions that are consistent with the observed masses, radii, and atmospheric boundary conditions. Generally, interior degeneracy remains substantial even at a fixed mass and radius. We further find that composition gradients significantly increase the range of viable interior structures. For example, the maximum hydrogen--helium (H--He) mass fraction can increase significantly, by a factor of about three at a 1,bar temperature of 300,K and by a factor of about 10 at 600,K for K2-18,b. For TOI-270,d, the increase is generally more than an order of magnitude. Composition gradients additionally weaken parameter correlations. For K2-18,b and TOI-270,d, the Spearman rank correlation between the H--He and iron abundances drops from sim0.7 to sim0.3, with a similar weakening between the H--He abundance and the rock-to-iron mass ratio. However, other correlations, such as that between the H--He abundance and the water-to-rock mass ratio, persist or even strengthen and remain non-negligible. Purely adiabatic models underestimate the range of plausible compositions and overestimate the impact of more precise measurements, atmosphere models, and host-star constraints on a more accurate characterisation. We suggest that interior models of sub-Neptunes should by default include more complex interiors, such as composition gradients and non-convective regions, when using data for interpreting the planetary structure, formation, and evolution.

S. E. van Terwisga, P. Woitke, A. M. Weiss, A. D. Bosman et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用原行星盘HD 143006的特殊几何结构（内盘遮挡外盘），通过同时建模14条ALMA谱线（涵盖CO、CN、HCN等9种分子）来研究恒星紫外辐射对盘化学和温度的影响。研究采用ProDiMo模型并引入"平衡模型"考虑气体缓慢的加热冷却过程，成功拟合了被照亮和阴影区域的所有观测数据。结果表明HD 143006外盘的C/O比略大于1（~1.05），紫外光化学和冰的缓慢脱附过程对分子线形成有重要影响。
英文原摘要
The inner disk of the T,Tauri star HD,143006 is inclined and casts a broad shadow over a large portion of the outer disk. We use this extraordinary geometry to study the impact of stellar ultraviolet (UV) irradiation on disk chemistry, temperature, and line formation. We present new ALMA observations of this disk, covering CO, 13 CO, C^18O, CN, HCN, HCO^+$, CS, and C_3H_2. Combined with previously published C_2H, N_2H^+ and H_2CO observations, these data constitute an extensive dataset of fourteen ALMA lines from nine different molecules. We subdivide these data into two disk halves: the illuminated part and the shadowed part. We develop ProDiMo models for these two disk halves by optionally including an inclined inner disk that blocks the stellar illumination of the upper layers of the outer disk without hindering the stellar illumination of the outer disk along the midplane. The observed line flux contrasts between the illuminated and the shadowed disk sides are not very pronounced. We explain this phenomenon by introducing ``equilibrated models'' that consider a slow heating and cooling relaxation of gas parcels orbiting in and out of the shadow. Our results fit the radial line intensity profiles of all ALMA lines, both in flux and radial shape, with an overall reduced ̧hi of about 2.3, both from the illuminated and the shadowed disk sides, along with two ALMA continuum images, where we see evidence of radial drift, and the spectral energy distribution (SED) beyond 20,μm, all with one model. In most cases, the line intensities only increase slightly on the illuminated side because the gas warms up slowly, although fast UV photochemistry significantly reduces the observable molecular concentrations. The dust temperatures increase even more rapidly, but this has little effect on the line formation. However, for the lines of N_2H^+ and CS, which probe deeper layers where the gas and dust temperatures are coupled, slow UV desorption of ices such as NH_3 and H_2S plays a crucial role. This can result in a brightening of these lines on the shadowed side. Our analysis suggests with high confidence that the carbon-to-oxygen ratio in the outer disk of HD,143006 is slightly greater than one (∼,1.05). The disk mass is fit to be as low as 0.0012,M_⊙. However, almost equally well-fitting models with a fixed disk mass of $0.021 M_⊙ can be obtained after readjusting the disk shape, dust, and gas heating and cooling parameters.

Rudy A. Morales, Barbara G. Castanheira, Detlev Koester, Antonio P. Fedi et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过HETDEX低分辨率光谱（R∼750）结合Gaia天体测量数据，从759个白矮星候选体中识别了18颗DZ型白矮星，其中11颗为新发现，通过Ca ii H和K吸收线直接证实了大气金属污染和行星碎片吸积的存在。采用结合多波段测光(GALEX、SDSS、Pan-STARRS)和视差信息的迭代拟合方法确定了这些白矮星的大气参数，揭示自动分类器对冷的金属污染白矮星的误分类问题。该研究表明低分辨率光谱与Gaia天体测量的联合应用能有效扩展金属污染白矮星样本，为演化星周围行星碎片的后续研究奠定基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present 18 DZ white dwarfs, identified by metal absorption features, most prominently the Ca ii H and K lines, from spectroscopic observations in the Hobby–Eberly Telescope Dark Energy Experiment (HETDEX). Of these objects, 11 are newly spectroscopically confirmed through the detection of Ca ii H and K absorption in the HETDEX spectra ( R ∼ 750). The presence of Ca ii H and K absorption provides a direct spectroscopic signature of atmospheric metal pollution, which is generally interpreted as evidence for the accretion of planetary debris. By crossmatching the continuum-selected objects in the HETDEX Source Catalog with the Gaia eDR3 white dwarf catalog, we recovered 759 white dwarf candidates. Of these, all were visually inspected to account for potential metal-polluted white dwarfs that may fall below the high probability threshold ( P WD ≥ 0.75), resulting in 18 objects showing clear Ca ii H and K absorption. We determine their atmospheric parameters using an iterative fitting procedure that combines HETDEX spectroscopy with multiband photometry from the Galaxy Evolution Explorer (GALEX), Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS), and Pan-STARRS, together with Gaia parallaxes. All 18 objects were misclassified by the automated spectral classifier, demonstrating that visual inspection remains essential for recovering cool, metal-polluted white dwarfs in large spectroscopic surveys. These results highlight the power of combining low-resolution spectroscopy with Gaia astrometry to expand the census of metal-polluted white dwarfs and identify systems for future follow-up studies of planetary debris around evolved stars.

Evolution of Dust In Protoplanetary Disks With Magnetized Winds

Olcay Goksu, Ralph Pudritz, Timmy Delage · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过混合盘演化模型，同时考虑湍流吸积、磁风驱动吸积和质量损失，系统研究了磁化原行星盘中的尘埃演化过程。研究发现风主导盘相比湍流主导盘更有利于尘埃增长和富集，但满足流-蚌不稳定性的条件仅在极端情况下（极弱湍流和极强风质量损失）才在内几个天文单位范围内出现。这些结果表明，现有的参数化风驱动演化模型难以在外部区域产生适合于流-蚌不稳定性的行星小体形成条件，需要考虑更物理完备的模型以产生长期稳定的尘埃陷阱。
英文原摘要
Observations and theory increasingly indicate that protoplanetary disks are weakly turbulent, implying that magnetized disk winds may dominate their angular momentum transport and long-term evolution. Yet dust evolution in wind-dominated disks remains poorly understood, despite its importance for planet formation. In particular, whether wind-dominated disk evolution can create regions with enhanced dust-to-gas mass ratios that enable planetesimal formation via the streaming instability is unclear. We build a parametric hybrid disk evolution model that includes turbulent accretion, wind-driven accretion, and wind-driven mass loss to investigate dust evolution in magnetized protoplanetary disks. We employ this model to carry out a parameter study in which the initial total accretion rate, the wind-driven mass loss rate, and the turbulence strength are varied. We find that although wind-dominated disks are more favorable to dust growth and concentration than turbulence-dominated disks, conditions suitable for the streaming instability arise only within the inner few au and only under the most extreme conditions: very weak turbulence ($α_\text{turb} = 10^{-5}$) where magnetized winds drive most of the accretion ($\dot{M}_\text{acc,tot} \simeq \dot{M}_\text{wind,acc}$), and wind-driven mass loss comparable to the accretion rate ($\dot{M}_\text{wind,outflow} \simeq \dot{M}_\text{acc,wind}$). Our results therefore imply that, beyond the inner few au, the parametric wind-driven evolution models considered in the literature do not by themselves produce conditions suitable for planetesimal formation via the streaming instability, motivating more physically complete models in which spontaneous, long-lived dust traps can arise.

An ALMA View on Molecular Complexity: Water, Methanol, and Methyl Formate in the HD 100546 and IRS 48 Disks

Luna Rampinelli, Stefano Facchini, Marta De Simone, Margot Leemker et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用ALMA观测了HD 100546和IRS 48两个原行星盘中的水、甲醇和甲酸甲酯的气相丰度,揭示了不同盘源中分子复杂性的演化特征。IRS 48盘表现出异常高的甲醇/水比值(>0.2),而HD 100546盘的该比值(~0.02)与彗星和年轻恒星目标的观测结果一致,暗示了盘中升华冰储备库的成分差异。研究结果表明气相分子比值直接反映了行星形成阶段冰的组成,为追踪原行星盘中分子复杂性的化学演化过程、连接恒星和行星形成的早期和晚期阶段提供了新的探针。
英文原摘要
Abstract Water and methanol are the fundamental constituents of interstellar and cometary ices; understanding their abundances in disks is therefore of high importance to reveal how this ice reservoir evolves across the timeline of star and planet formation. We present Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) Band 5 line emission observations of water and methanol in the warm Herbig disks of HD 100546 and IRS 48. While the IRS 48 disk is methanol-rich, water vapor is not detected, implying a high methanol-to-water column density ratio of >0.2, possibly reflecting a distinct ice composition. Methanol and water are both detected in the HD 100546 disk, leading to a CH 3 OH/H 2 O ratio of ∼0.02, consistent with the median of the cometary distribution and with the lower edge of the distribution of young stellar objects (YSOs). We suggest that the gas-phase methanol-to-water ratio in the HD 100546 disk directly reveals the ice reservoir subsequently sublimated in the warm disk stage. Moreover, we report a methyl formate-to-methanol ratio in the IRS 48 disk of ∼0.2, consistent with the upper edge of the distribution of YSOs. The methyl formate-to-methanol ratio in the HD 100546 disk is ∼2, much higher than in younger and older sources, possibly reflecting a chemical enhancement of gas-phase methyl formate. We additionally constrain non-LTE conditions, optical depths, and emitting regions of the methanol lines in both disks. These results offer a new opportunity to probe the chemical trail of molecular complexity in the intermediate-disk stage, bridging early and late phases of the star and planet formation history.

Dynamical Impacts of Accretion Streamers on Protoplanetary Disks

Han-Gyeol Yun, Jeong-Eun Lee, Jaehan Bae, Woong-Tae Kim · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过三维流体动力学模拟（质量吸积率10⁻⁸–10⁻⁶ M☉ yr⁻¹）系统调查了吸积流对原行星盘动力学的影响。研究发现顺行流可激发全局盘偏心率至~0.4，导致盘产生螺旋臂和新月形超密集区，同时经历显著变形和增强型恒星吸积；逆行流则高效移除角动量，形成紧凑盘甚至引发盘破裂。合成ALMA观测表明吸积流扰动产生的运动学特征（如矩图中的多普勒反转和位置-速度图中的波动）可持续~100 kyr，暗示部分无当前流迹象的盘中的扰动可能是历史吸积事件的遗迹。
英文原摘要
Abstract Recent observations suggest that accretion streamers are common in protoplanetary disks, yet their dynamical impact on disk evolution remains poorly understood. Using three-dimensional hydrodynamic simulations with mass-infall rates of 10 −8 –10 −6 M ⊙ yr −1 , we investigate how streamer accretion influences the structure and evolution of protoplanetary disks. We find that a prograde streamer can excite disk eccentricity globally to values as high as ∼0.4. The resulting eccentric disk develops prominent spiral arms and crescent-shaped overdensities whose spatial structures agree remarkably well with analytic eccentric-disk theory. It also undergoes significant warping, and exhibits enhanced and variable stellar accretion. In contrast, retrograde streamer accretion efficiently removes disk angular momentum, producing a compact disk and, in extreme cases, triggering disk breaking. Using synthetic Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) molecular-line observations, we show that streamer-driven perturbations generate observable kinematic signatures, including Doppler flips in moment maps and wiggles in position–velocity diagrams. Remarkably, these signatures can persist for up to 100 kyr after infall has ceased, suggesting that some kinematic disturbances observed in disks without currently detected streamers may be relics of past infall events. Finally, we discuss the implications of streamer-driven disk evolution for planet formation and planet–disk interactions.

“Mega-Earths” are Likely an Artifact of Stellar Activity

Casey L. Brinkman, Nicolas B. Cowan · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究质疑超大地球（质量>10M⊕的类地行星）的真实性，通过统计分析发现所有已报道的超大地球都绕活跃恒星运行，这种巧合的概率小于1%。研究认为超大地球很可能是恒星活动（如星斑）对径向速度法造成的干扰所产生的假象，而非真实的行星种群。该结论对行星形成理论具有重要意义，同时指出高斯过程在活跃恒星周围类地行星质量测定中的可靠性需要重新评估。
英文原摘要
Abstract Empirical relations and planet formation simulations suggest an upper mass limit for rocky planets of ∼10 M ⊕ , as cores above this will almost certainly undergo runaway gas accretion. Nevertheless, a small number of planets appear to have densities consistent with rocky Earth-like compositions above this threshold, indicating that unusual formation and evolutionary scenarios are capable of producing extremely massive rocky planets. However, all of these planets orbit active stars with log R HK ′ > −4.75. We find a <1% probability that all literature ultramassive rocky planets would coincidentally orbit such active stars, and find no astrophysical reason to expect mega-Earths to preferentially form around active stars. We therefore conclude that mega-Earths are artifacts of stellar activity, and we question the reliability of Gaussian processes to enable mass measurements for rocky planets around active stars.

Formamide formation on forsterite and Mg(OH)_2·H_2O

D. Campisi · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究采用密度泛函理论(DFT)方法,计算了NH₂与H₂CO在Mg(OH)₂·H₂O分子和橄榄石(010)表面上的加成反应,以探索镁富集环境(如原行星盘)中甲酰胺的形成机制。结果表明,镁离子通过促进NH₂加成后的质子转移,使该反应在气相中存在高势垒的步骤变为几乎无垒或低垒过程,在300 K附近可在纳秒时间尺度上快速进行。本工作为原行星盘等镁富集尘埃环境中甲酰胺(氨基酸和RNA前驱体)的形成提供了新的反应途径,并为气-尘埃天体化学模型的改进提供了分子水平的动力学和热力学约束。
英文原摘要
Formamide formation in the Universe has been the subject of debate in several studies due to its importance as a precursor to amino acids and RNA. Current studies focus on the gas-phase and icy grain formation routes. However, formamide formation on bare silicate surfaces or hydrated Mg-bearing molecular models remains largely unexplored in warm Mg-rich dust environments, such as those potentially relevant to protoplanetary disks. This study presents a quantum-chemical investigation of formamide formation on an Mg center and a silicate surface. Mg(OH)_2·H_2O, considered here as a possible post-shock hydrated Mg-bearing species, and the bare (010) forsterite (Mg_2SiO_4) surface, used as an analog of a bare dust-grain surface , may promote formamide formation via NH_2 addition to H_2CO. This may help clarify a potential additional pathway to formamide formation in warm Mg-rich environments. I employed density functional theory (DFT), a quantum chemistry method, to study the addition of NH_2 to H_2CO with an isolated Mg(OH)_2·H_2O molecular model and on the forsterite (010) surface. First, I benchmarked various theoretical methodologies (M06-2X, PBE, PBE+U5, and VV) against the gold standard CCSD(T)-F12 and evaluated the performance of double-zeta and triple-zeta basis sets. I then examined the ability of Mg(OH)_2·H_2O and the forsterite (010) surface to promote formamide formation. Finally, for the case with an entrance barrier, I computed unimolecular rate constants and half-lives to assess its kinetic behavior. Of the DFT methods I tested, M06-2X and PBE+U5 provide the closest agreement with CCSD(T)-F12 for the addition of NH_2 to H_2CO on an Mg center. Mg(OH)_2·H_2O promotes proton transfer within the NH_2–CH_2O intermediate via the O–H group coordinated with Mg following NH_2 addition to H_2CO, whereas on the forsterite (010) surface, a barrier-less addition pathway is found when NH_2 is initially physisorbed close to the formaldehyde center, followed by proton transfer leading to formamide formation. This quantum-chemical study shows that the Mg-bearing model systems considered here can promote rapid proton transfer following NH_2 addition to H_2CO, whereas the corresponding bare gas-phase reaction proceeds through a large barrier for H abstraction to form formamide. For the Mg(OH)_2·H_2O model, the reaction step from the pre-reactive complex is predicted to occur on nanosecond timescales at temperatures approaching 300 K. The present work characterizes a potential pathway to formamide formation in Mg-bearing environments and provides molecular-level energetic and kinetic constraints for its future assessment in gas-grain astrochemical models.
protoplanetary disk

CO gaps in cold transition disks around low-mass stars

A. A. Rota, N. van der Marel, E. F. van Dishoeck, T. Muto · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过ALMA高分辨观测三个低质量星周围的冷过渡盘，分析了尘埃空洞内的气体和尘埃结构，发现CO发射延伸至盘空洞内部且不同同位素具有不同的空洞半径。研究表明这些盘的内盘气尘比高达10^5，与更亮恒星周围的过渡盘无显著差异，暗示需要通过热化学模型进一步约束空洞结构。观测结果可能指示存在在离心轨道上运行的超木星质量伴星，但需要进一步建模验证。
英文原摘要
Transition disks with large inner dust cavities are promising targets to study regarding planet formation, as these disks likely host massive planetary companions. The structure of gas and dust inside the cavity are crucial for understanding the potential of planet formation in the regions. We investigated the structure of gas and dust in a sample of three cold inclined transition disks around K and M stars without evidence of an inner dusty disk. We present new high-resolution ALMA Band 6 data of both 230 GHz continuum at 0farcs02 (∼ 3 au) resolution and the 12 CO 2--1, ^13CO 2--1, and C^18O 2--1 isotopologues at 0 (∼ 20$ au) resolution. We analyzed the radial profiles, computed the brightness temperatures, and derived estimates of the gas and dust content of the inner disk at 1 au. All three disks show dust cavities of 30--40 au in radius. The CO emission extends inside the cavity with progressively smaller CO cavity radii for the more abundant isotopologues. The ^13CO brightness temperature profile is similar to that of other transition disks and full disks. No inner dust disk was detected in the ALMA continuum, which is consistent with a lack of near-infrared excess. Considering the accretion rates, the gas-to-dust ratio in the inner disk at 1 au is estimated to be as high as 10$^5. No significant differences in the brightness temperature or in the gas-to-dust ratio were found between our disks and other transition disks around more luminous stars, contrary to expectations. This highlights the importance of individual-target studies through thermochemical modeling or multi-transition analyses to constrain the dust and gas structures in the cavity. The observed disk structures could be explained by a super-Jovian companion on eccentric orbits, but further modeling is needed to confirm this conclusion.

Testing Models of Disk Formation and Gravito-Viscous Evolution Against Observations of Young Protoplanetary Disks

Fabiola Antonietta Gerosa, Paola Pinilla, Eduard I Vorobyov, James McKevitt · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过二维薄盘流体动力学模拟，在自引力和湍流粘性作用下模拟原行星盘的形成和早期演化过程，探究不同初始质量云坍缩形成的盘的性质。研究结果基本重现了观测约束（如Class 0盘的毫米波流量和后期阶段的尘埃盘半径），但在解释Class I和II阶段盘的气体和尘埃质量分布方面存在局限，特别是无法再现最小质量盘的观测特征。作者指出需要考虑恒星形成环境的影响来解决这一矛盾。
英文原摘要
Abstract Young protoplanetary disks have received increasing attention in recent years, due to observations revealing the presence of substructures in disks younger than one million years, suggesting that planet formation may begin earlier than previously thought. Other recent observations have provided new insights into the evolution of gas and dust in these early stages, enabling comparisons with evolved Class II disks. Yet the physical processes governing the earliest stages of disk evolution remain poorly understood. In this paper, we present two-dimensional thin-disk hydrodynamical simulations of the formation and early evolution of protoplanetary disks, under the influence of self-gravity and turbulent viscosity alone. We explore various properties of disks forming from the collapse of clouds with a range of initial masses and we compare our results with surveys of protoplanetary disks across multiple evolutionary stages. Our simulations broadly reproduce key observational constraints, including the mm fluxes of Class 0 disks and the dust disk radii of later stages. However, our models do not explain the observed gas and dust disk masses of Class I and II disks: while they account for the most massive observed disks, they fail to reproduce the least massive ones. We discuss that simulations incorporating the influence of the star-forming environment might be needed to resolve this discrepancy.

Ice deposition fronts in porous bodies from transient heating events in a protoplanetary disk

Stephen Li, Alice C. Quillen, Adam E. Rubinstein, Dominique Segura-Cox et al. · Icarus
我们研究了原行星盘中瞬时加热事件引起的多孔天体内冰沉积锋面的动力学演化。通过建立包含热扩散、水汽运输和相变过程的耦合模型,我们采用数值模拟方法揭示了加热诱发的冰升华-冷凝过程如何形成明显的沉积锋面结构。研究表明冰锋面位置和强度受初始孔隙度、加热强度和持续时间的控制,这对理解原行星盘中固体物质的空间分布和行星胚胎的化学成分演化具有重要意义。

BeAR: The Bern Atmospheric Retrieval code - A GPU-accelerated framework for low-resolution, high-resolution, and joint retrievals of exoplanet atmospheres

Esther Wang, Daniel Kitzmann, H. Jens Hoeijmakers, Joost P. Wardenier et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
BeAR是一个GPU加速的系外行星大气检索代码,实现了低分辨率、高分辨率和联合检索框架,其中高分辨率采用叉相关方法处理发射和透射光谱。该框架在NVIDIA GPU上实现快速前向模型计算(~1ms/次),允许对热木星WASP-77Ab的JWST和地面观测数据进行百万级模型采样,成功复现了已发表的大气成分、C/O比和金属丰度。联合检索结果验证了该行星亚太阳金属丰度和近太阳C/O比的特征,同时注入-恢复测试表明丰度比不确定性被低估约两倍。
英文原摘要
We present BeAR, the Bern Atmospheric Retrieval code and successor of Helios-r, and its new high-resolution cross-correlation framework for emission and transmission spectra. BeAR performs joint retrievals by adding the log-likelihoods of high- and low-resolution data computed from the same model atmosphere. The framework implements established cross-correlation log-likelihoods, with the line-depth scaling either fitted analytically or sampled as a free parameter, their per-pixel generalization, fast model filtering, and model re-injection adapted to this filtering. The forward model and likelihood run entirely on a graphics processing unit (GPU). One forward model takes about 1 ms on an NVIDIA GH200, allowing millions of models per minute on a GPU cluster. In separate retrievals of Gemini South/IGRINS and JWST/NIRSpec dayside spectra of the hot Jupiter WASP-77Ab, BeAR reproduces published abundances, C/O ratios, and metallicities to within 1.5 sigma, the preference for 13CO, and the thermal structure and line contrast to within 1 sigma. The joint retrieval confirms a sub-solar metallicity and a near-solar C/O ratio. A full IGRINS retrieval with 17 free parameters takes 27 to 42 minutes on a single NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5080 or about 14 minutes on eight RTX 4090 GPUs. Injection--recovery tests show that the posteriors underestimate the uncertainties of abundance ratios by about a factor of two.

The cold substellar frontier in JWST Extragalactic surveys: a robust parallax measurement yields the most distant known ultra-cold Y dwarf

Feng-Yuan Frey Liu, Derek J. McLeod, Xueqing Chen, Ben J. Sutlieff et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过JWST多历元近红外成像数据，对JADES-GS-BD-5这颗极冷Y型棕矮星进行了精确的视差测量和天体测量分析，确定其距离为64 pc，是已知视差测量Y型矮星中距离最远的天体。该目标天体的极低光度和冷表面温度使其进入行星质量体制，为研究冷木星大气提供了关键基准，同时揭示现有大气模型在非平衡化学和垂直混合过程的描述上存在不足。这项工作展示了JWST深场巡天作为构建统计完备、视差定标Y型矮星样本的强大优势。
英文原摘要
We present an analysis of JADES-GS-BD-5, an extremely cold Y dwarf identified in the JADES/GOODS-South field. Using multi-epoch JWST/NIRCam imaging spanning a $\simeq2$-yr baseline, we calibrate the JWST astrometry and obtain the first robust parallax measurement for a brown dwarf discovered in a JWST extragalactic deep field. We measure a proper motion of $μ_{\rm tot}=49.8^{+0.3}_{-0.3}$ mas yr$^{-1}$ and a parallax of $\varpi=15.7^{+1.4}_{-1.3}$ mas, corresponding to a geometric distance of $d=64^{+6}_{-5}$ pc, $\sim3\times$ further than that of the previously known most distant Y dwarfs with parallax-measured distances. We hence determine a tangential velocity of $v_{\rm tan}=15.0^{+1.4}_{-1.3}$ km s$^{-1}$, consistent with a Galactic thin-disk object. We find no evidence for binary acceleration, significant photometric variability, or a plausible host. The $J$-band spectral morphology of JADES-GS-BD-5 is consistent with existing Y-dwarf templates, while its extremely faint luminosity and overall near-infrared spectral profile confirm that it is one of the coldest dwarfs discovered to date. We therefore adopt a classification of $>$Y1, consistent with a NIRCam colour-based proxy of cY1.5. The robust parallax-determined distance allows us to determine its bolometric luminosity, $\log(L_{\rm bol}/L_{\odot})=-7.08^{+0.09}_{-0.09}$. Evolutionary modelling places JADES-GS-BD-5 in the planetary-mass regime and makes it a rare benchmark for the study of cold giant-planet atmospheres. Current atmospheric models fail to reproduce its SED in detail, pointing to the importance of disequilibrium chemistry and vertical mixing. Finally, we also report additional proper motion measurements for eight of the other nine known JADES Y-dwarf candidates, highlighting JWST extragalactic surveys as a powerful route to building a statistically useful, parallax-calibrated sample of Y dwarfs.

Validation of Habitable Zone Limits with a New Radiative–Convective Equilibrium Climate Model

Jacob Haqq-Misra, Eric T. Wolf, Ravi K. Kopparapu · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究开发了新的一维辐射对流平衡气候模型ExoColumn，对沿用30年的Clima模型计算的宜居带边界进行了独立验证。通过与多个模型对比校准和逐线基准测试，ExoColumn在内边界（0.970 au和0.966 au）和外边界（1.611 au）的计算结果与Clima模型轨道距离一致性达到几个百分点，证实了Clima宜居带边界的可靠性；同时提供了12颗不同恒星和多个行星质量条件下的更新宜居带系数供社区使用。
英文原摘要
Abstract The habitable zone (HZ) boundaries calculated with the Clima one-dimensional radiative–convective equilibrium (RCE) model have served as a standard reference for exoplanet target selection for three decades, but they have never been independently validated with a comparable model. We present ExoColumn , a new one-dimensional RCE model that couples an independently developed atmospheric column physics package to the ExoRT correlated- k radiative transfer core that is also used by the three-dimensional ExoCAM model. We calibrate ExoColumn to present-day conditions, which matches other RCE models to within a few kelvin through the troposphere. Line-by-line benchmarks show that the ExoRT and Clima radiation cores each reproduce the outgoing longwave flux to within a few percent, with the differences due to the spectral line databases used by each model. We follow the same inverse climate modeling method as previous studies have done to find inner edge moist- and runaway-greenhouse limits of 0.970 au and 0.966 au, and an outer-edge maximum-greenhouse limit of 1.611 au at a CO 2 partial pressure of 8.7 bar. We extend these calculations to 12 host stars spanning 2000–7200 K and to planets of 0.1, 1, and 5 Earth mass, and we provide updated HZ boundary coefficients for community use. The ExoColumn limits systematically fall closer to the host star than those of Clima but agree to within a few percent in orbital distance, which validates the robustness of the published Clima HZ boundaries.

Effect of tidal gravity and planetary rotation on the retrieved atmospheric abundances of close-in exoplanets

K Arnav, Gopal Hazra · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究开发了包含潮汐引力和行星自转效应的大气正演和反演模型框架，用于研究这些效应如何影响近距离系外行星的大气分子丰度；以WASP-12b和WASP-121b为例，发现有效重力的改变会导致过境深度变化60-600 ppm，进而改变反演得到的分子丰度。通过注入-恢复实验验证了这些重力诱导的变化在简单大气组成中具有统计显著性，但在复杂大气中由于反演简并性增加而降低。
英文原摘要
Abstract Most modern atmospheric retrievals adopt the simplifying assumption that the planetary atmosphere endures no planetary rotation and stellar tidal effect. However, for tidally locked close-in exoplanets, the gravitational influence of the host star and the rapid rotation of the planet can significantly modify the effective gravity, leading to changes in the atmospheric scale height and mixing ratios of molecular abundances. In this work, we develop a combined framework to include these rotation and tidal effects into a forward and retrieval model to study how they affect the molecular abundances of close-in exoplanets. We specifically apply our model to the planet WASP-12b, observed with HST, and WASP-121b, observed with JWST, and investigate how atmospheric retrieval parameters change when tidal and centrifugal corrections to gravity are included. The forward model calculation for strongly affected gravity in WASP-12b shows an increment in transit depth in the range of 300-600 ppm for major molecules, whereas for WASP-121b with relatively less gravity reduction shows variations of 60-140 ppm. The atmospheric retrievals for both planets with and without effective gravity corrections show differences in the retrieved molecular abundances. To validate the detectability of these gravity-induced changes, we perform synthetic injection-recovery experiments over a broad wavelength range, which show statistically significant shifts in the retrieved atmospheric parameters for simple atmospheric compositions, while more complex atmospheric compositions exhibit smaller changes due to increased retrieval degeneracies. Finally, we investigate the robustness of these results using non-isothermal P-T profiles and cloudy atmospheric models, and find that the inferred trends remain qualitatively consistent.

Prospects for detecting hot-Jupiter tidal decay through transit timing with the mission Earth 2.0

Wenqin Wang, Yue Wang, Di-Chang Chen, Hui Zhang et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过联合Kepler、TESS和Earth 2.0任务的凌星测时数据，评估了ET任务在检测热木星轨道衰减中的能力，以探测星-行星系统中的潮汐耗散。研究采用长期凌星时间变化(TTV)分析方法，预测了轨道周期变化的可测量精度。结果表明ET可将热木星轨道衰减测量精度提高7.2倍，有望在三个额外的热木星系统中检测到轨道衰减，从而扩展已知样本、深化对星-行星系统潮汐相互作用的统计理解。
英文原摘要
Orbital decay of hot Jupiters provides a unique opportunity to probe tidal dissipation in star-planet systems. However, despite extensive observational efforts, only one hot Jupiter, namely WASP-12b, currently exhibits conclusive observational evidence for orbital decay. Expanding this sample is therefore crucial for establishing a statistical understanding of tidal interactions in planetary systems. Long-term transit timing variations (TTVs) provide a direct observational probe of orbital decay induced by tidal dissipation between a hot Jupiter and its host star. The Earth 2.0 (ET) mission will revisit the original Kepler field, delivering high-precision transit timing measurements and extending the observational baseline to approximately 24 years (2009--2033). In this work, we aim to assess the capability of ET to detect orbital decay in hot Jupiters previously observed by Kepler. We generate four years of simulated ET light curves based on the expected photometric precision and observing cadence of the mission and analyze them jointly with archival Kepler and TESS observations. Using these combined datasets, we perform long-term TTV analyses and predict the achievable precision for measuring orbital period variations. We find that ET can substantially improve the precision of orbital decay measurements in hot Jupiter systems by a factor of 7.2. Assuming the stellar tidal quality factor inferred from previous observations, ET is expected to detect orbital decay in three additional hot Jupiter systems. The expanded sample of hot Jupiters with measured orbital decay will establish ET as a powerful probe of tidal dissipation in star-planet systems, providing tighter constraints on stellar tidal quality factors and new insights into the tidal evolution of hot Jupiters.

The JWST Early Release Science Program for Direct Observations of Exoplanetary Systems IX: A Multi-Wavelength Framework for Source Characterization in JWST Coronagraphic Imaging of the HIP 65426 System

Evelyn L. Bruinsma, Aarynn L. Carter, Sasha Hinkley, Beth A. Biller et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用JWST冠状仪多波段观测数据对HIP 65426系统进行更新分析,通过改进的数据处理流程重新测量已知系外行星HIP 65426 b的测光和天体测量参数,并搜索潜在的行星伴星。结果表明流程更新显著改变了某些滤波器的测光和天体测量结果,HIP 65426 b的光谱能量分布与云层大气模型一致(有效温度~1436 K),同时识别出21个候选伴星源,其中8颗背景恒星、4个星系、1个未知源,零颗新行星,为这些源提供了首个历元天体测量数据。
英文原摘要
We present an updated analysis of the JWST coronagraphic imaging data of the HIP 65426 system. We aim to search for and characterize potential planetary companions as well as provide updated astrometry and photometry for the known exoplanet HIP 65426 b following updates to the JWST and spaceKLIP pipelines. Our measured photometry and astrometry show significant differences to those in Carter et al. (2023) but are consistent to within $2.2σ$ in separation, $1.5σ$ in position angle, and $3.7σ$ in flux. Our results indicate that pipeline updates have changed the photometry and astrometry significantly in some filters, especially in terms of observed flux. We see the greatest changes in separation in F444W and F1550C, position angle in F250M, and flux in F250M, F300M, F410M, F1140C, and F1550C. The SED of HIP 65426 b remains consistent with a young, giant planet best fit by models with a cloudy atmosphere and an effective temperature estimate of ~1436 K. Using both NIRCam and MIRI data, we identified 21 candidate companion sources in addition to the known planet. Of the 21 sources, 13 were attributed to astrophysical sources with eight background stars, four galaxies, one unknown, and zero planets. While one of these sources was detected in Chauvin et al. (2017), the remaining point sources were undetected in previous ground-based observations, which makes this work an initial epoch of astrometry for these objects. One galaxy in particular appears as a point source in MIRI but is highly extended in NIRCam and exposes a potential drawback to single-epoch MIRI imaging in a blind search for companions.

A Search for Exoplanets around Northern Circumpolar Stars. XI. A Planetary Companion around HD 112987 and a Planetary Companion Candidate around HD 58545

Byeong-Cheol Lee, Shenghong Gu, Beomdu Lim, Yeon-Ho Choi et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过8年高精度径向速度观测北天极巨星HD 58545和HD 112987，结合多种恒星活动诊断指标和光度数据，以MCMC采样方法拟合开普勒轨道模型，以区分行星伴星信号与恒星内禀变化。结果确认HD 112987伴随一颗轨道周期554.96天、最小质量8.48木星质量的行星伴星，而HD 58545的径向速度信号可能混有恒星自转调制的贡献，需要进一步监测以确定其起源。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present precise radial velocity (RV) measurements of the northern circumpolar giant stars HD 58545 and HD 112987 obtained over 8 yr with the Bohyunsan Observatory Echelle Spectrograph and supplemented during the final 3 yr by the Network of Robotic Echelle Spectrographs. RV variations in giant stars may arise from orbiting companions or intrinsic phenomena, including pulsations, chromospheric activity, and rotational modulation. To distinguish these possibilities, we analyzed line-profile variations, line bisectors, chromospheric activity indicators, and photometric data, and modeled the RV time series with Keplerian orbits using Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling. For HD 58545, the best-fitting Keplerian model yields an orbital period of 450.44 − 4.63 + 3.35 days and a minimum companion mass of 3.65 − 0.58 + 0.61 M J . For HD 112987, we derive an orbital period of 554.96 − 4.67 + 4.14 days and a minimum companion mass of 8.48 − 0.73 + 0.96 M J . None of the activity diagnostics for HD 112987 exhibits significant variability at the RV period, consistent with a Keplerian interpretation. In contrast, several activity diagnostics of HD 58545 exhibit periodicities comparable to the RV period, which is also consistent within the uncertainties with the projected rotational timescale, P rot / sin i = 462 ± 33 days . Thus, rotationally modulated stellar variability may contribute to the RV signal. Although the signal can be described by a Keplerian model, the current data cannot distinguish unambiguously between a planetary companion and intrinsic stellar variability. We therefore conclude that HD 112987 hosts a planetary companion, whereas further monitoring is required to determine the origin of the RV signal in HD 58545.
radial velocity

Astrometric planet search around southern ultracool dwarfs

J. Sahlmann, P. F. Lazorenko, E. L. Martín, D. Ségransan et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过超过8年的VLT/FORS2高精度天体测量监测，检测了早型L矮星DENIS-P J1253108–570924周围恒星位置的偏差，发现了亚木星质量系外行星候选体PALTA-1 b（质量0.54-0.77木星质量）。该行星的轨道半长轴为1.6-3.4天文单位，是已知环超冷矮星运行的为数不多的巨行星之一。这项对20颗邻近超冷矮星的系统巡天最终发现了该行星及3个新的超冷双星系统。
英文原摘要
We report the discovery of PALTA-1 b, a sub-Jupiter mass exoplanet candidate orbiting the nearby early-L dwarf DENIS-P J1253108–570924 (DE1253–57) located at a distance of ~17 pc. The exoplanet was discovered by monitoring the L dwarf’s astro-metric motion with FORS2 at the Very Large Telescope over more than eight years and characterising the deviations from the expected single-source motion. The estimated planet mass is between 0.54 M Jup and 0.77 M Jup , depending on the uncertain mass of the ultracool dwarf host. The system’s mass ratio is about 0.010, and the relative separation is estimated at 1.6–3.4 au. If confirmed, PALTA-1 b would be one of the very few known examples of a giant planet orbiting a nearby ultracool dwarf. This discovery finalises a larger astrometric planet search survey that targeted 20 nearby ultracool dwarfs and previously discovered three new ultracool binary systems.
astrometric · planet candidate

No Significant Rotation of the Spiral Structure in the IM Lup Protoplanetary Disk

Yuto Hiraide, Mastaka Aizawa, Munetake Momose · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过重新分析IM Lup原行星盘的多历元ALMA观测数据,采用基于可见度的连续谱建模方法改进了盘几何结构的推导,发现之前报告的m=2螺旋结构的开普勒式旋转可由盘几何参数的不匹配所解释。修正后的螺旋图案速度与零旋转相符,挑战了引力不稳定性驱动螺旋形成的结论,同时识别出可能由嵌入行星或晚期吸积引起的大尺度单臂螺旋结构。研究表明精确测定螺旋图案速度需要更长时间基线和更高分辨率的观测。
英文原摘要
Spiral structures are often observed in protoplanetary disks, but their origins remain uncertain. Yoshida et al. (2025) reported a pattern speed consistent with local Keplerian rotation for the $m=2$ spiral in the dusty disk around IM Lup using multi-epoch ALMA observations, supporting a gravitational instability (GI)-driven origin. However, recent studies revise the disk geometry of IM Lup, motivating a reassessment of its spiral pattern speed. In this study, we reanalyze the disk geometry of the same multi-epoch data using visibility-based continuum emission modeling. The inferred disk geometries differ from those adopted in previous studies by a few degrees but are consistent across epochs and with recent independent estimates based on gas emission analyses. The revised geometries yield weaker residuals and more physically plausible radial spiral phase profiles. Unlike previous studies, our remeasured spiral pattern speeds are consistent with zero rotation within the uncertainties. We conclude that the previously reported Keplerian-like motion can be explained by a mismatch in the adopted disk geometry, which affects the spiral phases measured in Band 6 (2017, 2019) and Band 7 (2024) differently. In addition, we identify a tentative large-scale one-armed spiral-like structure extending beyond the known $m=2$ spiral, which may provide clues to additional perturbations, such as an embedded planet or late-stage infall. Future high-resolution Band~6 observations with a longer time baseline will be essential for more accurately determining the spiral pattern speed.
protoplanetary disk

Using Structure Functions to Characterize Protoplanetary Disk Velocity Residuals

Richard Teague, Sean M. Andrews, Jaehan Bae, Marcelo Barraza-Alfaro et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究提出利用二阶结构函数作为工具，在高噪声背景下恢复原行星盘速度扰动的结构特性，包括关联长度、径向标度和各向异性。通过形态学模型和参数拟合相结合的方法，该框架能够在引力不稳定性、磁旋转不稳定性和垂直剪切不稳定性的数值模拟中高显著性地识别和区分不同的速度结构，并在TW Hya的观测数据上证实了方法的有效性。该研究为利用高分辨率观测刻画原行星盘中湍流速度结构提供了统计学基础，有助于识别行星-盘相互作用和磁流体力学过程的特征信号。
英文原摘要
Protoplanetary disks, the birthplace of planetary systems, are highly dynamic environments. High angular and spectral resolution observations have revealed non-Keplerian motions in nearly every source studied, indicative of (magneto-)hydrodynamical instabilities or planet-disk interactions. However, these velocity perturbations rarely exhibit ordered structure, limiting our ability to attribute them to a specific mechanism. We propose the second-order structure function as a tool for recovering structural properties of velocity perturbations in the presence of significant noise. Using morphological models, we demonstrate how easy-to-calculate heuristics and parametric model fits together identify structure in noisy data and recover its properties, including correlation lengths, their radial scaling where genuinely present, and the anisotropy. We further outline a workflow to de-noise the data and to build a matched null model against which inferences are compared. Applying the framework to one numerical simulation each of the gravitational, magneto-rotational and vertical-shear instabilities, we recover their structure at high significance and the structural heuristics separate all three pairs, with the gravitational and vertical-shear instability simulations remaining the hardest to distinguish once observationally degraded. As a demonstration on real data, 12CO observations of TW Hya reveal azimuthally elongated velocity structure that persists once the large-scale m=1 component is removed. We also delineate where a Gaussian description breaks down for coherent features such as spiral arms, and how inclination affects the recovered heuristics. The methodology is implemented in the eddy package and provides a foundation for statistically characterizing turbulent velocity structure in high-resolution disk observations.
protoplanetary disk

Coordinated Space and Ground Monitoring of Accretion Bursts in a Protoplanetary Disk: MIRI and iSHELL observe the long-term modulation of gas within a small inner disk cavity

Jacob Hyden, Andrea Banzatti, Benjamin M. Tofflemire, Sebastiaan Krijt et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过JWST-MIRI和IRTF-iSHELL等多台仪器联合监测原行星盘中的双星DQ Tau系统，在三个连续吸积喷发周期内追踪了分子气体（CO和H₂O）的长期变化特征。关键发现是热分子气体的发射线宽度和光度随时间递减（~30-35天），反映出发射区域在双星扰动的内空腔（~0.2-0.4 au）内温度下降并向外衰退的动力学过程，这种变化主要受盘动力学而非吸积变化驱动的化学过程控制。这表明气体质量在盘边缘的堆积每~4-5个轨道周期调制喷发强度，揭示了原行星盘内部动力学与吸积过程的耦合机制。
英文原摘要
DQ Tau is a TTauri eccentric binary with pulsed accretion bursts regulated by its $\sim 16$-day orbital period. We have monitored this system with seven instruments from ground and space over 40 days. We present results on molecular gas variability from nine JWST-MIRI and nine IRTF-iSHELL epochs over three sequential accretion bursts with accretion luminosity pulses increasing by factors of $\sim 9$-80. A fundamental new measurement from this analysis is the line broadening of CO (from iSHELL) and H$_2$O (from MIRI) lines, which we use to estimate Keplerian radii and their variability in parallel to the line luminosity. This unprecedented dataset clearly shows that the molecular emission does not react like HI and the 5-$μ$m continuum with each accretion burst: the line luminosity and width of all hot molecular gas tracers decrease over $\sim30$-35 days, consistent with an emitting region decreasing in temperature and mass as it recedes to larger radii within the $\sim 0.2$-0.4 au inner cavity perturbed by the binary. The infrared molecular spectrum of DQ Tau is affected more by disk dynamics then by chemical changes triggered by variable accretion, supporting model expectations for chemical relaxation timescales that are much longer than the duration of these accretion bursts ($\gg 10$ days). The variability observed in all tracers in this study is consistent with a scenario where gas mass builds up near the disk edge to modulate the burst peak intensity and gas density in the inner cavity every $\sim4$-5 orbital periods. The origin of this modulation is still unclear.
protoplanetary disk

Migration and eccentricity damping timescales in overstable resonant planetary systems: hydrodynamic and N-body simulations

Zahra Afkanpour, Sareh Ataiee, Anna B. T. Penzlin, Alexandros Ziampras et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过流体动力学模拟和N体模型比较，调查了共振多行星系统中行星迁移和离心率阻尼的时间尺度，发现这两个量随演化过程时间相关且会发生符号反转，违反了传统的常数假设。研究表明，包含共旋转力矩饱和和行星离心率的基于力矩的方法能更好地重现流体动力学演化，为共振行星系统的流体模拟与简化N体模型之间建立了物理联系。
英文原摘要
Tidal interactions between planets and discs modify semimajor axes and eccentricities. These effects are represented by migration and eccentricity damping timescales in analytical and Nbody models, although they are assumed constant. This approximation is uncertain in resonant multiplanet systems, where coupled planet disc evolution can produce non-linear torque feedback. We investigate migration and eccentricity damping in two planet systems undergoing resonant and overstable evolution near the 2:1 mean motion resonance. Using hydrodynamic simulations with varying disc surface densities, viscosities, and aspect ratios, we measure both timescales from energy and angular momentum exchange between the planets and disc. We find that both timescales are time dependent and vary throughout the evolution. In resonant systems, sign reversals occur owing to eccentricity oscillations and disc torque variations. Moreover, migration timescales normalised by their expected dependence on planetary mass, disc surface density, and aspect ratio deviate between models. Likewise, the common relation between migration and eccentricity damping timescales is not recovered. We compare the hydrodynamic evolution with several Nbody approaches. Models with constant migration and damping timescales fail to reproduce the hydrodynamic evolution, whereas torque-based approaches provide better agreement. In particular, approaches including corotation torque saturation and planetary eccentricity reproduce the hydrodynamic evolution in the type I migration regime, whereas additional corrections are required when planets partially open gaps. These results show that time-dependent torque-based prescriptions provide a consistent physical link between hydrodynamic simulations and simplified Nbody models of resonant planetary systems.
disc evolution

Desorption of Water from Cosmic Silicates

C. Jäger, D. Köhler, Th. Henning, F. Langenhorst · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过实验测量不同含水硅酸盐在加热条件下的水分子解吸过程，发现物理结合水在473 K以下解吸，而化学结合水（羟基和Mg-OH基团）在773-923 K高温才能释放。这些高温过程与非晶硅酸盐向镁质正交辉石和橄榄石的重结晶相伴随，与JWST观测到的原行星盘内部的结晶硅酸盐特征相符。研究提出了一个新的原行星盘内部水库富集机制：硅酸盐中化学结合水的解吸可在远高于水升华温度的条件下提供水分子，为类地行星的原位成长提供水源。
英文原摘要
Abstract Recent JWST and ALMA observations of protoplanetary disks have identified water reservoirs in the inner regions of the disks. This would provide terrestrial planets forming therein access to a water reservoir. In situ formation of water molecules on grain surfaces was proposed as one possible formation process in the PDS 70 system, but transport by small silicate particles coupled to the gas could be another mechanism. Laboratory experiments on the thermally induced desorption of water molecules from different water-containing silicates at elevated temperatures provide another possibility for the enrichment of water molecules in the inner disk. Physically and chemically bound water in silicates can be released upon annealing processes. Water that is physically bound undergoes desorption at temperatures up to 473 K. In contrast, chemically bound water in the form of different silanol or Mg–OH groups in amorphous silicates or phyllosilicates can be released at higher temperatures, ranging from 773 to 923 K. The relevant temperatures are sufficient to trigger the solid-state recrystallization of amorphous silicates to Mg-rich orthopyroxene and olivine. The existence of these crystalline silicates has been substantiated through Spitzer and JWST-MIRI observations. Based on the experimental results, we postulate that the desorption of bound hydroxyl groups may serve as a mechanism for the formation of the water reservoir at temperatures up to 900 K, much higher than the water sublimation temperature of 100–200 K.
protoplanetary disk

Emulating distributions of planetary system architectures

Ky Potter, Lucas Brefka, Derek Bingham, Eric B. Ford et al. · Celestial Mechanics and Dynamical Astronomy
本研究开发了一个统计仿真器，用于快速近似行星形成N体模拟的结果分布，显著降低了计算成本。该仿真器以检测到的行星多重性和相邻行星对的轨道周期比、过境时间比及Hill半径间距等可观测量为目标，结合多重性校准模型、灵活的条件分布回归和概率空间中的依赖性建模方法。通过这一框架，可将详细的动力学模型集成到系外行星群体的基于模拟的统计推断中，使严格的贝叶斯推理在大样本调查中变得可行。
英文原摘要
Abstract Inferring the distribution of exoplanetary system architectures from survey data increasingly relies on forward modeling with N -body simulations, but the computational cost of these models makes rigorous statistical inference impractical. We develop a statistical emulator for the distribution of outcomes of planet formation simulations, enabling fast conditional sampling in place of expensive integrations. The emulator targets the joint distribution of detected multiplicity and three observables derived from neighboring planet pairs: orbital period ratios, period-normalized transit duration ratios, and orbital spacing in mutual Hill radii. Our approach combines a calibrated model for multiplicity, flexible regression models for conditional distributions, dependence modeling in a transformed probability space, and methods to capture rare events in the tails. We introduce diagnostics to assess calibration, dependence preservation, and reproduction of rare events. The resulting emulator accurately captures heteroskedasticity, dependence structure, and tail behavior of the simulator outputs while reducing computational cost by several orders of magnitude relative to direct N -body simulation. This framework enables integration of detailed dynamical models into simulation-based inference for exoplanet populations.

An analytical framework for atmospheric tides on rocky planets. II. Semidiurnal thermotidal torque

Pierre Auclair-Desrotour, Jacques Laskar, Mohammad Farhat, Gwenaël Boué · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究建立了大气热潮汐对类地行星自转影响的解析框架，通过对绝热和等温大气的全面分析，揭示了热潮汐力矩与潮汐频率及系统参数的依赖关系，特别是阐明了Lamb波共振的作用机制。解析模型与数值GCM模拟结果吻合良好，准确捕捉了同步自转附近的力矩峰值和共振放大效应，并通过引入热滞后成功解释了GCM结果中制动力矩与加速力矩的不对称性，表明间接热强迫机制在某些情景下主导了大气热潮汐。
英文原摘要
The thermodynamic acceleration induced by atmospheric thermal tides has a major impact on the rotation of temperate rocky planets, where gravitational and radiative tidal forcings are of comparable strength. This acceleration drives planets away from synchronous rotation, thereby affecting their climate, large-scale circulation, and surface conditions. These implications underline the need to better constrain the dependence of the thermotidal torque on the tidal frequency and on the physical parameters characterising the star-planet system, as this relationship remains insufficiently understood. In particular, the role of resonantly excited Lamb waves requires clarification due to its potential impact on Earth's length of day (LOD) during the Precambrian. Building on a previous work, which provided a fully analytical formulation of the atmospheric tidal response of rocky planets, we apply this framework to isentropic and isothermal atmospheres. We perform a comprehensive functional analysis of the resulting torque and compare the analytical predictions with numerical solutions from earlier general circulation model (GCM) studies. The analytical model agrees well with GCM results, accurately capturing the frequency dependence of tidal torque both for paleo-Earth and present-day Earth. More generally, the solutions capture the torque peak near synchronous rotation and the amplification associated with Lamb-wave resonance. Near resonance, the GCM results exhibit a stronger braking torque than accelerating torque. This asymmetry is reproduced by allowing tidal heating to lag behind insolation. The agreement with GCM spectra suggests that thermal tides in the benchmark simulations are driven predominantly by an indirect thermal forcing characterised by a substantial time delay, in contrast to the direct absorption of sunlight that dominates semidiurnal tides on present-day Earth.
atmosphere · atmospheric

The Composition of M-dwarf Exoplanet Hosts: Atmospheric Parameters and O, Mg, Si, and Fe Abundances from IR Spectroscopy

Zachary G Maas, Natalie R. Hinkel, Gregory N. Mace, Cayman T. Unterborn et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用红外高分辨率光谱(IGRINS和NIRSPEC)测量了40颗系外行星宿主M矮星的大气参数和O、Mg、Si、Fe丰度,通过与MOOG和MARCS合成光谱模型对比获得了可靠的化学成分信息。结合运动学分析识别了潜在的银河厚盘星,并根据Si/Mg和Fe/Mg摩尔比推算出这些M矮星系外行星的核心质量分数约为20-40%,为行星形成和化学演化模型提供了重要的观测约束。
英文原摘要
Determining M-dwarf abundances is an important step to discerning the chemical evolution of the Milky Way and characterizing exoplanetary systems, especially since these objects commonly host small exoplanets. However, a dearth of M-dwarf abundance measurements exist compared to Sun-like stars, partially due to their complicated optical spectra dominated by molecular absorption lines and low luminosities. To overcome these issues, we have obtained high resolution H-band spectra of 40 planet-hosting M-dwarf stars and 2 M-dwarfs in wide binaries with FGK primaries with IGRINS on Gemini South and on the McDonald Observatory 2.7m telescope, and NIRSPEC on Keck. Abundances were derived by comparing each observed spectrum to synthetic spectral grids generated with MOOG and MARCS atmospheric models. Effective temperatures were derived using OH and H$_{2}$O lines and [Fe/H] from Fe I and FeH lines. Both Mg and Si abundances were determined using atomic features. Overall, we find that our abundances are broadly consistent with previous literature M-dwarf abundances. We complemented our abundances analysis with UVW velocities and Milky Way stellar population membership probabilities and identified 2 potential thick disk stars from our kinematic and chemical analysis: GJ 1252 and L181-1 which was a previously known thick disk star. Kinematic stellar population probabilities and a low [Fe/H] abundance makes Kepler 42 a likely thick disk star, although uncertain Mg and Si abundances make classification difficult. Lastly, we have determined Si/Mg and Fe/Mg molar ratios for M-dwarf exoplanet hosts and find the abundance distributions leads to exoplanet core-mass fractions between 20 - 40$\%$.

Searching for Massive Remnant Planetary System Hosts with White Dwarf Gaia XP Spectra

Lou Baya Ould Rouis, J. J. Hermes, Pierre Bergeron, Simon Blouin et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用盖亚XP低分辨率光谱搜寻并确认高质量白矮星周围的残留行星系统，通过后续低分辨率光谱观测识别金属污染特征，确认了82%的候选体具有光球金属丰度。研究表明盖亚XP光谱能有效发现金属污染白矮星，但可靠的质量测定需要金属富集大气拟合而非仅依赖测光质量，从而为行星系统存活和形成提供了中等质量恒星演化端点的约束证据。该工作扩展了白矮星系外行星宿主样本，并发现了新的过渡碎片系统SDSS J1624+5404。
英文原摘要
Abstract Remnant planetary systems around massive white dwarfs ( M WD > 0.8 M ⊙ ) are intrinsically rare and remain poorly characterized. As the evolutionary endpoints of intermediate-mass B- and A-type stars, these white dwarfs provide valuable constraints on the formation and survival of planetary systems around higher-mass progenitors. In the absence of large, dedicated spectroscopic surveys, extremely low-resolution Gaia XP spectra provide a powerful means of identifying candidate metal-polluted white dwarfs. We obtained follow-up observations with low-resolution optical spectroscopy ( R = 500–4000) to search primarily for Ca ii H&K absorption in massive, metal-polluted white dwarf candidates selected from Gaia XP spectra to increase the sample of massive remnant planetary system hosts among objects with effective temperatures below ∼15,000 K. We find that Gaia XP spectra recover metal-polluted white dwarfs efficiently, confirming 82% ± 5% recovery of photospheric metals (59 of 72 targets) in high-confidence candidates of our sample (with classifier probability P DZ > 0.65). However, Gaia photometric masses derived from G , G BP , and G RP broadband photometry are not sufficient to identify the most massive systems, as reliable mass determinations require detailed metal-enriched atmospheric fits. We present a selection of cool white dwarfs confirmed to be both massive and metal-polluted, demonstrating that Gaia XP spectra can substantially expand the sample of white dwarf exoplanet hosts. Our search led to the discovery of a new transiting debris system, SDSS J1624+5404, the 21st published system to date and eighth to show periodic transits.

Accelerating Radiative Transfer in Planetary Atmospheres by Orders of Magnitude with Neural Network Emulators

Isaac Malsky, Tiffany Kataria, Natasha E. Batalha, Matthew Graham · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究采用编码器专用的transformer神经网络架构开发了行星大气辐射传输模拟器，在太阳成分热木星大气的一维剖面数据上进行训练，实现了与传统方法相比平均误差~1%的精度，同时在GPU批处理推理中获得100倍以上的速度提升。与LSTM基线模型的对比表明，在有限训练数据条件下transformer具有更高的准确性和鲁棒性，该机器学习模拟方法有望为行星大气模型中提供更快更精准的辐射传输计算。
英文原摘要
Abstract Radiative transfer calculations are essential for modeling planetary atmospheres. However, standard methods are computationally demanding and impose accuracy-speed trade-offs. High computational costs force numerical simplifications in large models (e.g., general circulation models) that degrade the accuracy of the simulation. Radiative transfer calculations are an ideal candidate for machine learning emulation; fundamentally, radiative transfer is a well-defined physical mapping from a static atmospheric profile to the resulting fluxes, and high-fidelity training data can be created from first-principles calculations. We developed a radiative transfer emulator using an encoder-only transformer neural network architecture, trained on 1D profiles representative of solar-composition hot Jupiter atmospheres. Our emulator reproduced bolometric two-stream layer fluxes with mean test set errors of ∼1% compared to the traditional method and achieved speedups of more than 100× in batched GPU inference. In matched comparisons against a long short-term memory model (LSTM) baseline, a transformer was consistently the more accurate and more robust architecture when training data were limited, while the LSTM achieved better accuracy at full scale. Emulating radiative transfer with machine learning opens up the possibility for faster and more accurate routines within planetary atmospheric models.
atmosphere · atmospheric

Robustness of C/O and mean molecular weight under disequilibrium chemistry

B. Burt, K. Heng, A. J. Cridland, S.-M. Tsai · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过结合解析不确定性传播和光化学正向模型,评估了利用凌日光谱推导的C/O比、O/H比和平均分子量(μ)等成分指标的可靠性。结果表明,分子丰度不确定性对元素丰度比(特别是C/O和O/H)产生了相当的分数不确定性,而对H₂/He主导大气中的μ的影响小两个数量级;光化学和垂直混合对凌日光谱探测深度处的C/O和μ影响有限。研究建议C/O仍是大气化学和行星形成历史的重要诊断工具,但需谨慎进行不确定性传播,而平均分子量可作为连接可观测大气与行星本体成分的相对稳定的种群诊断指标,特别是在亚海王星半径谷的研究中。
英文原摘要
Transmission spectroscopy increasingly reduces exoplanet spectra to derived compositional metrics, such as C/O, O/H, and mean molecular weight (μ), but these quantities do not carry molecular abundance uncertainties in the same way. We assessed the reliability of these metrics for hot Jupiters and sub-Neptunes. We combined analytic uncertainty propagation with photochemical forward models and representative published retrieval constraints. VULCAN Propagating molecular abundance uncertainties produced substantially larger fractional uncertainties in elemental abundance ratios than in μ. The fractional uncertainties in C/O and O/H were typically comparable to the inferred ratios, whereas the fractional uncertainty in μ was more than two orders of magnitude smaller in /He-dominated atmospheres. Photochemistry and vertical mixing redistributed individual molecules but produced only minor changes in C/O and μ at the pressures probed by transmission spectroscopy. H2 C/O remains an important diagnostic of atmospheric chemistry and planetary formation history, but its interpretation requires careful uncertainty propagation. Mean molecular weight may provide a comparatively stable population-level diagnostic for linking observable atmospheres to bulk composition, especially across the sub-Neptune radius valley.

On the Detectability of Compact Multi-Planet Systems via Gravitational Microlensing

Stephen R. Kane · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究计算了类似TRAPPIST-1系统的紧凑多行星系统作为引力微透镜透镜体时的可探测性,发现各行星产生的微透镜信号主要由八极矩项主导,7颗行星的联合信号即使在1毫星等精度下也仅为Δχ²~10⁻³,远低于标准探测阈值5个数量级。只有当行星质量增加约300倍(超过系统Hill稳定性临界值)或透镜距离极近(~15 pc以内)时才能实现探测,表明南希·格蕾丝·罗曼空间望远镜的微透镜通道无法有效探测这类系统,而其凌星通道则可在同一视场大量探测此类行星。
英文原摘要
Compact multi-planet systems orbiting ultracool dwarfs, such as TRAPPIST-1, may be a major population of terrestrial worlds with poorly constrained demographics. Individual close-in planets are expected to escape microlensing detection, but whether a compact system can produce a detectable collective signal remains to be quantified. We calculate the microlensing signal from each TRAPPIST-1 planet, and of all seven combined, if the system were a lens toward the bulge. For a 0.09 $M_\odot$ lens at 4 kpc, $R_E \approx 1.21$ AU, placing the planets at $s = 0.010$--$0.051~θ_E$, 12--63 times interior to the lensing zone. We measure each signal as the residual after fitting a single-lens model, which accounts for the barycentric dipole. Because the quadrupole term cancels between the two images to first order, the residual scales as the octupole ($q s^3$), reaching $|δA / A| \sim 10^{-6}$. The seven planets combined yield $Δχ^2 \sim 10^{-3}$ even at 1~mmag precision, five orders of magnitude below the standard detection threshold. Detection requires increasing all planet masses by a factor of $\sim$300, whereas the resonant chain loses its guarantee of Hill stability at a factor of $\sim$7. Only a lens within $\sim$15 pc of the source or the Sun places the outer planets in the lensing zone. The Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope is therefore not expected to detect a TRAPPIST-1-like system through microlensing, whereas its transit channel in the same fields can detect such planets in large numbers, marking a sharp boundary in the Galactic census between microlensing and transit sensitivity.

Simultaneous photometric, spectroscopic, and FAST radio observations of AD Leo

Tianhao Su, Liyun Zhang, Qingfeng Pi, Linyan Jiang · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过FAST射电望远镜、光学分光和测光观测同步监测活跃M矮星AD Leo，探究其耀斑事件中射电发射与色球活动的关联机制。观测发现4次射电爆发事件具有高圆偏振和毫秒量级精细结构，同时检测到32次光学耀斑，其能量分布遵循幂律分布，部分射电爆发与光学耀斑衰退相位及色球增强具有时间相关性。研究结果表明AD Leo耀斑中射电和光学活动存在可能但不确定的时间关联，为理解磁活跃恒星磁层非热过程提供了多波段观测证据。
英文原摘要
Aims. AD Leo is a magnetically active M-dwarf star, well known for its frequent and intense flare activity. These flares manifest across a broad range of wavelengths, from radio to optical: radio observations probe non-thermal processes in the magnetosphere, while optical photometry and spectroscopy trace the responses from the chromosphere and photosphere. However, multi-wavelength coordinated observations of AD Leo remain scarce. In this study, we conducted simultaneous multi-band observations of AD Leo, including radio and optical light curves as well as spectroscopic data, to investigate the relationship between chromospheric activity and radio emission during flare events. Methods. The Five-hundred-metre Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST) data cover the 1-1.5 GHz frequency range, and dynamic spectra were used to identify radio signals from AD Leo. Optical spectroscopic observations span the 3900-9800,Å wavelength range, and chromospheric activity was analysed using key diagnostic lines such as Hα, Hβ, Hγ, and He I D3. For the photometric data, flare events were detected and characterized by applying a repeated fitting method to the light curves obtained from Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) and the 85 cm telescope. Results. We detected four radio burst events with FAST during two observing epochs, characterized by high circular polarization, narrow bandwidths, and millisecond to sub-millisecond fine structures. The polarization handedness is stable within individual events but differs between epochs. Optical spectroscopy reveals 16 flare events with significant enhancements in multiple chromospheric lines, with Hα flare energies of ∼ 9.97 -6.27 29 Optical photometry identifies 32 flares from the 85 cm telescope, with flare energies ranging from to 10^33.55,erg and a flare frequency distribution slope of α ≈ -1.02, and 144 flares from TESS, spanning bolometric energies of with a slope of α ≈ -0.94. For the 2025 January 12 event, no reliable optical counterpart could be established because simultaneous 85 cm photometry was unavailable and the spectroscopic observations did not cover the radio-burst interval. On 2025 February 06, one radio burst temporally overlapped the decay phase of a small optical flare and enhanced chromospheric emission, whereas the other radio bursts occurred later. These observations therefore suggest a possible, but not definitive, temporal association between the radio and optical activity.

Imaging exoplanets below the telescope stability limit

Rodrigo Ferrer-Chavez, Jason J. Wang, Katherine L. Bouman, William T. Freeman et al. · Nature
本研究开发了一种基于物理模型的新型消星光方法，结合多步优化和自动微分技术来建立灵活的星光模型，能够有效补偿仪器不稳定性对系外行星成像的影响。在詹姆斯·韦伯太空望远镜NIRCam冠状仪的观测中，该方法相比标准技术灵敏度提升2-15倍，实现了对51 Eridani b在4.3 μm波段的首次探测。这项工作突破了望远镜稳定性限制，有望推动系外行星直接成像能力的提升，为未来罗马太空望远镜和宜居世界观测台等任务实现宜居地球成像创造条件。
英文原摘要
Abstract Exoplanet imaging is the main method for detailed characterization of planets orbiting beyond 1 au (ref. 1 ) but it requires suppressing the bright glare of the star to see the faint planet light. As well as sophisticated instrumentation 2,3 , effective image processing is necessary for starlight suppression, which continues to be a substantial limitation 4 . Standard algorithms use reference images for starlight modelling and thus they are limited by inevitable telescope instabilities that change from reference to science observation 5,6 . Addressing this challenge will increase the number of planets amenable to direct imaging and will be a notable step towards imaging exo-Earths with future observatories 7 . Here we present a new physics-based method for starlight subtraction. Our method combines a multistep optimization with a flexible physical model to produce a starlight model, allowing modelling and compensation of instrument instabilities. We use automatic differentiation (autodiff) 8 to optimize millions of model parameters. We demonstrate our method on observations from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) Near-InfraRed Camera (NIRCam) 9–11 coronagraph instrument. We achieve sensitivity 2–15 times superior to standard techniques, including the first detection of 51 Eridani b (ref. 12 ) at 4.3 μm. This is the first time, to our knowledge, that such an approach has demonstrated substantially improved sensitivity on real, on-sky coronagraphic imaging. Our framework can be extended to other current and future ground-based and space-based observatories, including the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope and the Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO), potentially relaxing the unprecedented stability requirements needed to make these a success.

The ALMA survey to Resolve exoKuiper belt Substructures (ARKS) XII. Constraints on large bodies embedded in discs

M. R. Jankovic, A. V. Krivov, T. D. Pearce, A. A. Sefilian et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过ALMA观测11个碎屑盘的毫米波数据和垂直厚度测量，采用碰撞级联和引力散射半解析模型，约束了盘内大天体的尺度和质量。研究发现供应碰撞级联的微行星小于20 km，总质量低于200地球质量，而引力扰动盘的天体虽然更大但不一定含有大部分盘质量。观测到的尘埃质量和盘的垂直厚度可分别由稳态碰撞模型和嵌入式大天体的引力扰动解释，但需要更先进的模型来解释非高斯垂直分布和厚度对粒径与半径的依赖关系。
英文原摘要
The ALMA survey to Resolve exoKuiper belt Substructures (ARKS) presents detailed constraints on structure and properties of a sample of 24 debris discs at millimetre wavelengths, including measurements of vertical thicknesses for a subsample of discs. We estimated sizes and total masses of large bodies that feed collisional cascades producing the observed millimetre dust in 11 ARKS discs with a well constrained vertical thickness. Separately, we estimated the sizes and total masses of large bodies that could be gravitationally stirring these discs, assuming that such stirring is the origin of the vertical thickness. We used a semi-analytic particle-in-a-box collisional model and a semi-analytic model of stirring through gravitational scattering. We accounted for the dependence of collisional damping efficiency on the average collision velocity. We find that planetesimals feeding the collisional cascades are less than 20 km in size, while the total masses in the planetesimals are lower than 200 M$_\oplus$. There is a good agreement between minimum disc masses inferred from millimetre and infrared observations. The hypothetical stirrers are always larger in size than the planetesimals, but they do not necessarily contain most of the disc mass. In agreement with previous work, the observed dust masses can be explained by steady-state collisional models with reasonable disc masses. The measured vertical scale-heights can be explained by gravitational stirring from embedded large bodies of reasonable properties. However, more advanced models are needed to address the observed non-Gaussian vertical profiles, along with the dependence of disc scale-height on particle size and stellocentric distance.

A comparison of particle and fluid approaches for modeling the streaming instability in global patch simulations

Dominik Hetzel Ostertag, Mario Flock, Paul Rosendahl, Andrea Mignone et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过全球盘片模型中的二维轴对称模拟，比较了流体和拉格朗日粒子两种方法在模拟流动不稳定性中的结果，采用FARGO3D和PLUTO两个代码分别进行尘埃流体和流体/粒子模拟。研究追踪了尘埃尺度高度、中面尘气比和最大尘埃密度等关键参数，分辨率研究表明流体模拟在高分辨率下收敛到高斯分布，而粒子模拟需要更高分辨率；两种方法在最高分辨率下的最大尘埃密度相差36%。综合计算性能和数值收敛性，流体方法被证实是研究流动不稳定性和行星形成中尘埃团聚的更高效且可靠的工具。
英文原摘要
Identifying the process relevant to planet formation is still a major task. Mechanisms have been identified that can trap dust and prevent it from migrating inward. A particular hydrodynamic instability, known as the streaming instability, is of high interest because it might cause the formation of dust clumps from which the first planetesimals form. We compared the outcome of nonlinear streaming instability simulations using two fundamental approaches in a more realistic global disk model. The results were not used to identify or disentangle the effect of numerical diffusion in fluid simulations, for instance, or the possibility of crossing particle streams. Instead, they enabled us to determine whether the Lagrangian particle approach is most suitable for numerical studies of the streaming instability. We performed 2D axisymmetric global and isothermal hydrodynamic simulations using the two codes FARGO3D (dust fluid) and PLUTO (fluid and particles) to investigate the temporal evolution of dust scale height, the midplane dust-to-gas ratio, and the maximum dust density, as well as to study velocity distributions and vorticities. For PLUTO particle simulations, we also investigated the influence of the gas Riemann solver and spatial reconstruction orders. Our results from a resolution study of up to 5120 cells per gas scale height indicate convergence between PLUTO fluid and FARGO3D. The two codes yield a Gaussian profile that is dominated by small-scale dust-depleted voids. The particle simulations with PLUTO have not yet converged because a transient midplane dust component persists, indicating the need for an even higher resolution or more particles. A more accurate Riemann solver or a higher-order spatial reconstruction scheme helps to cause the dust density profile to approach the converged structure. At the highest resolution, the maximum dust density in fluid simulations is only 36$%$ of that in particle simulations, which is similar to other code-comparison works. When the streaming instability is studied in a global sense, the choice of the Riemann solver and spatial reconstruction order becomes more important than the approach itself (fluid or particles). When the computational performance of the two dust treatments is included, the fluid approach provides an efficient and suitable tool for streaming instability simulations.

The impact of M dwarf stellar convection on exoplanetary atmospheric signatures. A 3D radiation-hydrodynamics simulation analysis for high-resolution spectroscopy

A. Chiavassa, M. Brogi, T. Masseron, R. Van den Broeck et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过3D辐射流体动力学模拟M矮星对流层的光谱特征，结合高分辨率光谱观测和过境分析，调查了恒星对流对系外行星大气检测的影响。研究发现，中心到边缘变化和Rossiter-McLaughlin效应产生的恒星噪声与系外行星信号相当，需要通过主成分分析等方法进行去趋势处理，同时揭示了当前水分子线表数据的不准确性。该工作建立了一个综合框架，为未来极大型望远镜对M矮星周围行星大气的无偏测量奠定了基础。
英文原摘要
M dwarf stars represent prime targets for the detection and characterization of small, temperate exoplanets; however, their complex activity and convective surfaces pose significant challenges for high-resolution spectroscopic studies. This work aims to investigate the impact of stellar convection on high-resolution spectroscopic observations of M dwarf stars and develop an integrated approach combining 3D radiative hydrodynamical simulations, spectroscopic modeling, and transit analysis to mitigate stellar noise. We employ 3D radiative hydrodynamical simulations of M dwarf stellar convection to generate synthetic spectra. We compare the synthetic spectra to actual observations of GJ 3470. We model a high-resolution transit sequence of the Neptune-size GJ 3470 b, including centre-to-limb variations (CLVs) and the Rossiter-McLaughlin effect (RME), and process the sequence via principal component analysis (PCA) to de-trend stellar effects. Comparison of synthetic spectra with actual observations shows that current H2O line lists remain inaccurate, with modest agreement in line strength and position for the chosen K-band interval. During the transit of GJ 3470 b, the amplitude of CLVs+RME is comparable to exoplanet signals. Current PCA can in principle de-trend such effects, but its behaviour is highly dependent on the copresence of telluric and planetary signals. While we are able to correctly detect the exoplanet with no evident bias from stellar residuals, this result is limited to a narrow spectral interval and a specific orbital configuration. This study demonstrates that 3D radiative hydrodynamical simulations are essential for accurately modeling convection-induced spectral variability in M dwarfs. Our integrated framework establishes a foundation to obtain unbiased measurements from future ELT observations of exoplanets orbiting M dwarfs.

M. Hadjara, A. Pomazan, H. Ji, Q. Dong et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对当前地基巡天对小型近地小行星(140 m以下)和太阳方向天体的观测盲点问题,提出在月球建立观测站的科学需求。研究采用在地球极端环境(阿塔卡玛沙漠、蒙古、南极、撒哈拉沙漠)部署和测试小行星巡天望远镜的方法,验证月面观测所需的关键技术在恶劣条件下的可行性。最终目标是建立地月一体化行星防御观测网络,显著提升近地小行星的早期预警能力。
英文原摘要
Near-Earth Asteroids (NEAs) represent both a scientific opportunity and a potential threat to human civilization. While current survey programs have successfully detected most large NEAs, a significant observational gap remains for objects smaller than approximately 140 m, which can still produce regional-scale devastation. Ground-based surveys are intrinsically limited by atmospheric conditions, weather, and solar avoidance constraints, which leave a significant fraction of near-Sun objects difficult to detect. In particular, Earth-based observatories remain largely blind to objects approaching from the Sun-ward direction, reducing available warning times. Future lunar observatories offer a promising solution to this limitation. A telescope operating on the Moon could enable observations of regions inaccessible from Earth, provide enhanced sensitivity, and significantly improve early detection capabilities. However, the lunar environment imposes severe engineering constraints, including thermal cycling, dust contamination, radiation exposure, and autonomous operation requirements. We propose the AMAS(Atacama-Mongolia-Antarctica-Sahara) project, an international initiative using Earth's most extreme environments as analogue sites for future lunar astronomical facilities. By deploying and testing specialized NEA survey telescopes in these environments, key technologies for future lunar observatories can be validated under realistic operational conditions. This paper presents the scientific rationale, engineering challenges, site selection strategy, and long-term roadmap toward a global Earth-Moon network for planetary defense.

The Hot Neptune Initiative (HONEI): IV. TOI-4129 b, an Adolescent Neptune in the Ridge

英文原摘要
Recent exoplanet demographics studies have uncovered the Neptune ridge: an overdensity of intermediate-size planets at orbital periods between 3--6~d. The origin of this pile-up remains unclear, but it could be a consequence of high-eccentricity migration (HEM). Here, we confirm TOI-4129\,b, a planet that may help to reveal the origin of the Neptune ridge. Using radial velocities from HARPS-N and transit photometry from TESS and four ground-based telescopes, we found that TOI-4129\,b has a relatively low density ($R_p = 5.7 \pm 0.2\, R_\oplus$, $M_p = 26\pm 6 \,M_\oplus$, $ρ_p = 0.8 \pm 0.2 ~\textrm{g}~\textrm{cm}^{-3}$) and orbits a $V = 12.9$ K dwarf star every 3.27~d, placing it at the inner edge of the Neptune ridge. We detect the stellar rotation period at 12.7~d in the photometry, implying a fairly young gyrochronal age of $1.20^{+0.16}_{-0.30}~$Gyr for the system. With a Transmission Spectroscopy Metric of $112\pm 30$, this adolescent Neptune is a good target for atmospheric observations with \textit{JWST} and \textit{Ariel}, and we also find that it is amenable to future constraints on the (three-dimensional) stellar obliquity. TOI-4129\,b will therefore be an important testing ground for the idea that planets in the Neptune ridge underwent HEM to arrive at their current locations.

The Neptunian ridge through the lens of TESS

Amadeo Castro-González · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用TESS数据独立验证了海王星山脊现象——在轨道周期3-6天处海王星大小行星的过密集区域，其出现率是邻近区域的两倍以上（99.9%置信度）。通过贝叶斯模型比较，发现对数正态分布优于单一幂律，暗示山脊具有不对称的形态，内侧边界较陡峭而外侧逐渐下降。这一形态特征与高偏心率迁移理论的潮汐破裂和轨道圆化效应预测相符，而PLATO数据将进一步约束山脊的精确形貌以验证其动力学起源。
英文原摘要
The Neptunian desert and savanna are separated by a ridge, an overdensity at orbital periods of approximately 3-6 d identified with Kepler. Like the hot Jupiter pileup at larger radii, this feature has been linked to the accumulation of successful outcomes of high-eccentricity migration (HEM). We revisit the ridge independently using TESS occurrence posteriors for FGK main-sequence stars. We quantify its excess over adjacent regions and compare six occurrence profiles using leave-one-out cross-validation and Bayesian evidence. For planets with radii of approximately 5-8 $R_\oplus$, the ridge has twice the occurrence density of the denser neighbouring region ($J=2.05^{+0.52}_{-0.44}$), with 99.9% posterior support (one-sided Gaussian equivalent of $3.1σ$). This contrast agrees with the discovery study and earlier Kepler occurrence measurements. Both predictive performance and Bayesian evidence strongly favour profiles peaking within the ridge over a single power law. The lognormal is preferred, suggesting a narrower inner wing and a more extended outer wing, although evidence for asymmetry remains weak. In the same period window, the analysis recovers the hot Jupiter pileup (approximately 10-16 $R_\oplus$) with a contrast consistent with that of the ridge and excludes an analogous excess among sub-Neptunes (approximately 2-4 $R_\oplus$) with $3.4σ$ posterior support. TESS thus provides an independent detection of the Neptunian ridge. Its possible asymmetry would be compatible with HEM, in which tidal disruption could produce a sharper inner edge and variations in circularisation efficiency a more gradual outer decline. Establishing whether the ridge has this shape requires more precise occurrence measurements, with PLATO's expected planet yield offering a promising opportunity to help unveil its morphology.
