POSEIDON. III. The Aligned Orbit of the Hot Neptune around the Hot Star WASP-195

Juan I. Espinoza-Retamal, Joshua N. Winn, Rafael Brahm, Luke B. Handley et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过Keck Planet Finder和NEID分光仪观测WASP-195系统中热海王星的Rossiter-McLaughlin效应,测得恒星自转与行星轨道的天球投影偏角λ = -10° ± 7°,表明该系统具有自旋-轨道对齐的特征。联合分析表明这一对齐的近圆形轨道与盘驱动迁移的形成历史相符,同时揭示了快速自转恒星周围热海王星测量稀少的原因在于其内禀出现率低和传统径向速度确认的困难,但快速自转反而能增强RM效应的可探测性,为突破小行星确认的瓶颈并扩展热星周围小行星的自旋偏角统计提供了新途径。
英文原摘要
Abstract Stellar obliquities provide important clues as to the formation and migration histories of planetary systems, but measurements remain scarce for Neptune-mass planets, especially those orbiting hot stars (above the Kraft break). Here we present observations of the Rossiter–McLaughlin (RM) effect in the hot-star/hot-Neptune system WASP-195 ( T eff = 6470 ± 100 K, v sin i ⋆ = 10.5 ± 1.1 km s −1 ) obtained with the Keck Planet Finder and NEID spectrographs. A joint analysis of these observations, archival photometry, and archival radial velocities yields a sky-projected stellar obliquity of λ = −10° ± 7°, consistent with spin–orbit alignment. This makes WASP-195 one of the few hot-star/hot-Neptune systems with a measured obliquity. Archival radial velocities from SOPHIE exclude Jupiter-mass planets within approximately 3 au at 5 σ confidence. The aligned and nearly circular orbit is naturally consistent with a history of disk-driven migration, although coplanar high-eccentricity migration or Roche-lobe overflow cannot be ruled out. We also investigate why so few Neptunes around hot stars have measured obliquities. Their scarcity likely reflects a combination of the lower intrinsic occurrence of short-period Neptunes around hot stars and the difficulty of confirming planet candidates in this regime, where rapid stellar rotation broadens spectral lines and hampers conventional radial-velocity confirmation. Rapid rotation also increases the detectability of the RM effect, a feature that could help to widen the planet confirmation bottleneck while expanding the obliquity census of small planets around hot stars.

Fitting Resonant Exoplanets Orbits. I. Properties and Applications of Fixed-Point Orbits

Chris W. Ormel, Tian Yi, Shuo Huang · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究开发了一种基于固定点轨道(fPO)的轨道拟合方法,将系外行星轨道限制在与平均运动共振相关的稳定周期构型中,通过减少自由参数并内禀地保证稳定性来改进传统轨道拟合方法。我们验证了耗散演化将共振系统驱向fPO状态,并将该方法应用于PDS 70、HR 8799和GJ 876等系统的观测数据,为这些系统中存在的共振配置提供了独立的动力学证据。这项工作为深入理解多行星系统的形成和早期动力学演化奠定了基础。
英文原摘要
Intact chains of mean-motion resonances may preserve information about the formation and early dynamical evolution of multiplanet systems. Although most mature multiplanet systems are non-resonant, the resonant fraction may have been substantially higher at young ages. Establishing whether exoplanet data are consistent with a resonant configuration is often challenging because the planetary orbits must be sufficiently well constrained. Moreover, while resonance configurations promote stability, standard orbital fitting methods do not impose such a dynamical constraint, with stability tested only a posteriori. As the first step in a series of papers aimed at favoring stable orbital solutions during the fitting process, we develop a method that restricts solutions to stable fixed-point orbits (fPOs)---coplanar, exactly periodic configurations associated with resonant dynamics. We show that dissipative evolution drive resonant systems toward an fPO state, motivating for fPO-constrained fitting for those planet systems that have avoided large-scale dynamical rearrangements since formation. The fPO model greatly reduces the number of free parameters and yields stable solutions by construction. We determine the best-fitting fPos through $χ^2$ minimization and apply the method to astrometric data for the directly imaged PDS 70 and HR 8799 systems and to radial-velocity data for GJ 876. For HR 8799 we independently recover a previously reported resonant solution, illustrating the fidelity of our method. For PDS 70 we find solutions fall in two groups, with the group where the outer planet c mass exceeds planet b more aligned with previous measurements. Finally, For GJ 876 our fPO fit is on par with previous unconstrained fitting, which strengthens the case that its planets reside deep in resonance.
radial velocity · astrometric

The Giant Planet Mass Function Across Stellar Mass and Metallicity: Massive Stars Make Massive Planets

Judah Van Zandt, Brendan Bowler · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用加州遗产巡天的719颗FGKM星及其径向速度和盖亚天体测量数据，调查了巨行星伴星质量分布与恒星质量和金属丰度的关系。通过考虑巡天完备性和参数不确定性的分层统计分析，我们发现高质量恒星（>1 M☉）和高金属丰度（[Fe/H]>0）的恒星周围不仅巨行星发生率更高，而且特征行星质量显著更大，表明恒星性质影响的不仅是伴星频率，还有其质量分布。在核吸积框架下，这一结果可能反映更大质量恒星具有更大的气体储备，或金属丰富盘中更早的核心形成使得气体吸积时间更长。
英文原摘要
Although giant planet occurrence correlates with stellar mass and metallicity, it remains unclear whether these properties also shape the companion mass distribution. We investigate this question using 719 FGKM stars from the California Legacy Survey and companion true-mass constraints from radial velocities and Hipparcos-Gaia astrometry. Our hierarchical occurrence analysis accounts for survey completeness and companion parameter uncertainties over giant planet and brown dwarf masses of M_c=0.4-50 M_Jup and semi-major axes of a=0.1-10 AU. We find that, compared to the rate among our full sample of 0.19 +/- 0.02 planets per star in this interval, giant planet occurrence is significantly enhanced around stars with high mass (M_star>1 M_sun; 0.39 (+0.06/-0.05) planets per star) and high metallicity ([Fe/H]>0.0; 0.30 (+0.04/-0.03) planets per star). We find evidence at the 2-3 sigma level that the planet mass function shape varies with stellar mass, with higher characteristic planet masses around stars with M_star>1 M_sun than around lower-mass hosts, based on differences in both the CDFs of fitted models and constraints on model shape parameters. For the full sample, occurrence declines above a few Jupiter masses and is consistent with either a plateau below ~3 M_Jup or a peak near 1-2 M_Jup. The brown dwarf desert begins at a companion-to-host mass ratio of 0.013-0.02. These results suggest that stellar properties influence not only the frequency of giant companions but also their characteristic masses. Within the core accretion framework, this shift could reflect larger gas reservoirs around more massive stars or earlier core formation in metal-rich disks, allowing greater gas accretion before disk dispersal.
astrometry

Image-constrained Modeling with Hubble and Keck Images Reveals that OGLE-2012-BLG-0563Lb is a Jupiter-mass Planet Orbiting a K Dwarf

David P. Bennett, Aparna Bhattacharya, Jean-Philippe Beaulieu, Naoki Koshimoto et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究采用哈勃太空望远镜和凯克自适应光学高分辨率成像数据，通过图像约束建模方法分析OGLE-2012-BLG-0563Lb微引力透镜事件的光变曲线，精确确定该系统中主星质量为0.801±0.033 M☉、行星质量为1.116±0.087 MJup，轨道分离存在近-远双重简并性。该方法识别并纠正了地基光度测量中的系统误差，表明图像约束建模对于未来罗曼空间望远镜数据的质量检验具有重要价值。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present high-angular-resolution imaging from the Hubble Space Telescope combined with adaptive optics imaging results from the Keck-II telescope to determine the mass of the OGLE-2012-BLG-0563L host star and planet to be M host = 0.801 ± 0.033 M ⊙ and M planet = 1.116 ± 0.087 M Jupiter , respectively, located at a distance of D L = 5.46 ± 0.56 kpc. There is a close–wide degeneracy in the light-curve models that indicates a star–planet projected separation of 1.50 ± 0.16 au for the close model and 8.41 ± 0.87 au for the wide model. We used the image-constrained modeling method to analyze the light-curve data with constraints from the high-angular-resolution image analysis. This revealed systematic errors in some of the ground-based light-curve photometry, which led to an estimate of the angular Einstein Radius, θ E , that was too large by a factor of ∼2. The host star mass is a factor of 2.4 larger than the value presented in the A. Fukui et al. discovery paper. Although most systematic photometry errors seen in ground-based microlensing light-curve photometry will not be repeated in data from the Roman Space Telescope’s Galactic Bulge Time Domain Survey, we argue that image-constrained modeling will be a valuable method for identifying possible systematic errors in Roman photometry.
microlensing

The PLATO Input Catalog of targets (tPIC) for the first Long-duration Observation Phase field

M. Montalto, G. Piotto, P. M. Marrese, L. Prisinzano et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
PLATO任务旨在探测太阳型恒星宜居带内的类地行星,本研究利用Gaia DR3的天体测量和测光数据以及星际介质三维图,为PLATO首个长期观测阶段南场(LOPS2)编制了包含217,741颗恒星的目标输入星表(tPIC2.2),其中包括FGK型矮星/亚巨星202,315颗和M矮星15,037颗。通过开发同质化算法从天体测量和测光参数推导恒星基本物理参数,该星表满足PLATO任务的所有科学需求,并为后续的宜居带行星探测提供了优化的观测目标资源。
英文原摘要
The ESA PLAnetary Transits and Oscillations of stars (PLATO) mission is designed to detect terrestrial planets in the habitable zones of solar-type stars. Owing to telemetry constraints, the selection of PLATO targets must be performed in advance. We present the first public release of the PLATO Input Catalog of targets (tPIC2.2), which provides the list of stars that will be observed during PLATO’s first Long-duration Observation Phase field at South (LOPS2) as part of its core science program. We used astrometric and photometric data from Gaia data release 3 (DR3), together with three-dimensional maps of the local interstellar medium, to identify stars belonging to the PLATO stellar samples in accordance with the mission requirements. The tPIC comprises 217,741 stars, including 202,315 FGK dwarfs and subgiants, 15,037 M dwarfs, and 789 known planet-host stars. The median distances of the samples are 512,pc for FGK stars and 133 ,pc for M dwarfs. We estimated interstellar reddening for almost all targets and developed an algorithm to infer fundamental stellar parameters (effective temperature, radius, and mass) homogeneously from astrometric and photometric observables. The tPIC fulfills all the science requirements of the PLATO mission. It also includes a list of stars that host known exoplanets, confirmed or still candidate, located within the LOPS2 field.

行星形成与盘演化7 篇

Measuring the gas mass of the protoplanetary disk around HD 179218 with CO isotopologues

Nina Weichmann, Ke Zhang, Leon Trapman, Ao Zhang · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
我们利用ALMA和NOEMA观测了HD 179218周围原行星盘的六条CO同位素谱线,通过热化学模型DALI拟合得到气体盘质量约8×10^-3 M☉和气尘比~270,超过星际标准值。该盘的CO雪线位于外边界之外,CO始终保持气态,提供了一个不受CO冻出污染的清晰基准案例,可用于研究原行星盘中的气体质量分布和气尘相互作用,同时气尘比的升高与毫米尘埃向内漂移相一致。
英文原摘要
Carbon monoxide (CO) isotopologues are widely used to estimate protoplanetary disk gas masses, but their reliability is often compromised by CO freeze-out and subsequent chemical processing. The disk around the luminous Herbig star HD~179218 ($L_* \sim 100\,L_\odot$, $M_* \sim 3.0\,M_\odot$) offers a rare test case of CO-based mass measurements. Its CO snowline lies beyond the outer disk edge, and thus CO remains in the gas phase throughout. We present ALMA and NOEMA observations of six CO isotopologue lines ($^{13}$CO, C$^{18}$O, and C$^{17}$O, $J$=2--1 and 1--0). Fitting the spectral energy distribution yields $M_{dust} \approx 3 \times 10^{-5}\, M_\odot$. Modeling CO isotopologue emission with the thermochemical code DALI yields the gas-disk characteristic radius ($R_{c} \approx 90$~au) and the gas mass ($M_{gas} \approx 8 \times 10^{-3}\, M_\odot$). These results yield a gas-to-dust ratio of $\sim$270 for our fiducial dust opacity ($a_{\rm max} = 1$~mm), above the canonical interstellar value of $\sim$100. The thermal structure of our best-fitting model confirms that CO never freezes out in this disk, so the elevated ratio is not an artifact of CO depletion; together with the compact millimeter dust disk relative to the gas disk, it is consistent with substantial inward radial drift of millimeter dust. In addition, we find a gas cavity inside 50\,au that encloses the compact millimeter dust disk---the inverse of a typical transition disk---which we attribute to photoevaporative clearing of the inner gas after the pebbles drifted inward. HD~179218 thus provides a clean benchmark for studying the gas mass distribution and gas--dust interaction in protoplanetary disks, free from the CO-depletion biases that affect colder disks.
protoplanetary disk

White dwarf pollution in wide binaries: the role of planetary mass

Constanza Norambuena, Carolina Charalambous, Claudia Aguilera-Gómez · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过N体模拟结合恒星演化模型,探讨了白矮星周围行星质量如何调控残留行星系统中的小行星盘演化及其向白矮星的吸积。研究发现低质量行星对小行星盘的扰动较弱,使小行星存活时间更长,因此污染可延续至冷白矮星阶段;而高质量行星则快速耗尽小行星盘,导致污染难以维持。行星质量决定了可供吸积的岩质物质的可用时间尺度,而双星的低倾角和低偏心率条件有利于污染发生。
英文原摘要
Atmospheric pollution in white dwarfs is interpreted as evidence for the accretion of rocky material from remnant planetary systems. In wide binaries, these reservoirs can be influenced by planetary perturbers. Here, we investigate how planetary mass affects the survival and delivery of planetesimals onto an evolving white dwarf. We perform N-body simulations incorporating time-dependent stellar evolution and a stellar companion, varying planetary masses and following the evolution of a planetesimal disc. We find that lower-mass planets perturb the planetesimal reservoir less efficiently, allowing more planetesimals to survive longer. Consequently, delivery onto the white dwarf can persist into the cool white dwarf phase, whereas high-mass planets deplete the reservoir earlier, preventing sustained pollution at late stages. Our results show that planetary mass regulates the timescale over which rocky material remains available for accretion, while the planet's location has minor impact. Pollution is more frequent in binaries with lower inclinations and eccentricities.
planetesimal

Planet-gap-induced ice lines form observable dust rings in protoplanetary disks

Kan Chen, Tommy Chi Ho Lau, Paola Pinilla · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究探讨了行星间隙诱导的CO₂冰线是否能在原行星盘中产生可观测的尘埃环。通过采用行星间隙的非光滑温度分布进行尘埃演化模拟，发现冰线处碎裂速度的跳跃导致尘埃在间隙边缘形成双尘埃环，在13 μm处呈现清晰的长期结构，而在毫米波段仅显示低对比度环，与ALMA观测结果一致。提出了多波长观测的诊断方法来区分冰线环与压力凸起陷阱的特征。
英文原摘要
Whether volatile ice lines can produce dust rings in protoplanetary disks remains debated. Previous modelling and observational studies place ice lines using smooth, monotonic disk temperature profiles. However, in substructured disks, the midplane temperature need not be smooth: a deep, planetary gap can locally heat, shift and multiply the ice-line locations. We adopt the non-smooth temperature profile of a hot planetary gap and use dust-evolution simulations to study how CO$_2$ ice lines, which imprint radial jumps in the fragmentation velocity $V_{\rm frag}$, affect dust evolution over 10 Myr and the observational signatures. For comparison, we also run models with uniform low ($V_{\rm frag}=1$ m/s) and high ($V_{\rm frag}=10$ m/s) fragmentation velocities. In the varying-$V_{\rm frag}$ model, dust piles up with enhanced grain sizes at the two CO$_2$ ice lines along the gap edges, appearing as clear double rings in dust observations at 13 $μ$m that persist over 10 Myr; at 1.3 mm, it shows only a modest ring-to-gap contrast of a factor of a few, consistent with the shallow, low-contrast rings in ALMA surveys. The uniform $V_{\rm frag}=10$ m/s model instead forms a single high-contrast ring at the pressure bump and suppresses the inward pebble flux to the inner disk by up to two orders of magnitude, whereas the uniform $V_{\rm frag}=1$ m/s model produces only weak substructure and strong outer-disk depletion. We propose a multi-wavelength test to distinguish these scenarios: ice-line rings stay at fixed radii from scattered light to the millimetre observation, whereas a pressure-bump trap produces a single dominant ring whose peak shifts inward at shorter wavelengths.
protoplanetary disk

The influence of dust composition on accretion outbursts

Nicolas Kaufmann, Anna B.T. Penzlin, Alfie Robinson, Alexandros Ziampras et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过轴对称尘气演化模型(TriPoD方法)调查了尘埃成分对原行星盘爆发式吸积的影响,包含死区热不稳定性、尘埃不透明度依赖加热冷却和挥发物蒸发凝聚过程。研究发现,当尘埃由硅酸盐和挥发物凝聚序列组成时,爆发会蒸发内0.5天文单位的大部分尘埃,而快速重新凝聚导致盘在爆发间重置;爆发周期和最大吸积率与尘埃升华温度存在直接相关性。
英文原摘要
Episodic accretion outbursts have been shown to occur in protoplanetary discs, where they are caused by thermal instability at the inner edge of the dead zone. These outbursts periodically heat up the dead zone, which significantly alters the composition, chemistry and accretion onto the star. We investigate how these accretion outbursts affect the inner disc composition and how different dust compositions and properties affect the outburst dynamics. We ran vertically integrated axis-symmetric dust and gas evolution models using the TriPoD method, including dust opacity-dependent heating and cooling, a dead-zone model, and compositional tracking of dust, including the evaporation and condensation of volatiles. When dust is considered to consist of a condensation sequence of silicates and volatiles, the outburst evaporates most of the dust within 0.5 au. The fast re-condensation and subsequent viscous evolution reset the disc between bursts. Additionally, we found that the burst cycle period and maximum accretion rate directly correlate with the dust sublimation temperature.
protoplanetary disc

The Hill sphere transits of β Pictoris c and the search for another 1981-like event

V. E. C. Bakker, M. A. Kenworthy, R. Stuik, K. Zwintz et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过2017-2023年期间来自BRITE卫星和地面观测台的多历元光度测量数据,搜索1981年β Pictoris系统中观测到的尘埃凌星事件的重复出现,并利用简单盘面模型检测β Pictoris c的Hill球过境中是否存在行星周围物质的信号。研究未在β Pictoris c的Hill球凌星期间发现与1981年事件相当的证据(排除该事件与β Pictoris c和b的Hill球相关的假说),但确定了Hill球内尘埃含量的上限约为10^22克,为行星周围环和卫星的形成理论提供了观测约束。
英文原摘要
Context. β Pictoris c is a young and nearby planetary system hosting an edge-on debris disk and at least three gas-giant planets. Their circumplanetary environments could host exomoons and rings, a detection of which would be highly informative for moon and planet formation theories. A photometric fluctuation of about 4% was seen towards β Pictoris in 1981, indicating the transit of dust in the system, which could be associated with the Hill spheres of the two inner planets. Aims. We searched for the origin of the 1981 event by searching for an analogous event in multi-epoch photometry from 2017 to 2023, and looked for signs of circumplanetary material in transits of the Hill sphere of β Pictoris c. Methods. Observations from the BRITE satellite and the bRing and ASTEP observatories were fitted to a model of the 1981 event to search for a similar event, and also searched for a signal consistent with a circumplanetary disk transit using a simple flat disk model. Results. No compelling evidence for a 1981 event during a Hill sphere primary transit is found, although we do find a candidate event around July 26, 2019. Due to the uncertainty in the time of closest projected separation of β Pictoris c, a search for a disk during the primary transit of 2018 could not be robustly determined, but bRing photometry for the second primary transit places an upper limit on the dust content of the Hill sphere of ∼10 22 grams of material. Conclusions. With no compelling detection of an event similar to that seen in 1981 during the transits of β Pictoris c in the photometric time series, we rule out the hypothesis that this event was related to the Hill spheres of β Pictoris c and β Pictoris b. Future observations of the next Hill sphere transit in 2028 can be realised with both ground-based observatories and with PLATO, whose first long-duration science pointing will include β Pictoris.
planet formation

The evolution of discs and the epoch of formation of giant planets around solar-type stars

Raffaele Gratton, Elisabetta Rigliaco · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过同时拟合33颗太阳型星的光谱能量分布和ALMA尘埃连续谱图像,约束了恒星参数和原行星盘的物理性质,并结合恒星演化模型推导了精确的恒星年龄。研究发现盘内尘埃质量和吸积率随时间演化,且在2-5 Myr年龄的超过一半恒星中观测到厘米级颗粒耗尽现象,这可能指示质量>30 M⊕的巨行星核心正在形成并阻止大颗粒向内漂移。这些观测证据表明在巨行星形成时期(约2 Myr左右),原行星盘的尘埃分布经历了显著变化。
英文原摘要
Disc evolution dictates the time for planet formation, providing the raw material to build the planets’ cores. Dynamical stellar masses derived from the kinematics of the gas in discs provide constraints on the ages of very young stars, offering a powerful approach to studying disc evolution and the epoch of giant-planet formation. We investigated a sample of 33 solar-type stars with bright protoplanetary discs and ages between 1 and 9 Myr. We modelled the spectral energy distributions and ALMA dust continuum images simultaneously to constrain the stellar radius and effective temperature, accretion rate, disc dust mass, the radii of the inner and outer disc regions (roughly separated at the ice line), and the maximum grain size in the inner disc. Combining the stellar masses and radii with stellar evolutionary models, we derived individual stellar ages. We found that the accretion rate and the dust mass of the inner disc evolve with time. The dust mass of the inner disc correlates with the observed accretion rates for stars younger than 2 Myr. A depletion of centimetre-sized grains in the inner disc is observed in more than half of the analysed stars with ages between 2 and 5 Myr, indicating the action of a mechanism that inhibits the inward drift of large grains, potentially associated with the progressive growth of giant-planet cores more massive than 30 M ⊕ , although the exact mass threshold remains uncertain. In addition, three discs exhibiting strong asymmetries in their ALMA continuum emission are associated with stars with ages of ∼2 Myr. The results provide observational indications of significant changes in the dust distribution during the epoch when giant planets may be forming.

Evolution of a hypothetical planetesimal disk in the HD 34445 planetary system

A. Terluk, R.S. Garcia, J.A. Correa-Otto, R. Gil-Hutton · Icarus
本研究通过N体数值模拟探讨了HD 34445系统中原行星盘对其已知行星构型的动力学响应与演化过程。研究表明,该系统中的微行星盘在现有行星轨道摄动下会经历显著的结构重组,包括轨道共振捕获、碰撞耗散和长期稳定性变化。这些结果为理解类似多行星系统中微行星盘的动力学演化提供了重要约束,有助于揭示行星-盘相互作用在系统形成和演化中的关键作用。

行星大气5 篇

Precision Requirements for Robust Small Planet Classification

Cayman T. Unterborn, Stephen R. Kane · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过不确定性传播分析,评估了利用"标称岩石行星区"(NRPZ)框架对小型系外行星进行岩石/挥发性/铁富集分类所需的质量和半径测量精度。在37颗候选岩石行星中,仅12颗已具备足够精度进行可靠分类,其余行星需降低约60%的联合测量误差,这需要数百至千次新的观测。研究表明单纯的质量-半径模型不足以对大多数行星进行分类,因此建议采用分阶段策略:优先改进测量精度以识别有歧义的系统,再配合JWST等大气观测作为补充验证。
英文原摘要
Classifying small exoplanets as rocky, volatile-rich, or iron-enriched is a prerequisite for efficient allocation of atmospheric characterization resources. The Nominally Rocky Planet Zone (NRPZ) provides a framework for this classification by comparing observed bulk densities against the expected density range for volatile-free rocky planets, calibrated from stellar abundance distributions. However, the efficacy of classifying a planet using the NRPZ is critically dependent on the precision of the underlying mass and radius measurements. Here, we perform an analytical propagation of observational uncertainties through the NRPZ classification framework for 37 candidate rocky exoplanets with radii below 2 Earth radii orbiting stars with visual magnitudes <11. Among the 37 planets, we find 12 already possess sufficiently precise mass and radius measurements to be confidently classified as Mini-Neptunes or Super-Mercuries. The remaining planets require approximately a ~60% reduction in the combined mass and radius uncertainties before they can be reliably classified. We find that 1-1,000 new observations are necessary to achieve these lower uncertainties, and these are likely lower limits. We argue that, while possible, this level of intense observation is likely not feasible for many of these planets and that mass-radius models alone are not likely to allow us to classify all but those planets with outlier densities. We therefore recommend a staged strategy in which improved mass and radius measurements are used to identify and prioritize ambiguous systems, followed (where feasible) by atmospheric observations with JWST or future facilities as a complementary test of whether selected planets retain substantial atmospheres.
atmosphere · atmospheric

Cometary impacts an unlikely pathway towards the origins of life on Earth

Richard J. Anslow, Catriona H. McDonald, Amy Bonsor, Paul B. Rimmer et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过结合彗星撞击模拟、彗星-大气相互作用和短周期彗星动力学演化模型,重新评估了早期彗星轰击对地球生命起源的贡献。研究发现,尽管彗星含有丰富的有机分子,但超高速撞击会导致这些脆弱有机物的大规模破坏,加之地球撞击轰击快速衰减,使得彗星成功递送原始生命化学所需浓度的有机物变成极其罕见事件。研究结果质疑了彗星撞击在地球生命起源中发挥重要作用这一长期假设。
英文原摘要
An early period of cometary bombardment has long been speculated to provide an important source of prebiotic feedstock molecules, required for the origins of life on Earth. In support of this interpretation, comets boast a rich diversity of volatile organic molecules, with many thought to be limiting for prebiotic chemistry. Modelling studies suggest, however, that a significant proportion of these molecules will be destroyed during hypervelocity impact. Here, we revisit the ability of comets to deliver prebiotic feedstock molecules in the high concentrations required for prebiotic chemistry, leveraging recent advances in cometary impact simulations, comet-atmosphere interactions, and dynamical modelling of short-period comets' origin and evolution. Taken together, the rapid decline in Earth's cometary impact bombardment and the efficient destruction of fragile cometary organics indicate that the successful delivery of prebiotic feedstock molecules was an exceedingly rare event. Our results therefore challenge the long-held assumption that cometary impacts played an important role in the origins of life on Earth.

The JWST Proto-PAH Project. Computational Modeling of the Emission Carriers

A. Ricca, G. C. Sloan, E. Peeters, J. Cami et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过第一性原理分子动力学模拟和密度泛函理论计算，生成了碳氢分子微粒的真实结构，并与JWST对D类碳星的观测光谱进行比较，以探寻这些天体红外谱线的发射载体。研究发现最佳匹配的分子结构含有缺陷芳香烃和氢化芳香烃域，通过脂肪链桥连接，而非传统的孤立多环芳烃模型。该工作揭示了这类分子微粒在紫外光驱动下的破碎过程可能导致多环芳烃和富瓦烯的形成，为理解碳星向行星星云演化过程中的化学变化提供了新的认识。
英文原摘要
Abstract The infrared spectra of many carbon-rich postasymptotic giant branch stars are dominated by emission features from aromatic and aliphatic hydrocarbons, but the chemical structure of the carriers remains unidentified. Class D sources show unusual emission profiles with strong aliphatic emission, providing a stringent test of carrier candidates. Here we investigate whether hydrogenated carbonaceous molecular particles can be the carriers of these features. Rather than assuming candidate geometries we generate the initial structures using ab initio molecular dynamics simulations and optimize them using density functional theory. We then compare the computed emission spectra to JWST NIRSpec and MIRI/MRS observations of Class D sources. The structures providing the best agreements contain sizeable domains with defect-bearing aromatics and hydrogenated aromatics connected by aliphatic bridges, in contrast to the “arophatic” cluster model of isolated two- to three-ring aromatics connected by aliphatic and olefinic bridges. As a representative example, we discuss in detail the molecular particle C 130 H 96 (radius of 5.5 Å) and compare its computed spectrum to the JWST spectra of IRAS 05110-6616, a Class D2 source with apparent shifts of the aromatic C–H out-of-plane bands. The calculated spectra are in qualitative agreement with the observed 3 μ m profile, the aliphatic C–H bending bands, the broad 8 μ m feature, the 11–14 μ m C–H out-of-plane region, and the bands at 16 and 21 μ m. We hypothesize that UV photodriven fragmentation of such molecular particles releases aromatics linked by aliphatics, followed by the formation of polycyclic aromatic hydrocarbons and fullerenes as these objects evolve into planetary nebulae.

Abstract Spectroscopic variability is detected in brown dwarfs across all spectral types, suggesting atmospheric patchiness in brown dwarf atmospheres. We present JWST/NIRSpec time series observations of the T8.0 planetary-mass companion Ross 458C. We compared our derived spectral variability and light curve to those obtained using Hubble Space Telescope (HST)/Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) observations more than 7.5 yr before and published by E. Manjavacas et al. We found that the light curve of Ross 458C is remarkably similar in the two epochs in terms of shape and variability amplitude potentially created by stable weather patterns. We measured the variability amplitude in the white light curve created using only the wavelength range covered by HST/WFC3 (1.10–1.63 μ m), obtaining a value of 1.47% ± 0.18%, which is consistent with that measured in the HST/WFC3 light curve.

Sushmita Deb, Kaushal Sharma, Ravi Kumar Kopparapu, Biman Jyoti Medhi · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society

Abstract Investigating the bulk interior and atmosphere of exoplanets has become a crucial component in understanding the birth and formation history of planetary systems. The presence of different chemical species in the core and atmosphere of the planets hint at different birth location of exoplanets in the same planetary system. In this work, we investigate the bulk structure of two planetary systems : TOI-270 & TOI-776 and explore their atmospheric properties. We provide a detailed spectroscopic and photometric estimates of the fundamental properties of the two host stars using optical spectra from HARPS, ESPRESSO and photometric data from Gaia, Pan-STARRS1, 2MASS and WISE. We employ machine learning models to estimate the radius and mass fractions of the different interior layers of the exoplanets. Abstract A comprehensive photometric and spectroscopic analysis of TIC 349005337, a highly active M-type detached eclipsing binary with an orbital period of 2.6079133 days, is presented utilizing high-cadence and long-term monitoring photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) and medium-resolution spectroscopy from the Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST). By integrating radial velocity curves with light curves, we determined the fundamental physical parameters for both components. The system consists of an M-dwarf primary ( M 1 = 0.517 ± 0.009 M ⊙ , R 1 = 0.518 ± 0.003 R ⊙ ) and a fully convective secondary ( M 2 = 0.298 ± 0.004 M ⊙ , R 2 = 0.357 ± 0.003 R ⊙ ). A total of 12 high-energy stellar flares, with the most energetic event reaching 3.87 × 10 34 erg, is identified, confirming the system is in a state of magnetic hyperactivity. The frequent superflares suggest that intense magnetic activity and significant starspot coverage play a dominant role in altering the stellar atmospheric structure. As the secondary component falls below the fully convective boundary, these energetic flares provide critical empirical constraints for distributed dynamo models in the low-mass regime. Our results highlight TIC 349005337 as an ideal laboratory for investigating the interplay between binary dynamics, magnetic reconnection, and the resulting impact on the atmospheric stability of potential orbiting exoplanets.

Accretion Rates and Thermohaline Convection in Polluted White Dwarfs

J. R. Fuentes, Matias Castro-Tapia, Jim Fuller, Marcus N. King · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究通过建立热盐析对流的时间演化模型，自洽耦合吸积物质运输与表面丰度变化，揭示了污染白矮星大气中重元素混合的物理过程。基于流体力学模拟和解析求解，我们得出混合层深度和表面丰度随时间的幂律演化关系，以及与吸积率的标度关系，为从观测丰度推断吸积率提供了实用处方。应用于G 29-38的结果表明，我们的模型得出的吸积率与X射线约束的差异可能源于吸积边界层的X射线光度被部分抑制。
英文原摘要
Abstract The atmospheres of polluted white dwarfs contain information about the composition of disrupted planetary material. However, interpreting the observed abundances requires a quantitative description of the mixing processes that transport accreted material through their envelopes. In particular, the accretion of metal-rich debris drives thermohaline convection in the star’s radiative zone. Previous studies have modeled this form of turbulence as an enhanced diffusive process, often inferring mass accretion rates M ̇ acc that exceed X-ray constraints. Here we develop a time-dependent model that treats thermohaline mixing as a propagating front whose evolution is coupled self-consistently to the surface abundance of heavy elements. We perform hydrodynamical simulations which demonstrate that thermohaline mixing drives the composition gradient toward values slightly above marginal thermohaline stability. Using this condition, we derive analytic solutions for the mixed layer depth h and surface abundance X surf , finding that both scale as t 1/2 at early times, transitioning to h ∝ t 4/27 and X surf ∝ t 10/27 at later times. Without gravitational settling, the thermohaline front never reaches a steady state, while including settling leads to an equilibrium surface abundance X surf , eq ∝ M ̇ acc 30 / 43 . Applied to G 29–38, our model matches the observed heavy-element abundance for M ̇ acc ∼ 1 × 1 0 10 –2 × 10 10 g s −1 , comparable to previous findings using stellar evolution codes. These rates exceed the X-ray estimate by about an order of magnitude, which may point to a partially suppressed X-ray luminosity from the accretion boundary layer. We also present a practical prescription for inferring accretion rates directly from the observed surface abundance and stellar parameters for other polluted white dwarfs.

Abstract We present a comprehensive spectroscopic census of the Orion Nebula Cluster (ONC), based primarily on low-resolution optical spectroscopy obtained with MMT/Hectospec. We derive spectral types, youth indicators, and accretion diagnostics for 1513 stars located in the ONC region, enabling the identification and characterization of pre–main-sequence stars. Mapping Stellar Obliquity Across Space and Time

Lauren I. Biddle, Brendan P. Bowler · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过比较114颗类太阳星的恒星倾角与行星轨道倾角,跨越原行星盘、碎片盘、温木星和热木星四个阶段和轨道距离,系统地探测恒星自转角动量与行星轨道角动量间的夹角(恒星倾斜度)的空间和时间演化特征。结果表明,所有样本的最小错位率(20-33%)在统计上一致,且不随轨道距离或系统年龄显著变化,暗示类太阳星的恒星倾斜度主要在形成时期确定,其后续演化有限。这些发现与形成后倾角演化罕见、仅限于小错位或局限于特定参数空间(如有近伴星、高温主星或长周期巨行星的系统)相符。
英文原摘要
The angle between a star's rotational angular momentum vector and the orbital angular momentum vector of its planets---stellar obliquity---encodes the initial conditions and dynamical history of planetary systems. Variations in obliquity with orbital separation may arise from either primordial disk structure (e.g., broken or warped protoplanetary disks with misaligned inner and outer regions) or subsequent dynamical interactions with planets or external perturbers. Comparing obliquities across orbital distance therefore probes the spatial imprint of these processes, while comparisons across age constrain their temporal evolution. To test for signatures of obliquity evolution in both space and time, we compare stellar inclinations with orbital inclinations for 114 single Sun-like stars spanning four samples that probe distinct regimes of orbital separation and age: 49 protoplanetary disk systems, 24 debris disk systems, 21 warm Jupiter systems, and 20 hot Jupiter systems. We find minimum misalignment rates of $33^{+7}_{-6}$% for protoplanetary disk systems, $21^{+9}_{-7}$% for debris disk systems, $23^{+10}_{-8}$% for warm Jupiter systems, and $20^{+9}_{-8}$% for hot Jupiter systems. These rates are consistent within 1$σ$ across all samples. The absence of significant variation in minimum-obliquity distributions with either orbital separation or system age suggests that stellar obliquities for Sun-like stars may be largely established at formation with limited subsequent evolution in both space and time. These results are consistent with post-formation inclination evolution being either infrequent, confined to small misalignments, or restricted to regions of parameter space not examined here---such as systems with close stellar companions, hot host stars, small planets, and long-period giant planets.

The trans-Neptunian object 2026 ED$_{138}$ was announced in 2026 October as the first dwarf-planet candidate in our solar system discovered by the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. The Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) observed it in five month-long sectors between 2019 and 2026, four of them before its discovery. We subtract every Gaia DR3 star from these crowded full-frame images with the TESS-Gaia Light Curve (TGLC) package, then remove static residuals with a running median. Stacking the images along its predicted path detects 2026 ED$_{138}$ in all five sectors at 5-21$σ$, and at 34$σ$ combined. Jorge Llop-Sayson, Andre Fogal, James Mang, William Thompson et al. · The Astronomical Journal

英文原摘要
Abstract ϵ Eridani b, the nearest known Jupiter analog, has its orbit and mass constrained from radial velocity (RV) and absolute astrometry, and its atmosphere from JWST/NIRCam imaging upper limits in A. Sanghi et al. Here we follow up that work with a self-consistent model of ϵ Eridani b that treats all available data within a single Bayesian framework. We extend the RV data with new measurements, update the treatment of the astrometry data with a new model, and include the NIRCam observations with a grid of evolutionary and atmospheric models. We find a mass of 0.91 ± 0.04 M Jup and an orbit consistent with previous work. The imaging data helps constrain planet effective temperature, atmospheric metallicity, and surface gravity. The constraints are dependent on model assumptions: for an atmosphere in chemical equilibrium, an otherwise low statistically significant feature in one of the epochs is recovered as the planet at high confidence. When assuming chemical disequilibrium, the posteriors exhibit a bimodal distribution, with one mode consistent with zero flux and the other coinciding with the flux of the tentative feature. Consistent with previous work, the clear atmosphere models are found to be viable at very enhanced metallicity, whereas the cloudy models yield more moderate values. Applying these models to the Roman Coronagraph, we find that the predicted reflected-light fluxes place every cloudy atmosphere our fit allows within the instrument’s expected sensitivity. A nondetection would be very constraining: a final contrast sensitivity of 2 × 10 −9 would rule out all cloudy atmospheres under our model assumptions.

英文原摘要
Abstract We present a Hubble Space Telescope (HST) Wide Field Camera 3 (WFC3) G141 phase-curve observation of the ultrashort-period rocky planet TOI-2431 b and detect its secondary eclipse in the near-infrared. Combined with the TESS optical eclipse, these measurements constrain the planet’s dayside brightness temperature, reflected light contribution, albedo, and heat redistribution. We detect the eclipse in the optical and near-infrared wavelengths of TESS and HST/WFC3 G141, with a planet-to-star flux ratio of 37 ± 10 ppm and 6 9 − 13 + 14 ppm , respectively. Assuming negligible reflected light, the joint TESS+HST fit yields a brightness temperature T b , joint = 252 4 − 84 + 77 K and a brightness temperature ratio of R joint = T b , joint / T p , max = 0.98 0 − 0.034 + 0.032 consistent with the zero-albedo, no-redistribution limit T p , max = 257 4 − 35 + 36 K . The HST phase curve is consistent with zero nightside emission and no measurable hotspot offset, consistent with inefficient day-night heat distribution. Under this thermal-dominated interpretation, TOI-2431 b lies at the zero-albedo, no-redistribution limit and contrasts with previously observed lava worlds. At optical and near-infrared wavelengths, however, thermal emission is degenerate with reflected light. For a geometric albedo of A g = 0.1, reflected light would contribute approximately 11 ppm in each bandpass, reducing the temperature to T b , joint ( A g = 0.1 ) = 240 7 − 102 + 91 K . Longer-wavelength observations are required to isolate the thermal component. Planned JWST/MIRI LRS observations are expected to substantially reduce this degeneracy and provide more direct constraints on heat redistribution and possible silicate-atmosphere spectral features. This marks the first secondary eclipse detection of a rocky exoplanet using HST and establishes TOI-2431 b as a benchmark target for studying properties of lava worlds.

Variable X-Ray Flux May Explain Variable Helium Escape from the Habitable-zone Exoplanet LHS 1140b

Collin Cherubim, Tim Cunningham, Jason A. Dittmann · The Astrophysical Journal Letters
本研究通过XMM-Newton空间望远镜观测宜居带类地行星LHS 1140b周围的X射线和极紫外辐射变化,探究其大气逃逸中氦吸收信号的时间变异性。观测发现X射线通量在18小时内变化幅度为8.5倍,而紫外和可见光通量稳定,长期XUV通量在年际尺度上保持稳定。研究结论认为短期XUV变率和耀斑事件比长期XUV变异更能解释观测到的氦逃逸变异,需要同步进行凌星近红外观测和恒星活动指标监测来确认两者的因果关系。
英文原摘要
Abstract A central goal in exoplanet science is to understand the conditions under which potentially habitable worlds retain atmospheres. This question is of special interest for worlds orbiting M stars, whose habitable-zone planets are more readily observable, but which also emit prolonged high-energy radiation that can erode planetary atmospheres. For one such world, LHS 1140b, a rocky planet orbiting in the habitable zone of an inactive star, time-variable helium absorption has been observed as the planet transited in front of its host star, consistent with atmospheric escape. We present X-ray/ultraviolet flux observations with the XMM-Newton space telescope, which serve to test whether the observed helium absorption variability can be explained by stellar X-ray and extreme-ultraviolet (XUV) flux variability. We detect X-ray flux variability with an amplitude of F max / F min = 8 . 5 − 4.4 + 9.4 within the 18 hr observing window. We do not detect variability in the UV or visible flux. The integrated X-ray flux is fully consistent with the X-ray flux measured in 2018, suggesting stable XUV flux over yearslong timescales. We find that the 2018 observation was not sensitive to the level of variability observed in 2026. We explore several models that fit both datasets and conclude that short-term XUV variability and/or small flares are more likely to explain the helium variability than long-term XUV variability. Simultaneous observations of the planet transit in the near-infrared and stellar activity indicators like XUV flux are critical to confirm the link between the observed helium signal variability and the observed X-ray variability.

lenscape: Fast, High-resolution $Δχ^2$ Maps of Microlensing Events

Michael Hippke · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对微引力透镜事件中行星参数简并问题,开发了lenscape软件包用于快速构建Δχ²映射。通过多级邻域传播和自适应轮廓细化策略,该方法显著加速了二元透镜分离-质量比参数空间的搜索,并有效抑制局部极值伪影。在MOA-2022-BLG-091事件中的应用表明,该方法可在台式计算机上于两小时内生成400×400高分辨率映射,为行星微引力透镜事件的参数约束提供了高效工具。
英文原摘要
Planetary microlensing analyses rely on $Δχ^2$ maps across the binary-lens separation $s$ and mass ratio $q$ to locate degenerate solutions and bound parameter uncertainties. Because every grid point requires a full light-curve optimization with finite-source magnifications, constructing these maps is often the primary computational bottleneck in event modeling. Here I present lenscape, an open-source Python package that accelerates $Δχ^2$ mapping and suppresses local-minimum artifacts through multi-stage neighbor propagation and adaptive contour refinement. Applied to the planetary event MOA-2022-BLG-091, lenscape reproduces the published $50\times50$ map in 14 minutes and generates a $400\times400$ map in under two hours on a desktop computer.
microlensing

2026 ED138: A Large Trans-Neptunian Object in Early Rubin Observatory Data, and How Many More May Remain

Matthew J. Holman, Mario Jurić, Kevin J. Napier, Jacob A. Kurlander et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用Rubin天文台数据发现并表征了大型TNO天体2026 ED138，其绝对星等为3.19（直径612-1530 km），是已知最亮的十大TNO之一，且为矮行星候选体，其轨道位于海王星12:7平均运动共振的边缘。通过对已知亮TNO的回收统计，我们估计银河面外还有约3个及更多类似天体未被发现，而银河面内10°范围内可能存在更多未被现有巡天覆盖的大型TNO。
英文原摘要
We characterize a large trans-Neptunian object (TNO), 2026 ED138, discovered in data taken by the NSF-DOE Vera C. Rubin Observatory with the linking code pointdexter, and validate it independently. 2026 ED138 has absolute magnitude $H_V=3.19$, implying a diameter of 612-1530 km for geometric albedos between 0.25 and 0.04 and making it one of the ten intrinsically brightest known TNOs and a dwarf-planet candidate. At $i\approx19.4$ it is bright enough in the sky for ground-based spectroscopy and light curves and for detailed study with HST and JWST. Archival DECam detections extend its arc to more than ten years (3789 days). Its orbit, with barycentric $a=43.09$ au, $e=0.139$ and $i=24.4^\circ$, lies at the edge of the 12:7 mean-motion resonance with Neptune, like that of the dwarf planet Haumea, but with $g-r=0.63$ it is too red to be a member of Haumea's collisional family. 2026 ED138 was missed by earlier surveys because it lies far south and near the crowded regions of the Galactic plane. Our recoveries of the known TNOs with $H_V<4$ suggest that about 3 more objects like them, and probably more, remain undiscovered away from the Galactic plane. More such objects probably lie within $10^\circ$ of the plane, which no search has yet covered to this depth.

PyMsOfa 2.0: A Pure-Python Implementation of the IAU Standards of Fundamental Astronomy Service

Dongjie Tan, Jianghui Ji, Jiacheng Liu · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
PyMsOfa 2.0 使用NumPy将国际天文学联合会(IAU)基础天文学标准(SOFA)库的全部247个例程重新实现为纯Python代码，无需编译外部库即可在多平台运行，并支持批量处理NumPy数组以便用于巡天管道和蒙特卡洛模拟。该实现保持与官方SOFA C版本的算法一致性，经过逐例程验证后达到机器精度或文档规定的精度要求，已应用于盖亚星表天体测量归约和CHES任务的天体测量研究。该软件包还包含改进的章动模型IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$等扩展模块，通过PyPI、GitHub和Zenodo发布。
英文原摘要
Astrometric calculations require authoritative algorithms so that every result is correct and reproducible. The Standards of Fundamental Astronomy (SOFA) service of the International Astronomical Union (IAU) provides such algorithms, officially released only in Fortran 77 and ANSI C. In an earlier paper we presented the first version of PyMsOfa, a Python package wrapping the SOFA C library. Here we present PyMsOfa 2.0, which re-implements the complete SOFA library in pure Python with NumPy. All 247 official SOFA routines are ported directly to Python, requiring no compilation of any external C library and running identically on Linux, Windows, and macOS. The routines operate on NumPy arrays, so arguments can be processed in batch, which is convenient for survey pipelines and Monte Carlo simulations. The package also provides an extension module with validated models not yet part of the official SOFA release, such as the improved precession--nutation model IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$. The reimplementation reproduces the official SOFA algorithms: all constants, series, and coefficients are taken unchanged from the SOFA source files, and routine names differ only by the pym prefix. We verify every routine against the official SOFA C release: most reproduce the C results to machine precision, the remainder within the accuracy tolerance stated in the SOFA documentation. The package has been applied to Gaia astrometric reduction and used in astrometric studies of the Closeby Habitable Exoplanet Survey (CHES) mission, and is available through PyPI, GitHub, and Zenodo.
astrometric

Multiple Regular Frequency Spacings in δ Scuti Stars from Automated Analysis of TESS Oscillation Spectra

Shravan M. Hanasoge, Shashwat Chamoli, Subrata Kumar Panda, Daniel R. Reese · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究针对快速自转δ Scuti星的振荡频谱难以解释的问题,开发了一套自动化统计框架来识别近似等间距的模式序列,并应用于TESS观测的4681颗δ Scuti星的振荡谱。分析结果表明许多星体内存在多个不同的等间距频率序列,这一现象超出了现有理论对快速自转声学模式的预测,暗示可能存在未知的模式族或其他物理机制控制这些星体的振荡谱结构。该方法为大规模恒星巡测中的规则模式序列研究提供了可扩展的工具和新的观测约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract The oscillation spectra of rapidly rotating δ Scuti stars are difficult to interpret because rotation modifies both the frequencies and spatial structure of the modes. Although theoretical studies predict that acoustic modes should organize into approximately equally spaced frequency sequences, systematic searches for such patterns have been limited by the absence of scalable, statistically robust detection methods. We present an automated statistical framework for identifying approximately equally spaced mode sequences in stellar oscillation spectra while quantifying the significance of each candidate detection. We apply this method to oscillation spectra of 4681 δ Scuti stars observed by the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). The analysis identifies regular mode sequences in a large number of stars and, in many cases, reveals multiple independent regular frequency spacings within the same oscillation spectrum. While the existence of regular sequences is broadly consistent with theoretical expectations for rapidly rotating acoustic modes, the occurrence of multiple dissimilar spacings is not readily explained by current models. Differences in acoustic travel times between known island-mode families appear insufficient to account for the observed diversity of spacings, suggesting that additional mode families or other physical effects contribute to the organization of oscillation spectra of δ Scuti stars. Our results provide a scalable framework for studying regular mode sequences in large stellar surveys and new observational constraints on oscillations in rapidly rotating stars.

Discovery and Characterization of the TOI-4468 Planetary System: A Transiting Hot Jupiter with a Lone Nearby Outer Companion

Joseph R. Livesey, Benjamin J. Hord, Juliette Becker, Andrew Vanderburg et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究通过联合NEID径向速度测量和TESS及地面观测的凌星光度测量，发现并表征了TOI-4468系统中的热木星TOI-4468 b和近邻亚海王星TOI-4468 c，该系统独特之处在于热木星外侧伴星的孤立配置而无内侧伴星。观测结果显示该系统未经历过激发相互倾角的早期世俗共振，这对理解热木星与外伴星的几何构型及热木星形成机制提供了重要约束。研究还识别出可能存在的624天周期外侧巨行星信号，为该系统的动力学演化和形成历史提供了新的研究线索。
英文原摘要
Abstract We report the discovery of two planets, a hot Jupiter (HJ) and a nearby outer sub-Neptune, orbiting the star TOI-4468. This system is unique among the current exoplanet census in that it features a close outer companion to a HJ without an accompanying inner companion. By jointly fitting radial velocity (RV) measurements taken with the NEID spectrograph and transit photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) and several ground-based observatories, we constrain the orbital periods, masses, and radii of these two planets. We confirm the planetary nature of the HJ TOI-4468 b ( R = 1.01 R J , m = 0.54 M J , P = 2.77 days). We also validate the outer planet TOI-4468 c ( R = 0.28 R J , P = 7.01 days) statistically, incorporating constraints from ground-based observations. We also identify, but cannot confirm, an additional RV signal that may be due to an outer giant in this system with an orbital period of 624 days. From the observed geometry of this system, we argue that it must never have encountered an early secular resonance that is thought to excite the mutual inclination of other HJ/outer companion systems. We discuss the possibility of an undetected inner companion, as well as potential implications for HJ formation.

Asteroseismic properties of young pulsators in the Perseus molecular cloud. Discovery of period-spacing patterns in HD 281229

Saakshi Wadhwa, Dario J. Fritzewski, Gang Li, Simon J. Murphy · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用TESS测光数据在英仙座分子云中识别和表征年轻脉动星,特别是中等质量的前主序星,以探测其内部结构和早期演化阶段的旋转特性。通过Lomb-Scargle周期图分析和迭代预白化方法,我们发现8个压力模式脉动星和2个重力模式候选体,其中HD 281229是迄今发现最年轻的具有可检测周期间距图样的恒星。通过同时拟合偶极和四极周期间距图样,我们获得了该星的渐近周期间距和近核旋转频率,证明了重力模式星震学在探测极早期演化阶段恒星内部结构和旋转性质方面的有效性。
英文原摘要
Very young pulsating stars, including pre-main sequence pulsators, provide a rare opportunity to probe stellar interiors and investigate rotation during the earliest phases of stellar evolution. However, they remain relatively unexplored, particularly in the intermediate-mass regime. We aim to identify and characterise young pulsating stars in the Perseus molecular cloud using photometric data from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). We compiled -based membership catalogues for the Perseus molecular cloud, applied selection criteria to isolate young intermediate-mass stars, and extracted light curves from TESS full-frame images. Variability classification was performed using Lomb--Scargle periodograms and iterative pre-whitening. For confirmed pressure-mode pulsators, we estimated the frequency of maximum pulsation power, ν_ Gaia max . For gravity-mode candidates, we searched for period-spacing patterns and fitted them using the traditional approximation of rotation. From the 148 membership-selected targets, we identified eight pressure-mode pulsators and two objects showing possible gravity-mode pulsations. We also analysed the previously known δ Sct star V705 Per, bringing the total sample to 149 targets. One of the gravity-mode pulsators, HD 281229, is the youngest star known to date with a detected and modelled period-spacing pattern. By simultaneously fitting the dipole and quadrupole period-spacing patterns, we estimate an asymptotic period spacing of (Π_0=4776 ) and a near-core rotation frequency of (f_ =1.317 ^ s rot d -1 ). These results highlight the potential of asteroseismology to probe the internal structure and rotation of very young stars. The detected period-spacing pattern in HD 281229 shows that gravity-mode asteroseismology can provide meaningful constraints on the properties of stars at early evolutionary stages.

The colors of protoplanetary disks. Impact of nonspherical dust particles on the polarized and total intensity colors

M. Roumesy, J. Ma, R. Tazaki, F. Ménard et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过三维辐射传输模型生成原行星盘的多波长散射光合成图像，系统研究了非球形尘埃颗粒的大小、形态和成分对盘色（偏振和总强度）的影响。研究发现偏振色对尘埃性质的敏感性更高，聚集体的单体尺寸而非聚集体尺寸是决定颜色的关键因素，且有效介质理论仅对低孔隙度聚集体适用。将色度测量与散射相函数结合可有效打破尘埃模型的简并性，对HD 100546的应用表明其红外色排除了致密颗粒和富有机物成分，而与含大单体的无定形碳聚集体相符。
英文原摘要
Dust properties in protoplanetary disks remain poorly understood despite their central role in planetary formation. A detailed observational characterization of their properties is crucial to improving our understanding of grain growth. Recent studies, based on analyses of scattered-light images and scattering phase functions, provide promising insights into dust characterization. However, the phase function alone is insufficient to constrain dust properties efficiently. In particular, degeneracies between dust size, composition, and morphology remain a major obstacle to linking observations to realistic dust models. We investigate the behavior of the disk color as a function of dust size, shape, and composition. The goal is to combine this with phase function measurements to most effectively constrain the dust properties. We used the 3D radiative transfer code MCFOST to generate synthetic scattered-light images of protoplanetary disks from the visible to the near-infrared, in both total and polarized intensity. We considered compact spherical grains using Mie theory. We used the optical properties computed with the T-matrix method or the discrete dipole approximation for irregular particles and for compact and fractal aggregates from the AggScatVIR database. We measured the disk colors directly from the synthetic images. We obtain five main results. (i) For aggregates larger than the wavelength, the monomer size plays a crucial role in determining the colors, whereas the aggregate size has a negligible effect, especially for low fractal dimensions. (ii) The polarized color is more sensitive to the dust properties than the total intensity color, likely because it combines the wavelength dependences of both the scattered intensity and the degree of polarization. (iii) The effective medium theory is a reliable approximation only for relatively compact aggregates composed of small monomers and becomes inaccurate for highly porous aggregates or those composed of larger monomers. (iv) The effective albedo is useful for predicting disk colors, but radiative transfer calculations are still required to account for the multiple scattering effect and quantitatively interpret disk colors. (v) For HD 100546, the red optical to near-infrared colors exclude compact grains and organic-rich compositions but are compatible with fractal or compact aggregates of amorphous carbon composition containing large monomers (200--400 nm). Our study of color based on synthetic data and its application to the example of the disk around HD 100546 shows that combining color measurements, or a color measurement with a polarized scattering phase function, offers a promising approach to breaking the degeneracy between dust models. Extending this approach to larger samples of disks with multiwavelength observations should provide better constraints on the properties and evolution of dust and motivate the use of color in future studies of protoplanetary disks.
protoplanetary disk

Diversity of planetary origins and compositions traced by stellar abundance differences among co-natal star systems

Bertram Bitsch, Fan Liu, Haiyang S. Wang · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过分析共生恒星对中的恒星丰度差异，推断曾被恒星吸积的行星的组成成分。研究发现，难熔元素（Fe、Mg、Si）和挥发性元素（O、C、S）丰度差异的不同组合模式反映了行星在不同位置（相对于水和碳晶粒蒸发线）的形成历史，以及行星在FeS蒸发线处迁移的证据。这些结果表明行星的不同组成与其形成位置直接相关，为理解系外行星组成和行星移动过程提供了新约束。
英文原摘要
So called co-natal stars are wide binaries that form from the same molecular cloud and are expected to share the same stellar composition. However, observations indicate stellar abundance differences in a small fraction of these co-natal systems. These stellar abundance differences have been attributed to the ingestion of planets by their host stars. In this study, we analyze a previous sample of co-natal stars to determine the composition of the planets that had been ingested by their host stars. The abundance differences in these co-natal pairs come in different flavours, where systems can show a positive abundance difference of the refractory elements (Fe, Mg, Si) paired with a positive, zero, or negative abundance difference of volatiles (O, C, S). Positive trends of refractory and volatile abundance differences can be explained (at minimum) by the ingestion of a single planet by one of the stars. However, positive trends of refractory abundance differences paired with zero or negative abundance differences of oxygen and carbon imply that both stars are required to ingest planets formed at different locations (i.e. interior or exterior to the water and/or carbon grain evaporation front). Furthermore, some systems show a sulfur abundance difference that is a factor of a few lower than the abundance difference of Fe, Si, and Mg, even though sulfur is mostly accreted via the moderately volatile compound FeS. We show that this can be explained by the migration of one of the planets across the FeS evaporation front, where the planet first accretes large amounts of sulfur with the solids and then no sulfur, while still accreting the other refractory elements before being ingested by its host star. Our study shows that the planetary compositions derived from the detailed stellar abundances are sufficient to explain the measured abundance differences of co-natal stars, giving us a handle on the composition of exoplanets. At the same time it implies very different planetary compositions caused by the different formation location of these planets, in agreement with planet formation models dedicated to the formation of inner super-Earths and sub-Neptunes.

Coupled evolution of Earth's H2-rich proto-atmosphere and oxidized magma ocean: Efficient H2O production through dissolved H2 transport and atmospheric buffering of the magma ocean redox state

Tatsuya Yoshida, Kirara Arima, Naoki Terada, Takeshi Kuroda et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究建立了耦合大气-地幔演化模型，包含原始地球岩浆洋与H2富集原始大气之间的扩散输运、熔体内氧化还原反应及H2O生成过程，模拟了行星冷却过程中两个圈层的化学耦合。结果表明，岩浆洋表面冷却驱动的密集对流使边界层快速更新，促进了大气H2向岩浆洋的输运和H2O高效生成，使两个圈层能在约10^4年内化学平衡。这一耦合演化过程通过大气与岩浆洋的氧化还原交换，既可缓冲固结过程中的极端氧化，同时有助于维持还原性H2富集大气，对早期地球水库的形成具有重要贡献。
英文原摘要
Giant impacts during Earth's final accretion likely generated global magma oceans, where the disproportionation of Fe^2+ at lower-mantle pressures and segregation of the resulting metallic iron into the core could have oxidized the magma ocean. Earth may nevertheless have had an H2-rich proto-atmosphere in chemical disequilibrium with the magma ocean, but the chemical coupling between this atmosphere and an oxidized magma ocean remains poorly constrained. Here we develop a coupled atmosphere-interior evolution model that includes diffusive transport in the boundary layer between the magma ocean and atmosphere, redox reactions in the melt, partitioning of oxide components and H2O among coexisting phases, and planetary cooling through a proto-atmosphere, with metallic iron produced by Fe^2+ disproportionation removed into the core as it forms. Atmospheric H2 transport into the magma ocean and subsequent H2O production become more efficient with increasingly frequent boundary layer renewal driven by buoyant overturn of dense melt cooled at the surface, allowing the atmosphere and magma ocean to chemically equilibrate within ~10^4 yr when the magma ocean is sufficiently deep and hot to extend into the lower mantle. Redox exchange with the atmosphere buffers the solidification-driven redox evolution of the magma ocean, preventing extreme oxidation or reduction of the coupled system. These results suggest that the coupled evolution could have contributed to early Earth's water inventory while maintaining a reducing H2-rich atmosphere.

The 3D Cosmic Shoreline for Nurturing Planetary Atmospheres

Zach K. Berta-Thompson, Patcharapol Wachiraphan, Catriona A. Murray · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究建立了一个统计框架，在逃逸速度、行星接收的恒星辐射通量和恒星光度构成的三维参数空间中，推导行星大气存在的宇宙分界线位置、形态和不确定性。通过将恒星光度显式纳入模型以避免难以测量的历史X射线和极端紫外辐射通量，研究发现临界通量阈值与逃逸速度的幂律关系指数为p=5.90±0.52（陡于经典值4），与恒星光度的关系指数为q=1.17±0.24，这意味着在光度低于log₁₀(L⋆/L⊙)=-2.23的M矮星（约M4V型）外，地球大小行星即使位于宜居带也难以保留大气。该模型为预测任意行星保有大气的概率提供了定量工具，可被近期JWST岩石行星观测严格检验。
英文原摘要
Abstract Various “cosmic shorelines” have been proposed to delineate which planets have atmospheres. The fates of individual planetary atmospheres may be set by a complex sea of growth and loss processes, driven by unmeasurable environmental factors or unknown historical events. Yet, defining population-level boundaries helps illuminate which processes matter and identify high-priority targets for future atmospheric searches. Here, we provide a statistical framework for inferring the position, shape, and fuzziness of an instellation-based cosmic shoreline, defined in the 3D space of planet escape velocity, planet bolometric flux received, and host-star luminosity. We circumvent the need to estimate each individual host star’s historical X-ray and extreme ultraviolet fluences by including luminosity in the definition of the shoreline, explicitly modeling how sharply such drivers of atmospheric escape intensify toward lower-luminosity M dwarf stars and marginalizing over the associated uncertainties. Using solar system and exoplanet atmospheric constraints, under the assumption that one planar boundary applies across a wide parameter space, we find the critical flux threshold for atmospheres scales with escape velocity with a power-law index of p = 5 . 90 − 0.43 + 0.61 , steeper than the canonical literature slope of p = 4, and scales with stellar luminosity with a power-law index of q = 1.1 7 − 0.20 + 0.28 , steep enough to disfavor atmospheres on Earth-sized planets out to the habitable zone for stars less luminous than log 10 ( L ⋆ / L ⊙ ) = − 2.2 3 − 0.21 + 0.18 (roughly spectral type M4V). This model provides quantitative predictions for the probability that any given planet possesses an atmosphere, which will be rigorously tested by upcoming JWST Rocky Worlds observations.

The GAPS Programme at TNG. LXXXI. The puzzle of the HeI absorption signal in the atmosphere of V1298Taub with GIARPS@TNG

G. Guilluy, J. Orell-Miquel, A. Sozzetti, M. C D'Arpa et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究利用TNG望远镜的GIARPS高分辨率传输光谱仪观测了年轻系统V1298Tau b大气中的HeI中红外三重线，通过四年基线内四次凌星的同步光学和近红外观测，结合Rossiter-McLaughlin效应和流体动力学模型，探讨了恒星活动与行星大气逃逸对HeI吸收信号的相对贡献。研究发现HeI吸收在四个观测历元中持续存在，尽管其深度受恒星XUV通量变化调制，这可能支持行星大气蒸发的假设，但同时揭示了在高活性主星系统中区分行星信号与恒星污染仍是重大挑战。

Atmospheric escape shapes the mass, radius, and atmospheric composition of close-in planets, primarily over their first tens of Myr. The young system (10,--,30 Myr) represents a benchmark for investigating photoevaporation and planetary evolution at early stages of a system's existence. The metastable ̋ei near-infrared triplet is a key proxy for exoplanetary atmospheric escape. We aim to detect and characterise ̋ei in the extended atmosphere of We analysed four transits spanning a four-year baseline using the GIARPS (GIANO-B + HARPS-N) observing mode at the Telescopio Nazionale . Using the simultaneous optical and near-infrared high-resolution transmission spectroscopy of ) to disentangle stellar activity from planetary evaporation. Deep H α Imaging Survey of IC 348 with the Hubble Space Telescope. I. Accretion Properties of Stellar and Substellar Objects

Lillian Yushu Jiang, Brendan P. Bowler, Yifan Zhou, Adam L. Kraus et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究基于哈勃空间望远镜的深度Hα成像观测，对IC 348星团（~2 Myr）中200个成员天体（跨越3 M⊙至4 MJup的恒星-亚恒星质量范围）进行了均匀的吸积率分析。结果显示37%的样本中检测到吸积，且纳入弱吸积者和非探测上限后，质量吸积率-恒星质量关系的斜率下降且散度增大，特别是对于自由浮动的行星质量天体，其吸积率被高估约一个数量级。这项研究表明样本选择（是否限制于有盘或吸积天体）对种群级吸积关系的形状具有主导影响。
英文原摘要
Abstract Accretion governs the growth of young stars and the early evolution of their circumstellar disks, yet population-level measurements of accretion are often hampered by heterogeneous diagnostics and by samples preferentially selected toward disk-bearing or accreting objects. This can impact the mass accretion rate–stellar mass ( M ̇ – M ⋆ ) relation, particularly at substellar masses. We present a uniform analysis of accretion in the ∼2 Myr old star-forming cluster IC 348 based on deep Hubble Space Telescope (HST) F656N imaging. Using H α excess as a single, homogeneous accretion tracer, we derive accretion rates and robust upper limits for 200 cluster members spanning the stellar-to-substellar mass regime (3 M ⊙ to 4 M Jup ). Accretion is detected in 37% ± 3% of the sample, with fractions of 34% ± 4% among stellar members and 46% ± 6% among substellar objects. For accretors alone, the inferred M ̇ – M ⋆ relation is consistent with those measured in similarly aged regions such as Lupus. In contrast, including weak accretors and nondetections increases the scatter and flattens the slope while lowering the intercept, demonstrating the strong influence of the low-accretion tail on population-level accretion relations. For free-floating planetary-mass objects in IC 348, extrapolating the accretors-only fit overpredicts M ̇ by approximately an order of magnitude compared to the fit that includes upper limits, which instead implies mass accretion rates of <10 −12 M ⊙ yr −1 . These results show that sample selection—specifically whether the sample is restricted to disk-bearing or accreting targets or instead is drawn from a complete membership census—is a dominant factor shaping population-level accretion relations across a wide dynamic range in stellar mass.

Mitigating scanning-induced systematics in Hipparcos andimplications for Gaia astrometry

A. Binnenfeld, S. Shahaf, R. I. Anderson, H.-W. Rix et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究针对空间天体测量任务中因一维扫描策略导致的系统误差，提出了相位-距离相关周期图的非参数扩展方法，能够在周期搜索阶段量化并减弱对扫描几何的依赖。通过对Hipparcos数据的分析，识别出与扫描规律相关的周期性特征，并用扫描角度控制统计量有效压制了这些虚假周期。该方法在Gaia DR4数据中验证了有效性，成功压制了~54天的系统周期同时保留了已知的真实轨道信号，为利用Gaia历元天体测量进行系外行星、褐矮星等伴星搜索提供了重要的系统误差诊断和处理工具。
英文原摘要
Space-based astrometric missions such as and rely on one-dimensional scanning strategies that couple the measured signals with both observation times and scanning geometry. This coupling can introduce spurious periodicities and complicate their interpretation. We present a non-parametric extension of the phase-distance correlation (PDC) periodogram that quantifies and reduces dependence on scan geometry at the period-search stage. Using data, we identified recurrent periodicities associated with characteristic scanning-law timescales and showed that they are strongly suppressed by the scan-angle-controlled statistic. We then applied the method to Data Release 4 (DR4) prerelease epoch astrometry, where a recurrent ∼54-day feature is suppressed across multiple sources while independently known orbital signals remain detectable. The method provides a diagnostic of scan-angle dependence and a way to mitigate its impact on period searches. These results are particularly relevant for DR4, where public epoch astrometry will enable large-scale searches for companions ranging from exoplanets to dormant black holes. Hipparcos Gaia Hipparcos Gaia Gaia

JWST/MIRI Observations of Amorphous Silicates in Interstellar Comet 3I/ATLAS

Cristina A. Thomas, Michael S. P. Kelley, David E. Harker, Martin A. Cordiner et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用JWST/MIRI光谱观测星际彗星3I/ATLAS，在距日2.21天文单位处获得其10微米硅酸盐发射特征，通过热发射模型分析表明其硅酸盐成分主要为无定形物质（结晶丰度上限为5.3%），以斜长石为主。该无定形硅酸盐组成与该彗星独特的挥发物和同位素组成一致，表明其物质经历的热加工过程极少，保留了星际行星体形成条件的原始信息。
英文原摘要
We present JWST/MIRI Low Resolution Spectroscopy (LRS) of 3I/ATLAS obtained post-perihelion at a heliocentric distance of 2.21 au. Our spectrum contains a prominent 10 micron silicate emission feature with a broad, smooth shape that is distinct from those commonly observed in Solar System comets. We model the spectrum using thermal emission from amorphous carbon, amorphous olivine, and amorphous pyroxene grains. Our models strongly favor a pyroxene-dominated, amorphous silicate composition and show a degeneracy between silicate grain size and the relative abundance of silicate and carbon dust. Across a set of representative models, the best-fitting crystalline abundance is zero, and we place an upper limit of 5.3% on the crystalline fraction of the silicates. We identify an emission feature near 6.83 microns that is consistent with aliphatic C-H-bearing material. The overwhelmingly amorphous silicate composition supports previous work that measured unusual volatile and isotopic composition in suggesting that the material incorporated into the object experienced little thermal processing and preserves information about the conditions in which this interstellar planetesimal formed.

Radio and high-energy variable emission from a multi-wavelength follow-up study of the infant planet-hosting star V1298,Tau

M. Damasso, D. Viganò, R. Spinelli, S. Kaur et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究通过射电（JVLA C波段）、X射线和近紫外多波段联合观测，研究了行星宿主星V1298 Tau的变时性特征及其与恒星磁活动的关联。观测结果表明该年轻K型星表现出显著的射电和高能变时发射，射电通量与光学曲线呈中等相关且随恒星自转调制，X射线探测到耀斑衰减相和与光学流量的相关性，整体符合前主序磁活跃星的Güdel-Benz关系。该研究为理解年轻多行星系统中行星形成环境的恒星磁活动特性提供了关键观测约束。
英文原摘要
V1298,Tau is a very young and magnetically active K1V star that hosts a benchmark multi-planetary system in which planet formation and evolutionary history can be studied at the earliest stages. Because of the high interest in this system, it has been the target of a number of multi-wavelength follow-up so far. Between 2025 and 2026, we conducted a campaign to monitor the variability of V1298,Tau in radio (band C), X-ray, and near-UV (NUV) bands with the support of publicly available contemporary optical light curve. We also investigated multi-band correlations to link the emission to stellar activity. Radio observations were carried out with the Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (JVLA) in band C (4.5--6.5 GHz) between the end of October 2025 and early February 2026. X-ray and NUV observations were carried out with the Swift satellite over 20 days, ending one month before the radio campaign began. We analysed the temporal variability of peak (integrated) fluxes in the C band and of X-ray (NUV) luminosities on various timescales by comparing it with the variability observed in the high-cadence optical light curve collected by the ASAS-SN survey. We observed both radio and high-energy time-variable emission. In the radio, JVLA detected emission in 90% of the visits, switching between high- (≥0.1 mJy/beam) and low-flux states in a matter of days or hours. The fluxes are moderately correlated with the optical light curve, suggesting modulation with the stellar rotation period. Except for one visit, the dynamic spectral analysis did not reveal short-term (minutes) variability, nor is there any evidence of circularly polarised emissions. Overall, the radio observations can be explained as gyrosynchrotron and/or cyclotron emission from the corona that is linked to stellar magnetic activity. At high energy, we detected significant variability in the X-ray luminosity (within the range ∼1.2--2.5times10 +30 erg/s), the decay phase of a stellar flare, and evidence of a correlation with the optical flux. Our data confirm that V1298,Tau follows the near-linear Güdel-Benz relation, with radio and X-ray luminosities consistent with those measured for magnetically active pre-main-sequence stars.

Spatial variability of CH4-to-CO conversion pathways in irradiated gas giant atmospheres: implications for chemical relaxation and 1D quench schemes

Maria Zamyatina, Ãric Hébrard, Harry Baskett, Roméo Veillet et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过三维全球环流模型模拟具有热化学反应的系外气态巨行星大气，揭示了CH₄到CO的转化途径随深度、经度和行星而变化的空间结构，发现1D假设将大气分层设置为垂直均匀的方法在描述化学非平衡时存在显著偏差。研究表明虽然1D淬灭方案能在较好的精度范围内定位淬灭层，但在淬灭层上方完全失效，且对次要碳承体的丰度预测误差可达两个数量级，这对精确模拟系外行星大气组成具有重要启示。
英文原摘要
Abstract Close-in gas giant atmospheres are in chemical disequilibrium. Solving 3D kinetics to capture one source of this disequilibrium, transport-induced quenching, is computationally demanding, so simplifications are used. General circulation models (GCMs) relax abundances on the timescale of a single conversion pathway. 1D models place a quench level and hold abundances vertically uniform above it. We test both simplifications on CH4 against aerosol-free, solar-composition GCM simulations with thermochemical kinetics for HAT-P-11b, HD 189733b, HD 209458b and WASP-17b. We find ten CH4-to-CO conversion pathways, varying with depth, longitude and planet. A single pathway controls CH4 quenching on the three cooler planets; on WASP-17b two compete. The CH4 quench level is 3D: where the atmosphere is hotter, or a faster pathway takes over, the conversion timescale shortens, the quench level rises and the quench-level abundance falls. On WASP-17b the pathway switch lifts the quench level over the substellar tropics, deepening equatorial CH4 depletion. A 1D quench treatment with the Zahnle & Marley timescale typically locates the quench level within a factor of two in pressure for CH4 and about four for CO on every planet. It fails above the quench level, where every quench scheme holds abundances vertically uniform. The minor carbon carrier, CH4 or CO depending on the planet, is wrong by up to two orders of magnitude, on HD 189733b in opposite directions at different latitudes. Quenching sets an abundance at the quench level but does not fix it thereafter; even without photochemistry, transport and conversion keep that abundance spatially structured.

Eclipse Properties and Superhump Evolution in the SU UMa-type Dwarf Nova Z Cha

Qi-Bin Sun, Sheng-Bang Qian, Li-Ying Zhu, Qin-Mei Li et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用TESS高时间分辨率光度测光数据，通过日食深度、日食时刻和正超驼峰振幅的准周期调制，揭示了食双星型矮新星Z Cha中偏心圆盘的存在及其进动特性，三个物理量在~2天周期内的相关变化证实了圆盘的偏心性和进动。在静止状态下，正超驼峰和可能存在的负超驼峰共存现象挑战了传统的倾斜圆盘模型，可能反映了圆盘内外区域逆向进动的复杂动力学过程。
英文原摘要
Abstract The advent of large-scale time-domain surveys provides both opportunities and challenges for understanding accretion disk evolution in cataclysmic variables (CVs). Using high-cadence photometry from the Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS), we investigate the eclipsing SU UMa-type dwarf nova Z Cha. Leveraging eclipses as a natural probe, we examine the evolution of the accretion disk through variations in eclipse depth, O – C of eclipse minima, and positive superhump (PSH) amplitude. During superoutbursts, all three quantities exhibit quasiperiodic modulations with a common period of ∼2 days, consistent with the precession period of an eccentric disk. We interpret these correlated variations as evidence of an eccentric, precessing disk: O – C traces the periodic shift of the system’s brightness center, while eclipse depth and PSH amplitude vary with the orientation of the disk bulge relative to the line of sight. In quiescence (Sectors 13 and 93), PSHs with periods of ∼0.0762 days show linearly decreasing amplitudes and periods, indicating gradual shrinkage of the eccentric disk and a slowing precession. Remarkably, a coherent signal with a period of ∼0.0729 days ( ϵ − ≈ −0.02) appears in the same quiescent intervals. This signal may represent negative superhumps (NSHs) coexisting with PSHs, although an orbital sideband of the PSH cannot presently be excluded with the available data. If confirmed as NSHs, their coexistence with PSHs would challenge the classical tilted-disk model, and could be explained by retrograde apsidal precession of an eccentric disk, where the inner disk precesses retrogradely (NSHs) and the outer disk progradely (PSHs); this interpretation remains to be tested by further observations.

The ALMA Survey of Gas Evolution of PROtoplanetary disks (AGE-PRO): Evolution of dust properties from multi-wavelength observations

Camila Pulgarés, Laura M Pérez, Carolina Agurto-Gangas, Lilian Luo et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
AGE-PRO项目通过多波长ALMA观测（1.3、1.5、3.1 mm）对30个不同演化阶段的原行星盘进行了尘埃特性研究。通过空间分辨的亮度廓线拟合和多波长建模，研究发现尘埃盘尺寸在演化过程中保持稳定，但尘埃质量随时间显著下降（衰减因子~10-40），这表明样本中的气尘比显著低于先前报告值，且大部分盘在长波段表现出光学厚发射特征。
英文原摘要
Abstract Understanding the evolution of solids in protoplanetary discs is important to comprehend the formation of giant planets cores and terrestrial planets. The ALMA Large Program AGE-PRO studied key disc properties in a survey of 30 protoplanetary discs across three evolutionary stages: the embedded phase (0.5-1 Myr), the middle-age phase (1-3 Myr), and the end of the disc lifetime (2-6 Myr). Preliminary dust properties were estimated using 1.3 mm observations, but a multi-wavelength analysis provides a better constraint of important properties such as the dust disc mass, the dust temperature, and the maximum grain size. We present new ALMA 3.1 mm and 1.5 mm (Bands 3 and 5) continuum observations of the complete survey of 30 AGE-PRO discs. We fitted the observed visibilities and derived radial brightness profiles, spatially resolving 20 discs from the AGE-PRO sample at the two studied wavelengths. A multi-wavelength modelling was performed including also the radial profiles from the 1.3 mm observations. Our results replicate trends obtained from the single wavelength analysis: the dust disc size seems to be constant over age and the dust disc mass decreases with time, with a factor ∼10-40 uncertainty from opacity assumptions. This result implies much lower gas-to-dust mass ratios for the discs in our sample than previously reported. In addition, the derived spectral indices are consistent with optically thick emission in a large fraction of the AGE-PRO discs, in particular for the Ophiuchus and Lupus discs.
