Radio and high-energy variable emission from a multi-wavelength follow-up of the infant planet-hosting star V1298 Tau

M. Damasso, D. Viganò, R. Spinelli, S. Kaur et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究对年轻多行星宿主星V1298 Tau进行了2025-2026年期间的多波段监测活动，采用JVLA射电观测、Swift卫星X射线/紫外观测和光学数据，系统研究其射电和高能变时性与恒星磁活动的关联。观测结果表明V1298 Tau的射电和X射线均呈现明显时间变化，射电通量在数小时至数天尺度内在高低态间切换，与恒星自转周期调制的光学曲线中等相关；X射线光度变化显著，证实该源遵循Güdel-Benz关系，整体辐射可由日冕的回旋加速和回旋辐射解释。
英文原摘要
V1298 Tau is a very young and magnetically active K1V star which hosts a benchmark multi-planetary system to study planet formation and evolutionary history at the earliest stages. Between 2025 and 2026, we conducted a campaign to monitor the variability of V1298 Tau in radio (band C), X-ray, and NUV bands, with the support of publicly available contemporary optical light curve, and to investigate multi-band correlations to link the emission with stellar activity. Radio observations were carried out with the Karl G. Jansky Very Large Array (JVLA) in band C (4.5--6.5 GHz). X-ray and NUV observations were carried out with the Swift satellite over 20 days, ending one month before the begin of the radio campaign. We analysed the temporal variability of peak (integrated) fluxes in the C-band and of X-ray (NUV) luminosities on various timescales, comparing it with the variability observed in the high-cadence optical light curve collected by the ASAS-SN survey. We observed both radio and high-energy time variable emission. JVLA detected emission in 90\% of the visits, switching between high ($\geq$0.1 mJy/beam) and low flux states in matter of days or hours. The fluxes show moderate correlation with the optical light curve, suggesting modulation with the stellar rotation period. Except for one visit, dynamic spectral analysis does not reveal short-term (minutes) variability, nor is there any evidence of circularly polarised emissions. Overall, the radio observations can be explained as gyrosynchrotron and/or cyclotron emission from the corona, and linked to stellar magnetic activity. At high-energy, we detected significant variability in X-ray luminosity (within the range ~1.2--2.5x10$^{+30}$ erg/s), the decay phase of a stellar flare, and evidence of correlation with the optical flux. Our data confirm that V1298\,Tau follows the near-linear Güdel-Benz relation.

White dwarf pollution in wide binaries I: the effects of stellar evolution

Constanza Norambuena, Carolina Charalambous, Claudia Aguilera-Gómez · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过REBOUND N体模拟探讨了宽双星系统中恒星演化不确定性对残存行星系统动力学演化的影响，采用MESA演化轨迹和潮汐作用模型追踪从主序星到白矮星阶段的演化过程。研究表明，恒星演化模型对行星存活率影响较小，而双星构型是控制岩质物质吸积效率的主导因素，更宽的双星分离能产生更高的吸积速率并维持行星碎片长期存在。结论是双星构型而非恒星演化不确定性是控制岩质物质动力学输运到白矮星的主要因素。
英文原摘要
Context. Atmospheric pollution in white dwarfs is interpreted as evidence for the accretion of rocky material from remnant planetary systems. Wide binary companions can influence the delivery of planetary material onto white dwarfs, making it essential to understand the interplay between stellar evolution and binary perturbations. Aims. We investigate how uncertainties in stellar mass-loss histories affect the dynamical evolution of remnant planetary systems in wide binaries, and assess their relative importance compared with binary perturbations in driving the accretion of rocky material onto white dwarfs. Methods. We perform REBOUND N-body simulations incorporating time-dependent stellar parameters interpolated from MESA evolutionary tracks and tidal interactions, following the dynamical evolution up to the white dwarf stage. We explore two stellar-evolution models with distinct mass-loss histories for wide binary systems hosting a Jupiter-mass planet and a planetesimal disc, spanning a range of binary architectures. Results. The adopted stellar-evolution model has only a minor effect on the survival of the planet, mainly affecting the timing and efficiency of planetesimal delivery during the white dwarf stage. Instead, the architecture of the binary primarily controls the pollution outcome: wider binaries produce the highest rates of accretion onto the white dwarf, consistent with the observed prevalence of polluted white dwarfs in wide binaries. Moreover, planetesimal reservoirs remain long-lived, with survival times increasing with binary separation, enabling Gyr-long metal pollution. Conclusions. Our results suggest that the architecture of wide binaries, rather than uncertainties in stellar evolution, is the dominant factor controlling the dynamical delivery of rocky material onto white dwarfs.

Saturation Level of the Streaming Instability

Yanqin Wu, Xuqi Chen, Joshua Bromley · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过分析原行星盘中流动不稳定性产生的湍流能量，建立了湍流强度与粒度参数、压力梯度和尘气比的定量关系：α ~ St Pi² f(ε)。该理论预测得到多个前期研究结果的验证和额外数值模拟的支持，并可应用于估计尘埃标度高度和压力凸起的天体物理意义。然而，作者指出当前仍无法将湍流强度与行星小体形成过程相关联。
英文原摘要
In proto-planetary disks, gas turbulence can cluster dust grains and catalyze the formation of planetesimals. This process depends on the strength of the turbulence. Here, we evaluate the turbulence strength that is induced by the streaming instability. Based on the amount of free energy available, we estimate the viscous parameter to be alpha ~ St Pi^2 f(epsilon), where St (< 1) is the Stokes number of the streaming grains, Pi is the dimensionless pressure gradient, and f (epsilon) is an explicit function of epsilon, the local dust-to-gas density ratio. This estimate explains the results of many previous studies, and is also supported by our additional tailor-made simulations. We discuss two easy applications of our result, one is a self-consistent dust scale height, one is the astrophysical relevance of a pressure bump. Lastly, while this work allows one to characterize the streaming turbulence, we emphasize our current inability to connect the strength of turbulence to the formation of planetesimals.

When the core ate the atmosphere

Vera Sparrman, Anders Johansen · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过建立行星吸积过程中挥发元素在大气、地幔和核中的运输模型，比较了流星体吸积和卵石吸积两种情景下原始地球的挥发元素分配。研究发现挥发元素的分配对吸积时间、金属-硅酸盐分配系数和大气-岩浆溶解度等因素高度敏感，其中早期交付（流星体吸积）会显著增加氢和碳向地核的迁移。结果表明地球硅酸盐体的挥发元素库存主要在岩浆洋阶段就已确立，地核在行星形成期间封存了大量挥发元素（早期交付情景中约70%的H和C）。
英文原摘要
The initial volatile element inventories in a planet's atmosphere, mantle, and core are established by delivery and transport during planetary growth. Conversely, the volatile abundances of Earth's planetary reservoirs record its accretion pathway. We model here volatile transport during planet formation for two different delivery scenarios, via pebble and planetesimal accretion, to predict the volatile inventories of proto-Earth at the end of its main accretion phase. We find that the distributions of volatiles between the atmosphere, mantle, and core exhibit strong sensitivity to volatile delivery timing, metal-silicate partition coefficients, atmosphere-magma solubilities, and planet mass. In particular, the early volatile delivery expected in pebble accretion scenarios substantially enhances core sequestration of hydrogen and carbon relative to continuous delivery during incremental planetesimal accretion. With our fiducial partition coefficients, the pebble accretion model produces a 760 bar carbon-dominated atmosphere with 39 bar of hydrogen and water and a few bars of nitrogen, consistent with volatile concentration estimates for bulk silicate Earth including the atmosphere. The corresponding planetesimal accretion model produces a more massive ($\sim$$1350$ bar) atmosphere with similar composition. The core stores $\sim$$70$% of both the H and the C budget in the early-delivery scenario, but $\lesssim$$50$% in the corresponding continuous delivery scenario. Overall our results demonstrate that substantial amounts of volatiles are sequestered into rocky planet cores during planet formation and that the volatile inventory of bulk silicate Earth was likely established already during the magma ocean phase of planet formation.

The T-matrix method for modeling the optical properties of grains in exoplanetary atmospheres

Giambattista Aresu, Giovanni La Mura, Giacomo Mulas, Cesare Cecchi-Pestellini et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究采用T矩阵方法计算系外行星大气中非球形颗粒(橄榄石、尖晶石、一氧化硅)在近红外到远红外波段的消光特性,对比了不同粒度和形态(从紧密球体堆积到多孔聚集体)颗粒的吸收散射截面差异。研究发现结构化颗粒的消光系数可相差2-3倍,在1μm以上波长范围内结构颗粒的吸收效率更高,等效球形颗粒的质量需增加2倍才能匹配其贡献。这些计算结果为系外行星大气观测的正演和反演模型提供了关键参考。
英文原摘要
Observations of exoplanetary atmospheres have revealed a rather complex co-existence between the gaseous and condensed phases, ranging from atmospheres apparently covered in clouds to clear ones, across a myriad of intermediate regimes. The interaction between solids and stellar radiation is of fundamental importance when forward or retrieval models are compared to observations. The simplest approach is to assume spherical particles, whose scattering and absorption properties are readily computed with Mie theory. More advanced computational methods, however, can also treat non-spherical condensates. Among these, the transition matrix approach has proven to provide a rigorous and efficient calculation of the extinction by non-spherical particles over a broad range of sizes and refractive indices. We here focus on the absorption and scattering cross sections of forsterite, spinel and silicon monoxide from the near to the far infrared range for particles of different sizes, with shapes ranging from compact ensembles of spheres to porous aggregates. We find that, depending on the size and the configuration chosen, the differences in extinction can be as high as a factor of 2-3, with structured grains being more efficient absorbers in the near infrared for sizes greater than 1 $μ$m. In a fiducial transmission spectrum we show that in the $λ= 1-5\,μ$m range the amount of spherical material needed to match the contribution of the structured grains can be a factor 2 higher. Such calculations, available for the community through a Zenodo repository, prove to be useful for interpreting recent and future observations of exoplanetary atmospheres.

英文原摘要
Abstract Recent observations of atmospheric escape from planets orbiting early-type stars indicate that planets with higher Roche filling factors have significantly higher mass-loss rates. For three of these planets, full-orbit observations have revealed the presence of large leading and trailing tails of escaping planetary material. These 3D outflow geometries encode information about the underlying outflow physics, and can also be used to constrain their stellar wind properties and to predict their long-term orbital evolution. We present new evidence for extended H α and H β absorption from escaping hydrogen for a fourth planet, WASP-12 b. We observed WASP-12 b with the Keck Planet Finder on Keck I over a period of approximately 8 hr centered on the transit. We find an H α and H β absorption signal in the stellar rest frame that is strongest during the transit (H α amplitude: 2.66% ± 0.25%, H β amplitude: 2.88% ± 0.43%), with evidence of both pre- and posttransit absorption. This measurement is consistent with outflow models where the escaping gas overflows the planet’s Roche lobe and is advected into the stellar rest frame before being tidally sheared into extended tails. This outflow behavior is qualitatively similar to previous H α observations of this planet as well as observations of extended outflows from other gas giants orbiting early-type stars. Longer-baseline observations spanning a full planetary orbit are required in order to fully map the extent of WASP-12 b’s outflow.

Space-based missions such as CoRoT, , and TESS have revolutionised asteroseismology and strengthened its synergies with exoplanet science. With PLATO and ARIEL on the horizon, these synergies will play an even more central role. Kepler We revisit the characterisation of the nearby K dwarf HD 219134 using advanced seismic diagnostics. Our goals are to investigate the reported tension between asteroseismic and interferometric radii, update the fundamental parameters of the star, refine the properties of its planetary system, and investigate the coupled rotational, magnetic, and star–planet evolution of the system, including the atmospheric mass‑loss histories of its planets. 英文原摘要
Exoplanets orbiting near their host stars experience intense irradiation, which can drive the expansion and escape of their upper atmospheres. The near-infrared metastable helium triplet at 1083.3 nm has emerged as a powerful diagnostic to probe these mass-loss processes. We present the first high-resolution transmission spectroscopy of the ultrahot Jupiter KELT-18b, probing its escaping atmosphere by analyzing a single transit obtained with the CARMENES spectrograph. Transmission spectroscopy and Doppler tomography were utilized to isolate the planetary signal. To mitigate contamination from the strong telluric OH lines overlapping the region of interest, two distinct data analysis procedures were applied: a standard Molecfit and OH-masking approach, as well as a principal component analysis (PCA)-based method. By combining the two complementary approaches, we successfully detect a He,I triplet absorption feature of 1.10 ± 0.22% (5.1σ). The signal exhibits a velocity shift consistent with zero and is attributed to the exoplanetary atmosphere. An injection test reveals that the PCA-based method significantly suppresses the retrieved signal. Additionally, using the ATES and TPM codes, we modeled the atmospheric photoevaporation and the helium triplet profile for different He/H ratios and stellar extreme ultraviolet (XUV) fluxes, accounting for instrument and planetary rotational broadening. From our simulations we derive a mass-loss rate and an evaporation efficiency for KELT-18b of 10.47 łeq 10 ( ) łeq 11.68 and 0.153 łeq η łeq 0.321, showing that the atmosphere of KELT-18b is undergoing photoevaporation. Furthermore, our simulations show good agreement with the observed transmission spectrum, allowing us to exclude very low He/H ratios at a 3σ confidence level for XUV fluxes up to approximately twice the nominal reconstructed value. M Our result expands the known parameter space of exoplanets with confirmed helium absorption, establishing KELT-18b as the most massive planet orbiting the hottest host star with such a detection. Furthermore, when employing PCA, we emphasize the need to perform injection tests to investigate whether any planetary signal is removed, not only near quadrature but also during transits.

On-sky dark-hole digging with implicit electric field conjugation on MagAO-X

S. Y. Haffert, J. Liberman, J. R. Males, L. M. Close et al. · Astronomy & Astrophysics
本研究针对高对比度成像中非共光路像差(NCPA)导致的散斑噪声限制近距离类地行星探测的问题,采用焦平面波前传感与控制技术,在MagAO-X仪器上实现了隐式电场共轭(iEFC)算法的在天观测。通过在HR4796A和Alpha Centauri的实际观测中验证,iEFC成功将NCPA诱导的散斑强度在外工作角和内工作角分别降低了2倍和20倍,验证了该算法在现实观测条件下的鲁棒性和有效性,为极大望远镜和巨麦哲伦望远镜等下一代自适应光学系统的集成奠定了基础。
英文原摘要
Direct spectroscopy is a very promising approach to characterizing the atmospheres of nearby rocky exoplanets, but non-common path aberrations (NCPA), which are differential aberrations between the science optical path and the adaptive optics optical path, can leak through the coronagraph and create speckles that mimic exoplanet signals. This limits the sensitivity of high-contrast imaging instruments at close angular separations - exactly the separations where we want to search for rocky exoplanets with current and future telescopes and instruments. We aim to actively remove the NCPA on-sky during observations by using focal-plane wavefront sensing and control with the newly upgraded MagAO-X instrument. MagAO-X is equipped with a unique second-stage adaptive optics (AO) system containing a dedicated deformable mirror (DM) for coronagraphic focal-plane wavefront control. Because this DM is placed after the science and AO beam splitter, it is not seen by the main AO loop. The DM has been recently upgraded from an ALPAO-97 to a Boston Micromachine Kilo-DM, which enables focal-plane wavefront control with the implicit electric field conjugation (iEFC) algorithm. We developed the necessary procedures to run iEFC with MagAO-X on-sky. We demonstrate the successful removal of NCPA on-sky with an iEFC interaction matrix that was calibrated on the MagAO-X internal source; this demonstrates the repeatability between our off-sky and on-sky alignment. The iEFC algorithm was tested on HR4796A and Alpha Centauri in 0.5” seeing conditions. The intensity of the speckles created by NCPA is reduced by a factor of 2 at the outer working angle and a factor of 20 at the inner working angle. This on-sky validation confirms the robustness and efficiency of iEFC under realistic observing conditions, which paves the way for its integration into next-generation AO systems for the Extremely Large Telescope and Giant Magellan Telescope.

New insights into the Martian ozone deficit problem from an uncertainty propagation study

Benjamin Benne, Paul I Palmer, Franck Lefèvre, Ehouarn Millour et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过全局敏感性分析量化了火星大气光化学反应动力学不确定性对臭氧模式的影响,使用秩相关系数识别出羟基自由基产生和臭氧直接损失反应对臭氧变率的控制作用最强。通过在3-D火星气候模型中实施有针对性的反应速率调整,臭氧柱密度和混合比在全季节全纬度得到系统性增加,与ACS MIR观测结果吻合度提高,表明反应动力学不确定性可解释火星臭氧缺陷的重要部分,减少了引入额外物理过程(如尘埃异相吸收)的必要性。
英文原摘要
Abstract Photochemical models of the Martian atmosphere have long suffered from a persistent “ozone deficit”. This discrepancy is typically attributed to missing physical or chemical processes, particularly involving odd-hydrogen chemistry. Here, we instead quantify the impact of uncertainties in chemical reaction kinetics on modelled ozone, which has received comparatively little attention. We perform a global sensitivity analysis using the 1-D configuration of the Mars Planetary Climate Model (MPCM) by perturbing all photochemical rates within their uncertainty ranges. Rank correlation coefficients (RCCs) are used to identify reactions that exert the strongest control on ozone variability. We find that reactions governing hydroxyl radical (OH) production and direct ozone loss dominate the anticorrelation with ozone, while oxygen-producing pathways are positively correlated. Several reactions involving the hydroperoxyl radical (HO2) and hydrogen peroxide (H2O2) that do not produce OH also contribute positively. Guided by these results, we implement targeted rate adjustments in the 3-D MPCM. These modifications produce systematic increases in ozone column densities and vertical mixing ratios across all seasons and latitudes, without perturbing the simulated water cycle. The resulting ozone fields show improved agreement with ACS MIR vertical profiles. Our results demonstrate that uncertainties in reaction kinetics can account for a substantial fraction of the ozone deficit, reducing the need to invoke additional processes, such as heterogeneous uptake on dust. We provide a list of key uncertainty reactions that should be prioritized in new lab campaigns under Mars conditions to improve model performance and better quantify biases before implementing new processes.

Coupled Orbital and Interior Evolution of Sub-Neptunes

Ritika Sethi, Sarah Millholland, Tim Hallatt · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过耦合行星冷却、大气逃逸和潮汐轨道迁移的演化模型,检验高偏心率迁移(HEM)是否能解释亚海王星在不同年龄阶段的观测丰度变化。模型预测HEM在早期(~15 Myr)内快速填充近距亚海王星轨道,与年轻到中等年龄阶段的观测趋势基本一致,但无法再现中等年龄到老年期的丰度急剧下降,表明需要引入额外物理过程来完整解释亚海王星的长期演化。
英文原摘要
Abstract Recent observations have yielded the first measurements of young exoplanet demographics. Close-in sub-Neptune occurrence rates appear to rise from young (10–100 Myr) to intermediate (100 Myr–1 Gyr) ages and then decline sharply in the old (≳1 Gyr) Kepler field population. In this paper, we test whether these observations can be explained by the effects of planetary cooling, atmospheric mass loss, and tidal orbital migration, which we model through a fully coupled evolution framework. The orbital evolution is assumed to operate exclusively through high-eccentricity migration (HEM), in an effort to estimate the maximum possible contribution from this migration channel. In reality, only a subset of planetary systems are expected to undergo HEM. We find that HEM rapidly populates the close-in sub-Neptune bin, producing a sharp rise in occurrence within the first ∼15 Myr. After this early phase, the occurrence evolves only weakly. While the modeled young- to intermediate-age evolution is broadly consistent with the observed trend within the uncertainty limits, it does not support the idea of a sustained young-to-intermediate rise driven by tidal migration. It also fails to reproduce the sharp decline in occurrence seen from intermediate to old ages, motivating additional physics or formation channels to be included in future models.
high-eccentricity migration · high eccentricity migration · tidal migration
系外行星观测1 篇

Companions of Hot Jupiters: two decades of radial velocity monitoring with CORALIE

Léna Parc, João P. Faria, François Bouchy, Jana Baron et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究对147个WASP热木星系统进行了为期18年的均匀径向速度监测，识别或精化了13个外侧巨行星伴星，并通过贝叶斯统计框架测量了不同轨道周期范围内伴星的出现率。研究发现伴星出现率随轨道周期增加而显著增加，且有伴星的热木星表现出更长的轨道周期和更高的偏心率，伴星偏心率随热木星周期递减、质量比随周期比增加，这些特征与高偏心轨道迁移机制的预期一致。结果支持高偏心轨道迁移是塑造有外侧伴星热木星系统的主要机制。
英文原摘要
Hot Jupiters are thought to form through multiple pathways, some of which can leave observable signatures in the architecture of their planetary systems. Massive outer companions, in particular, can provide important constraints on the dynamical histories of hot Jupiters. We aim to characterize the occurrence rate and orbital properties of additional giant companions in hot Jupiter systems, and to investigate whether these companions provide evidence for the role of dynamical interactions in shaping the hot Jupiter population. We perform a homogeneous long-term radial-velocity analysis of 147 WASP hot Jupiter systems observed with the CORALIE spectrograph between 2007 and 2025. We identify or refine 13 outer giant companions and use individual radial-velocity posteriors within a Bayesian occurrence-rate framework to measure the frequency of giant companions over orbital periods of 10--6250 days. The occurrence rate of additional giant companions increases strongly with orbital period, from {2}lt;0.01$ and 0.02 at 10--50 and 50--250 days to 0.05 and 0.17 at 250--1250 and 1250--6250 days, respectively, in agreement with the giant-planet occurrence of the general FGK population and with previous estimates of outer companions to hot Jupiters. Hot Jupiters with detected companions have longer orbital periods and higher eccentricities than those without. We also find that companion eccentricity decreases with hot Jupiter period, and that the companion-to-hot-Jupiter mass ratio increases with period ratio, both consistent with expectations from high-eccentricity migration (HEM). Our results support HEM as the mechanism shaping hot Jupiters with outer companions. Combining long-baseline radial velocities with future Gaia astrometry will further constrain companion masses, mutual inclinations, and dynamical histories.
radial velocity · astrometry · occurrence rate
行星形成与盘演化2 篇

Dynamical response of a protoplanetary disk to an FU Ori-type outburst

Roman Zolotarev, Yaroslav Pavlyuchenkov, Vitaly Akimkin · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究采用辐射流体动力学模拟研究了原行星盘对FU Ori型爆发的动力学响应，发现盘加热引发垂直高度增加并产生向外传播的扰动波，其峰值位置随时间演化为$r_{b}\sim t^{2/3}$。盘的竖向膨胀导致中心源光的消光显著变化，对于倾角在40°-60°范围内的盘，消光从弱($A_{V}<1$)变为强($A_{V}\sim10^2$)，衰减时间尺度约为100年。爆发激发的阴影波在爆发后数年产生的光度曲线偏差(V波段可达1-3星等)是该过程的特征表现，与吸积内禀变率无关。
英文原摘要
Episodic accretion events onto young stars can significantly change the global structure of their surrounding protoplanetary disks. Using radiation hydrodynamical simulations with the HURAKAN code, we study the response of the outer passive disk to a powerful FU Ori-type outburst. Disk heating initiates a factor-of-two increase in the vertical scale-height, which develops on a local keplerian timescale. This effectively launches an outward travelling wave with a bump whose radial position changes with time as $r_{b}\sim t^{2/3}$. After the outburst ends, new waves are launched as the disk structure re-equilibrates toward a quiescent state. The vertical expansion of the disk introduces significant time-variable extinction of the central source emission. Disks with inclinations in the range $40^{\circ}<i<60^{\circ}$ go from weakly ($A_{V}<1$) to a strongly embedded state ($A_{V}\sim10^2$), with the extinction decaying on a $\sim10^2$ yr timescale after the outburst. A fingerprint of the outburst-launched shadowing wave is a long-lived drop or a transient dip in the luminosity curve occurring several years after the outburst begins. This apparent luminosity variation is relatively large, up to $1^m-3^m$ in the V band, and is not related to the intrinsic accretion variability.
protoplanetary disk

ALMA’s Sharpest View of IRAS 08544–4431: Unveiling the Dust Distribution Asymmetries in the Circumbinary Disk

Maksym Mohorian, Devika Kamath, Elizabeth Cappellazzo, Kateryna Andrych et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究利用ALMA高分辨率观测（角分辨率~15 au）揭示了post-AGB双星系统IRAS 08544−4431的环双星盘尘埃分布特征，在半径~45 mas处发现了显著的方位角不对称结构，该不对称性与SPHERE/VLT的前向散射峰位置相符。12CO气体发射比尘埃连续谱更加延伸，且存在空间偏移，表明存在气尘耦合不足的现象，为理解post-AGB系统中的盘和尘埃演化提供了重要的观测约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract The interactions between post–asymptotic giant branch (post-AGB) binaries and their circumbinary disks play a central role in shaping the evolution of these systems. Circumbinary disks around post-AGB binaries are stable, though relatively short-lived, structures (10 4 −10 5 yr), and their physical properties and evolution remain poorly understood. In this study, we target IRAS 08544−4431, one of the best-studied post-AGB binaries. This system has well-constrained stellar and orbital parameters, exhibits well-defined characteristic refractory-depleted photospheric chemistry, and hosts a stable, sub-Keplerian circumbinary disk extending from a well-resolved sublimation rim at approximately 8 au to outer radii of approximately 1000 au. We probe the disk layers close to the midplane using Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) Band 7 continuum and 12 CO J = 3−2 observations of IRAS 08544−4431 obtained with baselines up to 16.2 km. These data provide the finest angular resolution as yet achieved with ALMA for dust-continuum observations of post-AGB binaries with an (almost) face-on disk (15 au; 10 mas). The resulting map of dust continuum enabled resolving disk inner rim, a largely smooth radial profile, and a prominent azimuthal asymmetry at a radius of ∼45 mas. Notably, the location of this dust asymmetry coincides with the forward-scattering peak reported in SPHERE/VLT observations of IRAS 08544−4431. The 12 CO emission is more extended than the dust continuum and confirms the disk rotation in this system. However, the global 12 CO map shows indications of a spatial offset from the dust asymmetry, suggesting some degree of gas–dust decoupling. Together, these observations establish IRAS 08544−4431 as a key laboratory for studying disk and dust evolution in a parameter space distinct from that of young stars with protoplanetary disks.

A Clearer View of HAT-P-1 b: JWST NIRSpec G395H Reveals Water, Carbon Dioxide, and Possibly Hydrogen Sulfide

Reza Ashtari, Stephen P. Schmidt, Guangwei Fu, Avinash Verma et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究利用JWST NIRSpec G395H在2.7-5.3 μm波段对热木星HAT-P-1 b进行传输光谱观测，结合哈勃太空望远镜的光学和近红外数据进行0.3-5.3 μm的大气成分分析。大气反演强烈证实了H₂O和CO₂的存在（贝叶斯因子分别为8.9和52.3），并初步发现了H₂S的信号，指示该行星大气接近化学平衡状态，金属丰度约为太阳的10倍，C/O比< 0.52。这些观测结果表明HAT-P-1 b拥有富含氧的大气，主要由整体富集而非强烈的非平衡过程驱动，为热木星大气比较研究建立了重要基准。
英文原摘要
Abstract As part of the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) Exoplanet Grand Tour Survey, we use panchromatic transmission spectroscopy to connect HAT-P-1 b’s previously studied optical and near-infrared atmosphere to the longer-wavelength molecular bands accessible with JWST. We present JWST Near-infrared Spectrograph G395H transmission spectroscopy of the hot Jupiter HAT-P-1 b over 2.7–5.3 μ m, and combine the new spectrum with archival Hubble Space Telescope Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph and Wide Field Camera 3 observations for a 0.3–5.3 μ m atmospheric analysis. We independently reduce the JWST data with the Eureka! , FIREFLy , and Tswift pipelines, finding mutually consistent transmission spectra across the G395H bandpass. Atmospheric retrievals yield strong evidence for H 2 O and CO 2 with Bayes factors of log 10 B H 2 O = 8.9 and log 10 B CO 2 = 52.3 , while providing tentative evidence for H 2 S ( log 10 B H 2 S = 1.4 ). The joint H 2 O and CO 2 constraints favor an atmosphere near chemical equilibrium, with log 10 M/H = 0.9 9 − 0.14 + 0.19 , corresponding to ∼10× solar or ∼9× relative to the near-solar metallicity host star, and a 3 σ upper limit of C/O < 0.52. Because H 2 O and CO 2 provide a metallicity comparatively insensitive to vertical mixing in this temperature regime, their combined detection suggests the composition is dominated by bulk enrichment rather than strong disequilibrium transport. We find no significant evidence for clouds; instead, the persistence of molecular structure across the spectrum argues against strong cloud muting. The tentative H 2 S signal, if confirmed, would further suggest limited photochemical processing at the pressures probed. Together, the molecular inventory, enriched metallicity, and low C/O ratio point to an oxygen-rich atmosphere and establish HAT-P-1 b as a benchmark for comparative studies of hot Jupiter atmospheric composition.

热木星与短周期行星2 篇

Identifying deformed exoplanets with JWST and Ariel: A target selection metric for phase curve observations

Vanesa Ramirez, Yamila Miguel, Billy Edwards, Babatunde Akinsanmi et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过开发变形度量D来评估JWST和Ariel任务通过全轨道相位曲线观测约束热木星流体Love数的潜力,该度量结合了预期的潮汐变形幅度与光度测量信噪比。针对Ariel目标表进行应用后,识别出WASP-33 b、WASP-76 b和HAT-P-32 b为最优目标,模型模拟表明JWST单次观测可将Love数h₂f约束精度达到σ_{h₂f}≈0.09,这足以对行星内部结构提供有意义的约束。
英文原摘要
The shape of an exoplanet encodes fundamental information about its internal structure. For close-in giant planets, strong stellar tidal forces induce measurable deformations that can be probed through high-precision photometry. These deformations are quantified by the fluid Love numbers, which characterise a planet's response to external perturbations and are directly linked to its internal density distribution. In this work, we assess the potential of JWST and the future Ariel mission to constrain the Love numbers of hot Jupiters through full-orbit phase curve observations. To identify the most promising targets for this science case, we introduce a new detection metric, the deformation metric (D), which combines the expected deformation amplitude with the anticipated photometric signal-to-noise ratio. We apply this metric to the Ariel target list and identify WASP-33 b, WASP-76 b, and HAT-P-32 b as the three most favourable targets that currently lack Love number measurements. Using WASP-76 b as a benchmark case, we perform end-to-end retrieval simulations and show that a single continuous JWST phase curve observation can constrain the Love number $h_{2f}$ to a precision of $σ_{h_2f} \approx 0.09$, while a single Ariel visit could achieve $σ_{h_{2f}} \approx 0.18$. Such precision is likely sufficient for Love number measurements to provide meaningful constraints on planetary interior models and to distinguish between competing interior-structure scenarios, opening a new observational avenue for probing the deep interiors of exoplanets.
hot Jupiter

TOI-1408: A Metal-rich Star with Lithium Overabundance Hosting a Multi-planet System.

G A Galazutdinov, S A Korotin, D B Poklad, E I Kazanina et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过高分辨率高信噪比光谱分析TOI-1408星系的恒星参数和化学丰度,确定该金属丰富恒星([Fe/H]=+0.26 dex)具有极端锂超丰(A(Li)=2.44,约为太阳值的30倍),这种高金属性与极端锂超丰的组合在温度高于6200 K的宜居星中极为罕见。通过凝聚温度-丰度关联分析排除了行星撞击吸积的可能,结构模型表明浅层冷对流包层的存在能有效保存锂元素,解释了观测到的锂超丰现象,为多行星系统宿主星的演化机制研究提供了新的约束。
英文原摘要
Abstract TOI-1408 is recognised as a star hosting at least two planets: the hot Jupiter TOI-1408b and the inner sub-Neptune TOI-1408c. Using high-resolution spectra with a high signal-to-noise ratio (S/N ∼ 350) and resolving power ranging from λ/Δλ = 52, 000 to 115,000, we refined the star’s fundamental parameters. The derived values are Teff = 6340 ± 40 K, log g = 4.3 ± 0.1, vturb = 1.4 ± 0.1 km s−1, and [Fe/H] = +0.26 ± 0.10 dex. We determined the abundances of 12 chemical elements in the stellar atmosphere, accounting for deviations from local thermodynamic equilibrium. For most elements, the abundance excess relative to the solar value is comparable, except for copper and particularly lithium, which show significantly higher excesses of +0.38 dex and +1.46 dex, respectively. The latter corresponds to an absolute abundance A(Li)=2.44, roughly 30 times the solar value adopted in this study (A(Li)⊙ = 0.98). This combination of high metallicity and extreme lithium overabundance is rare among planet-hosting stars with Teff &gt; ∼6200 K. We found no evidence of accretion of a rocky planet’s material onto the central star based on the correlation of abundance with the condensation temperature of chemical elements. The structural modelling reveals a shallow, cool convective envelope that preserves lithium from destruction, providing a natural explanation for the observed overabundance.

Singles in Disguise: Kepler's Low-SNR Single-Transiting Systems as Incompletely Detected Multis

Daniel A. Yahalomi, David Nesvorny, David Kipping, Ben Cassese et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过ExoMOD种群合成模型和开普勒DR25目录数据,揭示了开普勒单行星系统中低信噪比信号过量的真实原因:多行星系统倾向于具有更紧凑的轨道构型和更短的平均轨道周期,因此在观测中表现出更高的平均信噪比,而长周期低信噪比信号被优先性地编目为单行星系统,实际上大多为未被完全探测的多行星系统。该结果表明开普勒的单-多行星系统比例异常可完全由凌星探测的几何效应和周期敏感性解释,无需调用虚假正信号或开普勒二分性假说。
英文原摘要
NASA's Kepler mission found more systems with one transiting planet (``singles'') than expected from those with several (``multis''). This excess was first taken as evidence for a Kepler dichotomy -- a second population of intrinsically single or highly inclined systems -- but later work argued that selection effects acting on a single population can reproduce it. Singles also show a puzzling excess of low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) detections relative to multis. The question arises whether this excess reflects false positives, a selection effect, or an inherent population difference. Here we show, using the ExoMOD population synthesis model and the Kepler DR25 catalog, that multis drawn from the same parent population as singles have shorter mean orbital periods, and thus higher-SNR detections on average. Detecting several planets in one system favors compact architectures, so long-period, low-SNR detections are preferentially cataloged as singles, most of them intrinsically multi-planet systems whose other planets went undetected. The low-SNR excess of singles therefore requires neither false positives nor a Kepler dichotomy but rather only the geometry and period sensitivity of transit detection.
population synthesis

Chemical Demographics of Star-forming Regions

Maria N. Drozdovskaya, Timea Csengeri, Maria Teresa Beltrán, Jes Jørgensen et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
该研究指出当前对恒星行星形成区复杂有机分子(COM)化学的理解受到观测偏差限制,缺乏对大样本原恒星和星核的系统光谱巡天导致无法准确评估COM丰度与物理条件、环境和演化时标的依赖关系。研究提出利用下一代ALMA2040开展大规模化学人口统计巡天,对数千个前星和原星源进行高灵敏度宽波段观测,以系统地探测COM化学复杂性在不同质量体系、星团反馈过程、银河位置和冶金度上的依赖性。这些统计上可靠的COM清单对理解行星形成过程中可用的化学成分库至关重要,将直接约束系外行星大气组成。
英文原摘要
Our understanding of complex organic chemistry during the formation of stars and planets is shaped by a handful of cherry-picked sources with bright molecular emission lines. This observational bias impedes our assessment of how universal chemical complexity is during star formation. The lack of systematic wideband spectral surveys for statistically significant samples of protostars and cores is a major roadblock to properly probing how COM inventories and abundances depend on the vast range of physical conditions, local cloud and global galactic environments, and evolutionary timescales of low- and high-mass star formation. In turn, this limits our ability to constrain and test physicochemical models, which depend critically on these parameters. The next-generation ALMA2040, with a 10x improvement in line sensitivity, is required to observe COMs, that we perceive as common today, in a fraction of the observing time that is needed today with ALMA. Such capabilities will propel us into the era of chemical demographics, whereby wideband (>100 GHz) spectral surveys at high sensitivity (routinely probing COM abundances down to 0.01% relative to CH3OH) will be executed towards >1000-10000 prestellar and protostellar sources. With robust chemical inventories towards large source sample sizes, we will be able to probe for dependencies of chemical complexity on mass regime, feedback processes in a cluster, Galactic position, metallicity, and time-variable accretion. Only with statistically significant samples at high sensitivity will we be able to assess the ubiquity and homogeneity of COM chemistry across the protostellar mass spectrum and natal cores. Constraining the diversity and availability of such species is critical for our understanding of the chemical reservoir available for planet formation, which eventually shapes (exo)planetary atmosphere compositions.
planet formation

Chemistry on solar system scales

Catherine Walsh, Maria Drozdovskaya, Dmitry Semenov, Alice Booth et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究探讨原行星盘内的化学组成如何决定行星和系外行星的初始化学成分，特别是碳、氧、氮及硫、磷等关键分子在内行星形成区的分布。研究采用观测天文学方法，通过对比当前ALMA设备的观测能力与所需的ALMA2040设施需求，提出需要在灵敏度和空间分辨率上分别提高10倍和3-4倍才能在行星尺度上解析原盘化学结构。主要结论表明，下一代观测设施ALMA2040将能够绘制类地行星形成区内的化学成分分布图，探测潜在宜居世界的物质前驱体，从而完整理解行星成分的物理化学调控机制。
英文原摘要
The initial chemical composition of planets, primitive bodies in our solar system, and exoplanets is ultimately set by their nascent protoplanetary disks. Yet observing key carbon-, oxygen-, and nitrogen-bearing molecules (as well as rarer but important sulphur- and phosphorus-bearing species) in the inner (exo)planet-forming zone ($\lesssim 10$~au) remains extremely challenging with current facilities. Therefore, to construct a complete picture of the chemistry of planet formation and understand how (exo)planetary composition is set by physical and chemical processes within the protoplanetary disk, a more powerful observing facility, i.e., ALMA2040, is needed. Compared to current ALMA (the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array) this new facility must provide >~ a factor of 10 in line sensitivity at a spatial resolution of <~ 5 au at 140 pc, i.e., a factor of 3 - 4 better resolution to low surface brightness emission than currently possible. This distance includes many star-forming regions such as Taurus and Lupus that are host to populations of Sun-like stars. ALMA2040 will enable mapping of disk chemical composition on terrestrial planet-forming scales (within the water snowline) and detect molecules that are the feedstock for potentially habitable worlds.
planet formation · protoplanetary disk

Multi-wavelength Polarization as a Probe of Planet Formation

Ian Stephens, Sarah Sadavoy, Akimasa Kataoka, Zhe-Yu Daniel Lin et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究探讨了多波长偏振观测作为揭示行星形成过程的探针，能够追踪原行星盘中的行星形成、尘埃演化和磁场对吸积的影响。当前ALMA在观测盘结构偏振特征时面临长积分时间、样本观测时间成本高、谱线偏振敏感度不足等挑战，而升级后的ALMA2040通过更大的集光面积、多波段能力和精确的偏振定标，将能够通过连续波和谱线偏振测量精确约束流动不稳定性、尘埃物理性质和磁场结构，从而显著推进对原行星形成的理解。
英文原摘要
Polarization reveals details of circumstellar disks that are not readily available from total intensity observations. Via multi-wavelength polarimetric observations of disks, we can trace how planets form, how dust grains evolve at the onset of planet formation, and how magnetic fields (B-fields) influence accretion. Since polarized emission from these disks is typically at levels from 0.5% to a few percent of the total emission, very high sensitivities are needed. Current ALMA capabilities make these key measurements of protoplanetary disks challenging due to (1) long integrations with separate observations to measure multi-wavelength polarization of substructures, (2) prohibitive time requests to observe large samples of disks, (3) limited sensitivity and frequency coverage for polarization in spectral lines; and (4) insufficient calibration of polarization in all the Stokes parameters. An ALMA2040 with large collecting area, increased bandwidth with multi-band capabilities, and accurate polarization calibration will provide a great leap in our understanding of the formation of protoplanets through polarization. Specifically, such an ALMA2040 can use polarized emission from the continuum and spectral lines to: (A) provide stringent tests of streaming instabilities; (B) accurately constrain grain shape, size, porosity, and alignment; (C) resolve polarization of substructures for a large sample of disks with resolutions less than 5 au; (D) quickly survey 100s of disks; and (E) measure the B-field strength and morphology of disks.
planet formation · protoplanetary disk

Planetary system architectures in the final stages of planet formation

Patricia Luppe, Zoe Roumeliotis, Sebastian Marino, Quentin Kral et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究综述了行星形成末期的系统动力学特征，通过分析碎屑盘的结构来追踪行星-小行星相互作用、巨大碰撞等关键过程，揭示10-几百Myr时间尺度内类地行星的装配历史。当前高分辨率观测虽已检测到多个系统中的行星动力学印迹和气体成分，但主要限于亮源年轻系统，对暗弱盘层的覆盖严重不足。未来次毫米设施通过显著提升灵敏度，将首次实现对全光谱类型外围小行星带的统计调查，并能直接成像行星碰撞过程和测量挥发物传输，从而系统揭示晚期行星形成的多样性动力学机制。
英文原摘要
The processes in the last stages of planet formation determine the final assembly and composition of planets. After the protoplanetary discs dissipate, a system of planets and planetesimals -- analogues of the Asteroid and Kuiper Belts -- remains. Ongoing collisions between these bodies produce second-generation dust and gas that form a debris disc. Resolved images of such belts allow us to trace the dynamical history of these systems, including giant impacts and planet-belt interactions, during the crucial era from 10 to a few 100 Myr of terrestrial planet formation. High-resolution mm and optical/near-IR imagers have revolutionised our view of the brightest planetesimal belts, providing first glimpses of diverse structures, many of which show plausible planet imprints, while ALMA has revealed gas in a growing number of systems. Yet these results are strongly biased toward the brightest, mostly young belts around early-type stars, and even for these, crucial details remain undetectable at the required resolution due to their typically low surface brightness. A future (sub-)mm facility with an order of magnitude leap in continuum sensitivity and 10x higher line sensitivity would transform our understanding of late-stage planet formation by enabling the first statistically meaningful surveys of faint outer planetesimal belts across all spectral types, imaging planetary collisions in the inner regions of planetary systems, and gas measurements that trace volatile delivery to terrestrial planets. For the first time, this would reach the faint majority of belts, rather than only the rare bright discs accessible today.
planet formation · protoplanetary disc · planetesimal

Planet formation on solar system scales

Francesco Zagaria, Andrés F. Izquierdo, Jaehan Bae, Philippine Griveaud et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对巨行星形成过程中与原行星盘和其他天体的相互作用如何决定行星系统结构这一关键问题,提出利用下一代亚毫米干涉仪ALMA2040观测木星级气态巨星形成过程的方法。通过灵敏度提升25倍(连续波)和10倍(线发射)以及角分辨率提高3倍,ALMA2040将能够检测150 pc内类日恒星周围类木星行星的(亚)毫米辐射,动力学表征巨行星与原行星盘的相互作用,从而揭示气态巨星如何开凿间隙、吸积物质、形成卫星和影响行星系统架构的过程。
英文原摘要
Giant planets are often regarded as primordial protoplanetary "architects". Their formation and interactions with planetesimals and other planets during and after the protoplanetary disc phase are key to setting the blueprint of the planetary systems we observe today. Catching nascent gas giants is our best opportunity to uncover these crucial steps in the planet formation process and shed light on the origins of our own solar system. Over the last decade, ALMA has revolutionised our understanding of planet formation; still, detecting signatures of Jupiter-like planets has remained out of reach. We argue that ALMA2040, a next-generation sub(mm) interferometer with improved sensitivity ($\times25$ in the continuum and $\times10$ for line emission) and a $\times3$ higher angular resolution compared to ALMA, will be sensitive to (sub)mm emission around a forming Jupiter-like planet orbiting a Sun-like star within $150\,{\rm pc}$ and allow kinematically characterising interactions of Jovian and sub-Jovian planets with their natal disc. ALMA2040 will provide unique and unprecedented insights into how gas giants carve gaps, accrete material, form satellites, and affect the architectures of their co-evolving planetary systems.

A New Method for Constraining 3D Exoplanet Atmospheres: Application to WASP-94A b's Panchromatic Spectrum

Sagnick Mukherjee, Luis Welbanks, Guangwei Fu, David K. Sing et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究针对系外行星大气昼夜不对称观测的解释问题，提出了一个新的推断框架，通过测量凌日时间随波长的偏移（时间-偏移谱）来避免传统跛足深度拟合中的反相关性，从而将分子丰度的检索精度提高3-6倍。以WASP-94A b为应用案例，结合NIRISS/SOSS和NIRSpec/G395H观测数据，首次获得了0.8-5 μm的全景肢部分辨谱，揭示了其晨昏两侧显著不同的气溶胶分布和温度差异（415±58 K），测得的金属丰度与主星一致。
英文原摘要
A growing number of transiting exoplanets exhibit spectroscopic evidence that their leading and trailing limbs differ. Interpreting these observations is now the limiting step. The standard approach of fitting limb-resolved transit depths results in highly anti-correlated transit depths, and treating them as independent measurements can result in strongly biased ($3σ$) retrieved molecular abundances. We present an inference framework that avoids these correlations by using the temporal signature of limb asymmetry instead. This wavelength-dependent shift in mid-transit time, or `time-shift spectrum', carries the limb-asymmetry information, is measurable from existing light curves with a spherically symmetric transit model, and is uncorrelated with the spherically-averaged transit depth. Simultaneously fitting the resulting transmission and time-shift spectrum with an independent atmospheric column for each limb enables the retrieval of limb-resolved properties from two independent measurements. We demonstrate that this approach reduces the mean squared error on the retrieved molecular abundances by factors of $\sim30-50$ relative to next best of the alternative approaches currently adopted in the field, and improves retrieved abundance precision by $\sim3-6\times$. We apply the framework to WASP-94A b and present its panchromatic limb-resolved spectrum, spanning 0.8-5~$μ$m and combining NIRISS/SOSS and NIRSpec/G395H observations. The planet's morning limb remains aerosol-obscured across this entire range, while its evening limb constrains the abundances of H$_2$O, CO$_2$, CO, and H$_2$S. We measure a limb-to-limb temperature difference of $415\pm58$~K at 1~mbar. This limb-resolved analysis finds [M/H]=+0.32$^{+0.11}_{-0.11}$ and C/O=0.724$^{+0.013}_{-0.013}$, consistent with the host star.
atmosphere · atmospheric · aerosol

ALMA2040: Submillimeter-wave Limb Sounding of Venus and Mars

Hideo Sagawa, Arianna Piccialli, Miriam Rengel, Shohei Aoki et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究提出利用升级后的ALMA进行金星和火星的亚毫米波掩星观测，通过高角分辨率（~0.01角秒）实现大气的垂直分层测量。该方法能够以行星大气标高（金星5 km，火星11 km）的空间分辨率，获得风场、温度和痕量物种的三维分布，从而在2040年代为航天器的瞬间观测提供长期地面监测支持，深入研究金星上层大气环流、火星全球沙尘暴、CO2光化学、水汽流失和硫化学等过程。
英文原摘要
Venus and Mars are unique laboratories for understanding the evolution and diversity of terrestrial planets. Despite their broadly similar rocky compositions, their atmospheres, climates, and surface environments have evolved into dramatically different states. Understanding the physical and chemical processes responsible for these divergent pathways is essential for interpreting terrestrial exoplanets and their habitability. By the 2040s, spacecraft missions will provide unprecedented close-up observations of Venus and Mars, while ground-based facilities can provide the long-term monitoring needed to place these snapshots in the context of atmospheric variability and planetary-scale circulation. ALMA2040 can uniquely bridge these two approaches by combining high-resolution submillimeter heterodyne spectroscopy with routinely accessible, extremely high angular resolution. In particular, high-resolution limb sounding would enable vertically resolved measurements of atmospheric winds, temperature, and trace species from the ground. An angular resolution of approximately 0.01" would allow spatial scales comparable to the atmospheric scale heights of Venus (around 5 km) and Mars (around 11 km) to be resolved. This capability would enable three-dimensional studies of Venusian upper-atmospheric circulation and Martian global dust storms, as well as systematic investigations of CO2 photochemistry, water loss through HDO/H2O fractionation, and Venusian sulfur chemistry and cloud formation. By repeatedly observing different local times, seasons, and solar-activity conditions, ALMA2040 would extend spacecraft measurements from snapshots to long-term four-dimensional characterization. It would therefore provide a unique observational framework for directly connecting spacecraft discoveries with the temporal and spatial evolution of planetary atmospheres.

WALTzER: Wide-band Atmospheric Laboratory for Transiting Exoplanet Research

A. G. Sreejith, L. Fossati, M. Lendl, P. E. Cubillos et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
WALTzER任务通过在紫外到近红外波段(240-1700 nm)同时进行低分辨率光谱和高精度测光,研究凌星系外行星大气的起源、演化及其上下层大气之间的联系。该任务搭载35 cm卡塞格林望远镜和三个共轴光学通道,能够表征30多颗富气体系外行星的大气特性,同时最小化仪器系统误差。这一宽波段同步观测能力为研究行星大气的多层结构和大气损耗机制提供关键数据。
英文原摘要
Understanding the origin and evolution of planetary atmospheres, and the connection between their lower and upper atmospheric layers, remains a fundamental challenge in exoplanet science. Addressing these questions requires observations spanning a broad wavelength range, from the ultraviolet to the near-infrared, together with the ability to simultaneously characterise planetary and stellar variability. The Wide-band Atmospheric Laboratory for Transiting Exoplanet Research (WALTzER) is an ESA F3 mission candidate designed to address this need through simultaneous low-resolution spectroscopy and high-precision photometry of transiting exoplanets. WALTzER covers simultaneously the near-ultraviolet (NUV: 240--325 nm) and visible (VIS: 450--820 nm) bands with two medium resolution spectrographs and the near-infrared (NIR: 900--1700 nm) range with a broadband photometer. We outline the main science drivers and present the mission concept and instrument design. WALTzER's broad, simultaneous wavelength coverage enables the detection and characterisation of the atmospheres of more than 30 gas-rich exoplanets and provides data relevant for a broad range of Solar System and Galactic astrophysics science cases. The payload consists of a 35 cm Cassegrain telescope feeding three co-aligned channels (i.e. NUV, VIS, NIR), sharing a common optical train to minimise instrumental systematics, while maximising observational efficiency.

A first look at the protoplanet WISPIT 2 b with VLTI/GRAVITY+

GRAVITY+ Collaboration, K. Abd El Dayem, R. Abuter, N. Aimar et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究利用VLTI/GRAVITY+对年轻原行星WISPIT 2 b（年龄~5 Myr）进行了首次详细观测，通过光谱能量分布分析、高精度测天和K波段谱线研究，确定了该原行星的有效温度（900-1300 K）、轨道离心率和大气性质。研究结果独立确认了WISPIT 2 b是一个自发光的点状伴星，位于过渡盘间隙内距主星~60 au处，虽未直接探测到环行星盘但给出了其空间范围的上限。
英文原摘要
Confirmed observations of young protoplanets actively undergoing the process of formation have remained rare to date. In this Letter, we present new insights into the nature of the recently identified protoplanet WISPIT 2 b (age $\sim$ 5 Myr) from VLTI/GRAVITY+ observations. Through the analysis of the spectral energy distribution (SED) and astrometry of WISPIT 2 b, with the inclusion of new GRAVITY+ data, we investigate the nature of the protoplanet's thermal emission and orbital properties. We performed an orbit fit with the inclusion of new high-precision GRAVITY+ astrometry to provide an updated orbital solution. We also analyzed the medium resolution GRAVITY+ K band spectrum in combination with new VLT/SPHERE and literature photometry, exploring both the atmospheric properties and the constraints on the circumplanetary environment. Finally, we checked for any signal from a resolved circumplanetary disk in the GRAVITY+ data. We detected the protoplanet WISPIT 2 b at $\sim$322 mas separation from its host star. The new GRAVITY+ astrometry prefers an orbit with a moderate eccentricity. Analysis of the SED reveals an effective temperature in the 900 to 1300 K regime and a slight preference for atmospheric models including clouds over pure blackbody emission. While the GRAVITY+ observations do also not resolve a circumplanetary disk, we place an upper limit on the possible spatial extent of such a disk. The new GRAVITY+ observations independently confirm WISPIT 2 b as a self-luminous and point-like companion residing inside the gap of the transitional disk at $\sim$60 au from its host star. Further observations - either at higher signal-to-noise ratio or at longer wavelengths - are required to characterize the atmosphere and examine the presence of circumplanetary material from thermal emission of the dust.

Photometric Study of the Fastest-rotating Near-Earth Asteroid 2024 XA1

Xiliang Zhang, Xiangyu Fan, Man-To Hui, Marco Micheli et al. · The Astronomical Journal
我们对近地小行星2024 XA1进行了测光观测研究,通过开发新方法从恒星追踪模式下的拖尾图像中提取光变曲线。研究结果表明该小行星具有双峰光变结构,自转周期为1.63±0.05秒,相对亮度振幅约46%,使其成为目前已知自转最快的近地天体。
英文原摘要
Abstract Near-Earth asteroid 2024 XA1 was discovered on 2024 December 3 at 05:55 UT by the 2.3 m Bok Telescope of the University of Arizona. At 07:50 UT on the same day, subsequent orbit refinement confirmed a 100% impact probability, with atmospheric entry predicted at approximately 16:15 UT over eastern Siberia. We obtained observations of 2024 XA1 with the Lijiang 2.4 m telescope in the final ∼1 hr prior to its atmospheric entry. Due to the fact that sidereal tracking was used, the asteroid appears as a long bright trail in each exposure. We developed a method to extract light curves from asteroid trails in such trailed images and evaluate the reconstruction quality by comparing the observed trail image with the model image generated from the recovered light curve. Applying this approach to 2024 XA1 we successfully extracted its light curve. We found that the light curves of 2024 XA1 exhibit a double-peaked structure with two maxima and two minima per cycle. We derived a double-peaked rotation period of P rot = 1.6 3 − 0.04 + 0.06 s and a peak-to-peak relative flux amplitude of about 46%. This period would make 2024 XA1 the fastest-rotating near-Earth object currently known.

散射与动力学演化1 篇

Dynamical Tides during High-eccentricity Migration Produce the Hot-Jupiter Pileup, Neptune Ridge, and Neptune Desert

J. J. Zanazzi, Morgan MacLeod, Marta L. Bryan, Suvrath Mahadevan · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究探究高偏心率迁移过程中动力潮汐对热木星轨道分布的影响机制。通过三维流体动力学模拟，展示了潮汐激发的基本模式（f-modes）如何通过激发行星大气中的超声速冲击，在不同冷却条件下产生不同结果：扩散冷却导致轨道圆化并聚集在热木星堆积和海王星岭附近，而风驱动的外流则剥离大气使行星核心停留在海王星沙漠区。该机制统一解释了不同行星质量间的周期分布差异，并预言沙漠中的亚土星应具有大的自转轨道倾角并产生明亮耀斑。
英文原摘要
Abstract The period distribution of hot gaseous exoplanets depends strongly on mass. Clustering between the orbital periods of ∼3 to ∼5–6 days is seen for sub-Saturns (“Neptune ridge”) and Jovians (“hot-Jupiter pileup”), contrasting with a sharp deficit interior to 3 days for sub-Saturns, not seen for Jovians (“Neptune desert”). During high-eccentricity migration, tidally excited fundamental-modes ( f -modes) act as a reservoir for orbital energy, and can take gaseous planets formed beyond several astronomical units and place them at short separations. However, how f -modes relinquish their energy into the planet interior is unknown. Here, we show how f -modes can not only circularize orbits—causing clustering near the Neptune ridge and hot-Jupiter pileup—but can also shock and unbind mass, leaving sub-Saturn cores in the Neptune desert. Our hydrodynamical simulations demonstrate that close approaches tidally excite f -modes, whose supersonic velocities shock gaseous envelopes. Atmospheres cool by radiative diffusion or winds when shocks penetrate shallow versus deep depths. Planetary structural and orbital evolution is followed over many periastron passages using an iterative map: shocks that diffusively cool circularize and bunch orbits near the hot-Jupiter pileup and Neptune ridge, while shocks that drive outflows unbind envelopes and place gas-giant cores in the Neptune desert. Sub-Saturns that dwell in the desert are predicted to arrive with large spin–orbit misalignments after producing luminous flares.

Extremely Low Mass Ratio Contact Binaries. III. Photometric and Spectroscopic Investigations of 11 Systems

Youmo Lai, Kai Li, Raul Michel, Difu Guo et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过多波段地基光度观测和LAMOST光谱数据对11个极低质量比接触双星系统进行了详细调查，利用PHysics Of Eclipsing BinariEs程序模型化光变曲线以获取光度参数，并通过TESS高精度数据揭示了主星表面黑子的经度迁移现象。O-C分析和轨道动力学计算表明这些系统的轨道周期呈现长期增加或减少的趋势，其形成机制需要超过饱和磁制动和引力辐射的额外角动量损失过程。
英文原摘要
Abstract We present photometric and spectroscopic investigations of 11 totally eclipsing contact binaries with mass ratios below 0.15, classifying them as extremely low mass ratio contact binaries. The ground-based multiband light curves were modeled with PHysics Of Eclipsing BinariEs to derive the photometric parameters of these systems. The Transiting Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS) light curves of J011323 and J002747 show pronounced asymmetries and continuous variations. Markov Chain Monte Carlo modeling suggests that these variations are related to longitudinal migration of spots on the primary components. Spectral subtraction of the Large Sky Area Multi-Object Fiber Spectroscopic Telescope (LAMOST) spectra reveals excess chromospheric emission in six systems, while no detectable excess emission is found in the other five. The O − C analysis indicates secular period increases in six systems and secular decreases in five systems. We estimated their orbital angular momenta and performed a dynamical analysis. Within our adopted semianalytical framework without energy transfer, the formation of these observed systems requires additional angular momentum loss beyond saturated magnetic braking and gravitational radiation, but this requirement could be modified by different assumptions on mass transfer or radius evolution.

Data-driven Modeling of Telluric Features and Stellar Variability with StellarSpectraObservationFitting.jl

Christian Gilbertson, Eric B. Ford, Samuel Halverson, Evan Fitzmaurice et al. · The Astronomical Journal
本研究开发了StellarSpectraObservationFitting.jl软件包，通过数据驱动方法同时建模恒星光谱中的遥测特征和恒星变率，采用高斯过程正则化在光谱级别联合拟合时变特征，从而能够利用更大比例的可用光谱数据。在NEID分光器数据上的验证表明，该方法相比基于CCF的传统流程可降低2-6倍的光子噪声误差，达到亚毫秒级的径向速度精度，有效支持类地系外行星的探测。
英文原摘要
Abstract A significant barrier to achieving the radial velocity (RV) measurement accuracy and precision required to characterize terrestrial mass exoplanets is the existence of time-variable features in the measured spectra, from both telluric absorption and stellar variability, which affect measured line shapes and can cause apparent RV shifts. Reaching submillisecond accuracy using traditional techniques often requires avoiding lines contaminated by stellar variability and/or changing tellurics, and thus discarding a large fraction of the spectrum, lowering precision. New data-driven methods can help achieve extremely precise and accurate RVs by enabling the use of a larger fraction of the available data. While there exist methods for modeling telluric features or the stellar variability individually, there is a need for additional tools that are capable of modeling them simultaneously at the spectral level. Here, we present StellarSpectraObservationFitting.jl ( SSOF ), a Julia package for measuring Doppler shifts and creating data-driven models (with fast, physically motivated Gaussian Process regularization) for the time-variable spectral features for both the telluric transmission and stellar spectrum, while accounting for the wavelength-dependent instrumental line spread function. We demonstrate SSOF ’s state-of-the-art performance on data from the NEID spectrometer on the WIYN 3.5 m Telescope for multiple stars. We show SSOF ’s ability to accurately identify and characterize spectral variability, provide ∼2–6× smaller photon-limited errors over the NEID CCF-based pipeline, and match the performance of SERVAL , a leading template-based pipeline, using only observed EPRV spectra.

A Search for Dust in the Outflow of the HH212 Class 0 Protostellar System

Adele L. Plunkett, Per Bjerkeli, Jon P. Ramsey, Daniel Harsono et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过ALMA多频段尘埃连续谱观测，在HH212原恒星系统的分子外流腔中检测到尘埃辐射延伸，并利用第7和第9波段数据估计了尘埃的谱指数和风启动区的尘埃质量。研究发现外流区存在可观的尘埃质量，表明原恒星外流具有将尘埃从原行星盘中驱出的能力，这可能影响该系统的行星形成物质预算。
英文原摘要
Perhaps the most visually striking phenomenon during star formation is the ejection of material along the direction of the protostar's rotational axis. Observed in the form of molecular outflows and/or atomic jets, these features carry away mass, energy and angular momentum from the system. Given the known dust content of protoplanetary disks, and the energetics of outflows, it has been suggested that it should be possible to lift dust from disks via winds, yet limited observational evidence has been presented in the literature. Any detection of dust within protostellar outflows not only puts constraints on the dust content in outflows, but if a sufficient amount of dust can be removed from the system, the mass budget for planet formation could also be affected. We present re-imaging of archival, multi-frequency dust continuum observations taken with the Atacama Large Millimeter/sub-millimeter Array (ALMA) towards the HH212 protostellar system. We detect an extension in the dust emission, and using multi-frequency data in Bands 7 and 9, we estimate the spectral index of the emitting dust and the dust mass in the wind launching region. We find a small but still significant amount of dust mass coincident with the molecular outflow cavity. This finding is assessed in relation to the hypothesis that the outflow could be capable of transporting dust away from the HH212 protoplanetary disk.
planet formation · protoplanetary disk

Planet forming disk populations and their connection to the environment

Thomas J. Haworth, Giovanni Rosotti, Carlo Manara, Richard Alexander et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究旨在表征不同恒星团环境中的行星形成盘种群分布，通过推断其与团质量的关联来理解环境条件（紫外辐射、气体储量、恒星密度）对盘演化的影响。研究采用ALMA2040的增强灵敏度，将观测范围扩展至3kpc距离内的大质量恒星团，以获得统计意义上的盘质量分布（斜率和中值），超越当前仅能检测少量盘的限制。结合吸积率测量，该工作将揭示不同团质量和星形成环境下行星形成的多样演化途径及其物理机制。
英文原摘要
Existing sub-mm observatories such as ALMA have provided statistics on the planet-forming mass reservoirs within circumstellar disks around stars in stellar clusters out to distances of $\sim$400pc. However, these disks are not where most planets form. Many stars, and hence planets, form in more massive clusters, which are only found at larger distances. Environmental conditions that affect disks - such as UV radiation field, ambient gaseous mass reservoir, and stellar density - change significantly as a function of cluster mass, implying that our current knowledge of the disk population from current facilities is severely biased. Characterising planet formation in stellar clusters towards the peak of the cluster mass function requires determining the distribution of planet forming disk masses out to $\sim$3kpc, which encapsulates the nearby parts of the Sagittarius arm of the Milky Way and contains multiple massive stellar clusters. ALMA2040 will provide the leap in sensitivity needed to move beyond detections of only a handful of disks and robustly measure the slope and median of the disk mass distribution at these distances. This will mark the transition from studying individual detections to population-level studies of planet formation across diverse cluster environments. This has synergy with existing and upcoming measurements of accretion rates in these distant clusters, which combined provide a measure of the accretion timescale, enabling us to distinguish various evolutionary pathways and assess how they depend on cluster mass and star-forming environment.
planet formation

HD 152384: A 5 Myr old debris disk system showing a gaseous ring of refractory elements at the dust sublimation radius

Mario van den Ancker, Daniela Iglesias, Olja Panić, Monika Petr-Gotzens et al. · arXiv (astro-ph.EP)
本研究通过VLT高分辨率光谱观测HD 152384（5 Myr年轻A型星）的环星气体，利用Keplerian运动学模型揭示了74条窄双峰发射线来自距星0.09-0.12 au处的几何薄气环，该位置恰好对应尘埃升华半径。通过光学厚度分析和金属丰度测量，研究表明该系统的气体和尘埃来自岩质行星小体碰撞产生，碰撞尘埃向内螺旋运动至升华半径处积聚，为理解早期行星体内的暴力动力学过程提供了新的观测窗口。
英文原摘要
Context: Circumstellar gas around young stars provides key insights into planet formation and disk evolution. HD 152384, a young (5 Myr) A0 V star in Sco-Cen, shows hybrid-disk traits: a modest infrared excess from small dust grains and atomic emission lines dominated by refractory elements, but its disk structure and origin remain unclear. Aims: We characterise the kinematics and excitation of the circumstellar gas around HD 152384 and constrain the physical properties of its emitting region. Methods: We use new high-resolution optical and near-infrared VLT/UVES and VLT/CRIRES spectra to re-examine the circumstellar environment of HD 152384, previously suggested to host a broad, gas-rich disk. Results: The observations reveal 74 narrow, double-peaked emission lines of Fe, Mg, Si, and Ca, with similar profiles and a 103 km/s peak separation. Keplerian modelling places the gas in a geometrically thin ring at 0.09-0.12 au, close to the dust sublimation radius (0.10-0.14 au for a sublimation temperature of about 1500 K). The Fe I emission is optically thick, whereas the optically thin Mg I lines imply 2.2 x 10^22 kg (3.6 x 10^-3 Earth masses) of magnesium gas in the ring. Conclusions: The dust and gas properties are consistent with a secondary origin involving collisions of rocky planetesimals. We propose that small collision-produced dust grains spiral inward and sublimate near the dust sublimation radius, accumulating atomic gas there. HD 152384 thus represents a young analogue of gas-bearing debris disks such as Beta Pictoris and gaseous debris disks around metal-polluted white dwarfs, offering a new window on the early violent processing of rocky planetesimals.
planet formation · disk evolution · planetesimal

Mapping Molecular Ion Distributions in a Diverse Sample of Protoplanetary Disks

Deryl E. Long, L. Ilsedore Cleeves, Charles J. Law, Fred C. Adams et al. · The Astrophysical Journal
本研究通过ALMA对7个原行星盘进行了迄今最大规模的分子离子分辨观测调查,检测了HCO+、H13CO+、N2H+和DCO+的分布情况。研究发现分子离子分布与连续谱特征无明显相关性,但揭示了Herbig星与T Tauri星之间的显著差异,包括Herbig源独特的中心峰N2H+发射和部分T Tauri源存在径向一致的离子环结构。这些观测结果表明,原行星盘的电离环境受中心星性质和环境因素的强烈影响,可能存在不同的电离机制。
英文原摘要
Abstract Observations of ionization-tracing molecules in protoplanetary disks suggest that their ionization environments are diverse due to varying degrees of cosmic-ray modulation, stellar activity, and environmental differences that alter the impact of ionizing radiation. To better understand ionization chemistry in disks, we present the largest survey of resolved observations of molecular ions to date. Using new and archival Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array observations we detect HCO + , H 13 CO + , N 2 H + , and DCO + toward seven protoplanetary disks (AS 209, DM Tau, GM Aur, HD 163296, LkCa 15, MWC 480, and V4046 Sgr). We compare distributions of ions to continuum features and find no robust relationships between the two. The observations do, however, reveal distinctions between Herbigs and T Tauris and hint at a dichotomy between two types of T Tauri ionization environments. The Herbig sources exhibit unique centrally peaked N 2 H + J = 4−3 emission, which we speculate could be tracing either a highly ionized surface layer or a zone of CO destruction, possibly due to their strong UV fields. Three T Tauri sources in our sample exhibit bright, radially coincident rings in all optically thin molecular ion lines. We speculate that these radially coincident ion rings are evidence of a global change in ionization, possibly at the edge of a “T Tauriosphere.” While it is not yet clear what factors most strongly influence the distribution of ion emission, this survey more than doubles the number of resolved ion observations in disks and points to potential distinctions in ion emission morphology related to the central star and environment.
protoplanetary disk

Spectroscopy of TESS selected hot variable stars

H L Worters, D Kilkenny, P Németh, A S Baran et al. · Monthly Notices of the Royal Astronomical Society
本研究通过TESS卫星数据筛选了300余颗热变星候选体，利用低色散分光法测定其光谱类型和大气参数。样本中包含164颗sdB星、77颗sdB双星、21颗激变变星等，其中TIC 360220395等特殊天体可能是老行星星云核。这一谱学巡查为研究紧密双星系统、白矮星冷却过程及相关行星动力学环境提供了重要的恒星参数基础。
英文原摘要
Abstract We report spectral types for over 300 stars selected as candidate hot subdwarf stars and shown to be variable by data from the Terrestrial Exoplanet Survey Satellite (TESS). Atmospheric parameters determined from low dispersion spectrograms are provided for many of the targets. In our sample, we find 164 sdB stars, 77 sdB binaries, 21 cataclysmic variables, and small numbers of main sequence, white dwarf, sdO, sdOB and intermediate helium rich subdwarf stars. Several stars appear rather peculiar and are discussed in more detail; TIC 360220395, for example, appears to be an old planetary nebula nucleus.
